1	PACKAGE BODY         "PKG_DMF0014" IS
2	
3	    PROCEDURE P_DMF0014_GET_SPREAD_TITLE
4	    (
5	        P_START_YYYYMMDD VARCHAR2
6	       ,P_END_YYYYMMDD   VARCHAR2
7	       ,P_BUCKET_TYPE    VARCHAR2
8	       ,P_USER_ID        VARCHAR2
9	       ,P_MENU_ID        VARCHAR2
10	       ,O_RC1            OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
11	       ,O_RESULT_CURSOR  OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
12	    )
13	    /*
14	        * Program ID   : DMF0014_GET_SPREAD_TITLE
15	        * Description  :
16	        * Program History
17	        *===================================================================================================
18	        *   Date       In Charge      Description
19	        *===================================================================================================
20	        * 2017/12/22  SongDH         Initial Release PJ20170278 전지본부 자동차 GSCM(DM/SCP) 구축 프로젝트
21	        * 2022/10/26  KDW2819        [C20221024-000507]CUST FCST Upload 오류 수정
22	        * 2026/03/15  김준규        [PJ20264226] NextSCM Pilot 설계 및 개발
23	        *
24	        */
25	     AS
26	
27	        V_IN_PARA_DATA VARCHAR2(4000);
28	        V_LOG_TYPE     VARCHAR2(20) := NULL;
29	        N_LOG_COUNT    NUMBER := 0;
30	        V_LOG_MESSAGE  VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
31	        V_DESCRIPTION  VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
32	        D_EVENT_DATE   DATE := SYSDATE;
33	        V_PROC_INPUT   CLOB := NULL;
34	        V_SQL_CODE     VARCHAR2(100) := NULL;
35	        V_SQL_ERRMSG   VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
36	        V_BACKTRACE    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
37	        V_PROGRAM_NAME VARCHAR2(150 CHAR) := $$PLSQL_UNIT;
38	    BEGIN
39	
40	        V_IN_PARA_DATA := JSON_OBJECT('p_start_yyyymmdd' VALUE P_START_YYYYMMDD, 'p_end_yyyymmdd' VALUE P_END_YYYYMMDD, 'p_bucket_type' VALUE P_BUCKET_TYPE);
41	
42	        IF P_BUCKET_TYPE = 'DAILY' THEN
43	            OPEN O_RC1 FOR
44	                SELECT 'D_' || BASIS_YYYYMMDD AS COLUMN_NAME, BASIS_YYYYMMDD COLUMN_LABEL, A.DAYOFWEEK SMALL_LABEL_NAME, 'Y' AS EDIT_YN
45	                FROM   SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
46	                WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= P_START_YYYYMMDD --TO_CHAR(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'YYYYMMDD')
47	                AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= P_END_YYYYMMDD; --TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 6), 'YYYYMMDD')
48	        ELSIF P_BUCKET_TYPE = 'WEEKLY' THEN
49	            OPEN O_RC1 FOR
50	                SELECT 'D_' || MIN(A.BASIS_START_DATE) COLUMN_NAME, MIN(A.BASIS_START_DATE) COLUMN_LABEL, A.WEEK_NAME SMALL_LABEL_NAME, 'Y' AS EDIT_YN
51	                FROM   SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
52	                WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= P_START_YYYYMMDD --TO_CHAR(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'YYYYMMDD')
53	                AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= P_END_YYYYMMDD --TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 6), 'YYYYMMDD')
54	                GROUP  BY A.ISO_YYYYWW, A.WEEK_NAME
55	                ORDER  BY COLUMN_LABEL;
56	        ELSIF P_BUCKET_TYPE = 'MONTHLY' THEN
57	            OPEN O_RC1 FOR
58	                SELECT 'D_' || MIN(A.BASIS_YYYYMM) || '01' AS COLUMN_NAME
59	                      ,MIN(A.BASIS_YYYYMM) || '01' AS COLUMN_LABEL
60	                      ,A.MONTH_NAME SMALL_LABEL_NAME
61	                      ,'Y' AS EDIT_YN
62	                FROM   SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
63	                WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= P_START_YYYYMMDD --TO_CHAR(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'YYYYMMDD')
64	                AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= P_END_YYYYMMDD --TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 6), 'YYYYMMDD')
65	                GROUP  BY A.MONTH_NAME;
66	        END IF;
67	
68	        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
69	            SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS MSG, TO_CHAR(N_LOG_COUNT) AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
70	    EXCEPTION
71	        WHEN OTHERS THEN
72	            V_LOG_TYPE   := 'ERROR';
73	            V_SQL_CODE   := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);
74	            V_SQL_ERRMSG := SQLERRM;
75	            V_BACKTRACE  := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;
76	            PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT
77	                                ,V_LOG_TYPE
78	                                ,N_LOG_COUNT
79	                                ,V_LOG_MESSAGE
80	                                ,V_DESCRIPTION
81	                                ,D_EVENT_DATE
82	                                ,V_PROC_INPUT
83	                                ,V_SQL_CODE
84	                                ,V_SQL_ERRMSG
85	                                ,V_BACKTRACE);
86	            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001
87	                                   ,'[' || $$PLSQL_UNIT || '] SQLCODE=' || SQLCODE || ' / SQLERRM=' || SQLERRM || ' / BACKTRACE=' ||
88	                                    DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE || ' / PARAMS=' || V_IN_PARA_DATA);
89	    END P_DMF0014_GET_SPREAD_TITLE;
90	
91	    PROCEDURE P_DMF0014_CUST_FCST_EXCEL_UPLOAD
92	    (
93	        P_MODE          INTEGER
94	       ,P_CREATED_BY    VARCHAR2
95	       ,P_PROGRAM_ID    IN VARCHAR2
96	       ,P_SHEET_NAME    IN VARCHAR2
97	       ,O_RESULT_CURSOR OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
98	    )
99	    /*
100	        * Program ID   : DMF0014_CUST_FCST_EXCEL_UPLOAD
101	        * Description  : DMF0014_CUST_FCST_EXCEL_UPLOAD
102	        * Program History
103	        *===========================================================================================================
104	        *   Date       In Charge      Description
105	        *===========================================================================================================
106	        * 2018. 01. 02   SongDH     Initial Release PJ20170278 전지본부 자동차 GSCM(DM/SCP) 구축 프로젝트
107	        * 2022. 10. 26   KDW2819    [C20221024-000507]CUST FCST Upload 오류 수정
108	        * 2023. 01. 25   kdmcc14 [E20230119-000186-RPA 고객 FCST 가 소수점 단위로 Upload 될 경우 Error 팝업 및 사유 알람기능]
109	        * 2023. 02. 28   chano0612 [E20230227-000456]소형사업부 RPA 업로드 시 GSCM Fail 발생 -- EXCEL UPLOAD
110	        * 2024. 02. 15   kcw3408   [E20240208-001344]고객 수요 분석 시스템_Cust.FCST upload 문의의 건(데이타 없는데 Ctrl + End 키로는 인식되어 Length가 0인 컬럼을 Check 추가)
111	         * 2026.03.24 김준규            [PJ20264226] NextSCM Pilot 설계 및 개발
112	         * 2026.07.08 Claude          [DEDUP/NORMALIZE FIX] 잔존배치 정리 + 같은 초 이중 스테이징 dedup + 일자행 숫자표기 정규화
113	        */
114	        --DMF0014_CUST_FCST_EXCEL_UPLOAD('1', 'hwayoung.lim');
115	     AS
116	
117	        V_IN_PARA_DATA   VARCHAR2(4000);
118	        V_PROGRAM_ID     VARCHAR2(40 CHAR) := P_PROGRAM_ID; --'DMF0014';
119	        V_SHEET_NAME     VARCHAR2(40 CHAR) := P_SHEET_NAME; --'FCST Upload';
120	        V_SQL_TEXT       VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
121	        V_CREATION_DATE  VARCHAR2(40 CHAR);
122	        V_COUNT          INTEGER;
123	        V_START_YYYYMMDD VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
124	        V_END_YYYYMMDD   VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
125	        V_DIVISION       VARCHAR2(10 CHAR);
126	        V_PROGRAM_NAME   VARCHAR2(200) := $$PLSQL_UNIT;
127	        V_LOG_TYPE       VARCHAR2(30 CHAR);
128	        N_LOG_COUNT      INTEGER := 0;
129	        V_LOG_MESSAGE    VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);
130	        V_DESCRIPTION    VARCHAR2(500 CHAR);
131	        D_EVENT_DATE     DATE;
132	        V_PROC_INPUT     CLOB := NULL;
133	        V_SQL_CODE       VARCHAR2(100) := NULL;
134	        V_SQL_ERRMSG     VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
135	        V_BACKTRACE      VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
136	        V_STEP           VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
137	        v_err_cnt        NUMBER;
138	        /* ⭐ [2026-08-14 허위성공 차단] 적재 실적 추적용 */
139	        v_ins_cnt        NUMBER := 0;   /* INSERT 가 반환한 행수 */
140	        v_live_cnt       NUMBER := 0;   /* 적재 후 원장에 **실제로 남아있는** 행수 */
141	        d_ins_from       DATE;          /* 적재 시작 시각 — 재확인 범위 기준 */
142	        /* 양식(헤더) 검증용 — E20260911-DMF0014FORM */
143	        v_hdr_meta       VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);   /* SCM_ADM_EXCEL_COLUMNS.HEADER_CONTENT */
144	        v_hdr_expect     VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);   /* 기대 헤더 = 앞 13토큰(식별 컬럼) */
145	        v_hdr_file       VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);   /* 업로드 파일의 헤더 행 */
146	        /* 복수 오류 집계용 — E20260911-DMF0014MULTI */
147	        v_err_summary    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
148	        v_err_kinds      NUMBER := 0;
149	        /* [E20260923-DMF0014VALMSG] 검증 메시지 헤더 라벨 (V_TITLE 행 ATTRIBUTE1~13) */
150	        v_h1  VARCHAR2(150 CHAR); v_h2  VARCHAR2(150 CHAR); v_h3  VARCHAR2(150 CHAR); v_h4  VARCHAR2(150 CHAR); v_h5  VARCHAR2(150 CHAR);
151	        v_h6  VARCHAR2(150 CHAR); v_h7  VARCHAR2(150 CHAR); v_h8  VARCHAR2(150 CHAR); v_h9  VARCHAR2(150 CHAR); v_h10 VARCHAR2(150 CHAR);
152	        v_h11 VARCHAR2(150 CHAR); v_h12 VARCHAR2(150 CHAR); v_h13 VARCHAR2(150 CHAR);
153	    BEGIN
154	
155	        IF UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1).COUNT > 1 THEN
156	            V_PROGRAM_NAME := $$PLSQL_UNIT || '.' || UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1) (2);
157	        END IF;
158	
159	        V_IN_PARA_DATA := JSON_OBJECT('p_mode' VALUE P_MODE
160	                                     ,'p_created_by' VALUE P_CREATED_BY
161	                                     ,'p_program_id' VALUE P_PROGRAM_ID
162	                                     ,'p_sheet_name' VALUE P_SHEET_NAME);
163	
164	        -- LOG 용 변수 설정
165	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'START';
166	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## Start of procedure ##########';
167	        V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
168	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
169	        PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
170	
171	        SELECT TO_CHAR(MAX(T.CREATION_DATE), 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3')
172	        INTO   V_CREATION_DATE
173	        FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD T
174	        WHERE  T.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
175	        AND    T.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
176	        AND    T.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY;
177	        --  AND UPPER(METADATA1_VALUE) != 'BASIS YYYYMM';
178	        IF V_CREATION_DATE IS NULL THEN
179	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';
180	            V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';
181	            V_DESCRIPTION := 'Data Not Found [' || TO_CHAR(V_CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') || ']';
182	            D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
183	            PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
184	        END IF;
185	
186	        --DBMS_OUTPUT.put_line('V_CREATION_DATE:'||V_CREATION_DATE||',');
187	        --DMF0014_CUST_FCST_EXCEL_UPLOAD('1', 'hwayoung.lim');
188	        IF P_MODE = 0 THEN
189	            -- [DEDUP FIX 07-08] (1) 잔존 배치 정리 — 현재 배치(V_CREATION_DATE) 외 본인 이전 업로드 행 제거
190	            BEGIN
191	                DELETE FROM SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
192	                WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
193	                AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
194	                AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
195	                AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') != V_CREATION_DATE;
196	            END;
197	
198	            -- [DEDUP FIX 07-08] (2) 같은 초 이중 스테이징 dedup — 동일 배치에 타이틀행(Division*)이
199	            --   2개 이상이면 두 번째 타이틀부터 전부 삭제 (10행이 20행 Duplication Key 오탐 차단)
200	            BEGIN
201	                DELETE FROM SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
202	                WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
203	                AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
204	                AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
205	                AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
206	                /* [E20260818-BATCHKEEP] 같은 초 재업로드 시 배치가 병합된다(CREATION_DATE 초 단위).
207	                   종전 조건(UPLOAD_ID >= 2번째 타이틀행)은 **먼저 올린 행을 남기고 새 행을 지워서**
208	                   ①남은 행 상태가 'UPLOAD' 가 아니라 L208 SELECT INTO 가 ORA-01403 → HTTP 500
209	                   ②또는 병합 잔존분이 'Duplication Key.' 로 잡히는 증상을 만들었다.
210	                   ⇒ **마지막 타이틀행 앞을 지워 최신 업로드분만 남긴다.** */
211	                AND    UPLOAD_ID < (SELECT MAX(UPLOAD_ID)
212	                                     FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
213	                                     WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
214	                                     AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
215	                                     AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
216	                                     AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
217	                                     AND    UPPER(ATTRIBUTE1) = 'DIVISION*');
218	            END;
219	
220	        BEGIN
221	            SELECT ATTRIBUTE1
222	            INTO   V_DIVISION
223	            FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
224	            WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
225	            AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
226	            AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
227	            AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
228	            AND    UPPER(ATTRIBUTE1) <> 'DIVISION*'
229	                  --AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'SUCCESS'
230	            AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
231	            FETCH  FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;
232	        END;
233	        ELSE
234	        BEGIN
235	            SELECT ATTRIBUTE1
236	            INTO   V_DIVISION
237	            FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
238	            WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
239	            AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
240	            AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
241	            AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
242	            AND    UPPER(ATTRIBUTE1) <> 'DIVISION*'
243	                  --AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'SUCCESS'
244	            AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE IN ( 'SUCCESS')
245	            FETCH  FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;
246	        END;
247	        END IF;
248	
249	        -- p_mode = 0 이면 Validation
250	        IF NVL(P_MODE, 0) = 0 THEN
251	            BEGIN
252	                UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD T
253	                SET    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD', MESSAGE_TEXT = NULL
254	                WHERE  T.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
255	                AND    T.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
256	                AND    T.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
257	                AND    TO_CHAR(T.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
258	                AND    NVL(T.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE, 'X') != 'UPLOAD';
259	            END;
260	
261	            BEGIN
262	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
263	                --scm2_mgr.xLOG('RESET Status = UPLOAD', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 1, TRUE);
264	                V_LOG_TYPE    := '10';
265	                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## RESET Status = UPLOAD ##########';
266	                V_DESCRIPTION := P_MODE;
267	                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
268	                PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
269	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
270	            END;
271	
272	            BEGIN
273	                UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
274	                SET    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'SKIP'
275	                WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
276	                AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
277	                AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
278	                AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
279	                AND    (ATTRIBUTE1 IS NULL OR LENGTH(ATTRIBUTE1) = 0 OR UPPER(ATTRIBUTE1) = 'TOTAL');
280	            END;
281	
282	            BEGIN
283	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
284	                --scm2_mgr.xLOG('SKIP EMPTY LINE      ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 2, TRUE);
285	                V_LOG_TYPE    := '20';
286	                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## SKIP EMPTY LINE ##########';
287	                V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
288	                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
289	                PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
290	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
291	            END;
292	
293	            BEGIN
294	                UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
295	                SET    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'V_TITLE'
296	                WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
297	                AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
298	                AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
299	                AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
300	                AND    UPPER(ATTRIBUTE1) = 'DIVISION*';
301	            END;
302	
303	            -- [NORMALIZE 07-08] 일자-tier 행(ATTRIBUTE1 NULL) 숫자표기 정규화 — Excel 숫자셀 유입분
304	            --   (과학표기 2.0260706E7 / 소수점 20260706.0)을 'YYYYMMDD' 문자로 환원 (ORA-01841 차단).
305	            --   8자리/비숫자/NULL 은 불변. 데이터행(ATTRIBUTE1 존재)은 미대상.
306	            BEGIN
307	                UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
308	                SET    ATTRIBUTE14 = CASE WHEN ATTRIBUTE14 IS NULL OR REGEXP_LIKE(ATTRIBUTE14, '^[0-9]{8}$') THEN ATTRIBUTE14
309	                                  WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(UPPER(ATTRIBUTE14), '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?(E\+?[0-9]+)?$') THEN TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE14), 'FM99999999')
310	                                  ELSE ATTRIBUTE14 END,
311	                       ATTRIBUTE15 = CASE WHEN ATTRIBUTE15 IS NULL OR REGEXP_LIKE(ATTRIBUTE15, '^[0-9]{8}$') THEN ATTRIBUTE15
312	                                  WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(UPPER(ATTRIBUTE15), '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?(E\+?[0-9]+)?$') THEN TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE15), 'FM99999999')
313	                                  ELSE ATTRIBUTE15 END,
314	                       ATTRIBUTE16 = CASE WHEN ATTRIBUTE16 IS NULL OR REGEXP_LIKE(ATTRIBUTE16, '^[0-9]{8}$') THEN ATTRIBUTE16
315	                                  WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(UPPER(ATTRIBUTE16), '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?(E\+?[0-9]+)?$') THEN TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE16), 'FM99999999')
316	                                  ELSE ATTRIBUTE16 END,
317	                       ATTRIBUTE17 = CASE WHEN ATTRIBUTE17 IS NULL OR REGEXP_LIKE(ATTRIBUTE17, '^[0-9]{8}$') THEN ATTRIBUTE17
318	                                  WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(UPPER(ATTRIBUTE17), '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?(E\+?[0-9]+)?$') THEN TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE17), 'FM99999999')
319	                                  ELSE ATTRIBUTE17 END,
320	                       ATTRIBUTE18 = CASE WHEN ATTRIBUTE18 IS NULL OR REGEXP_LIKE(ATTRIBUTE18, '^[0-9]{8}$') THEN ATTRIBUTE18
321	                                  WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(UPPER(ATTRIBUTE18), '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?(E\+?[0-9]+)?$') THEN TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE18), 'FM99999999')
322	                                  ELSE ATTRIBUTE18 END,
323	                       ATTRIBUTE19 = CASE WHEN ATTRIBUTE19 IS NULL OR REGEXP_LIKE(ATTRIBUTE19, '^[0-9]{8}$') THEN ATTRIBUTE19
324	                                  WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(UPPER(ATTRIBUTE19), '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?(E\+?[0-9]+)?$') THEN TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE19), 'FM99999999')
325	                                  ELSE ATTRIBUTE19 END,
326	                       ATTRIBUTE20 = CASE WHEN ATTRIBUTE20 IS NULL OR REGEXP_LIKE(ATTRIBUTE20, '^[0-9]{8}$') THEN ATTRIBUTE20
327	                                  WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(UPPER(ATTRIBUTE20), '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?(E\+?[0-9]+)?$') THEN TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE20), 'FM99999999')
328	                                  ELSE ATTRIBUTE20 END,
329	                       ATTRIBUTE21 = CASE WHEN ATTRIBUTE21 IS NULL OR REGEXP_LIKE(ATTRIBUTE21, '^[0-9]{8}$') THEN ATTRIBUTE21
330	                                  WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(UPPER(ATTRIBUTE21), '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?(E\+?[0-9]+)?$') THEN TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE21), 'FM99999999')
331	                                  ELSE ATTRIBUTE21 END,

716	                       ATTRIBUTE150 = CASE WHEN ATTRIBUTE150 IS NULL OR REGEXP_LIKE(ATTRIBUTE150, '^[0-9]{8}$') THEN ATTRIBUTE150
717	                                  WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(UPPER(ATTRIBUTE150), '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?(E\+?[0-9]+)?$') THEN TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE150), 'FM99999999')
718	                                  ELSE ATTRIBUTE150 END
719	                WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
720	                AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
721	                AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
722	                AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
723	                AND    ATTRIBUTE1 IS NULL;
724	            END;
725	
726	            -- [2-tier support] 다운로드 원본(주차 상단/일자 하단) 지원: 일자-tier 행(ATTR1 NULL, ATTR14=YYYYMMDD)의
727	            --   기간칸을 V_TITLE 행으로 복사. 1단(일자가 헤더행) 파일은 EXISTS 가드로 미동작.
728	            BEGIN
729	                UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD VT
730	                   SET (ATTRIBUTE14, ATTRIBUTE15, ATTRIBUTE16, ATTRIBUTE17, ATTRIBUTE18, ATTRIBUTE19, ATTRIBUTE20, ATTRIBUTE21, ATTRIBUTE22, ATTRIBUTE23, ATTRIBUTE24, ATTRIBUTE25, ATTRIBUTE26, ATTRIBUTE27, ATTRIBUTE28, ATTRIBUTE29, ATTRIBUTE30, ATTRIBUTE31, ATTRIBUTE32, ATTRIBUTE33, ATTRIBUTE34, ATTRIBUTE35, ATTRIBUTE36, ATTRIBUTE37, ATTRIBUTE38, ATTRIBUTE39, ATTRIBUTE40, ATTRIBUTE41, ATTRIBUTE42, ATTRIBUTE43, ATTRIBUTE44, ATTRIBUTE45, ATTRIBUTE46, ATTRIBUTE47, ATTRIBUTE48, ATTRIBUTE49, ATTRIBUTE50, ATTRIBUTE51, ATTRIBUTE52, ATTRIBUTE53, ATTRIBUTE54, ATTRIBUTE55, ATTRIBUTE56, ATTRIBUTE57, ATTRIBUTE58, ATTRIBUTE59, ATTRIBUTE60, ATTRIBUTE61, ATTRIBUTE62, ATTRIBUTE63, ATTRIBUTE64, ATTRIBUTE65, ATTRIBUTE66, ATTRIBUTE67, ATTRIBUTE68, ATTRIBUTE69, ATTRIBUTE70, ATTRIBUTE71, ATTRIBUTE72, ATTRIBUTE73, ATTRIBUTE74, ATTRIBUTE75, ATTRIBUTE76, ATTRIBUTE77, ATTRIBUTE78, ATTRIBUTE79, ATTRIBUTE80, ATTRIBUTE81, ATTRIBUTE82, ATTRIBUTE83, ATTRIBUTE84, ATTRIBUTE85, ATTRIBUTE86, ATTRIBUTE87, ATTRIBUTE88, ATTRIBUTE89, ATTRIBUTE90, ATTRIBUTE91, ATTRIBUTE92, ATTRIBUTE93, ATTRIBUTE94, ATTRIBUTE95, ATTRIBUTE96, ATTRIBUTE97, ATTRIBUTE98, ATTRIBUTE99, ATTRIBUTE100, ATTRIBUTE101, ATTRIBUTE102, ATTRIBUTE103, ATTRIBUTE104, ATTRIBUTE105, ATTRIBUTE106, ATTRIBUTE107, ATTRIBUTE108, ATTRIBUTE109, ATTRIBUTE110, ATTRIBUTE111, ATTRIBUTE112, ATTRIBUTE113, ATTRIBUTE114, ATTRIBUTE115, ATTRIBUTE116, ATTRIBUTE117, ATTRIBUTE118, ATTRIBUTE119, ATTRIBUTE120, ATTRIBUTE121, ATTRIBUTE122, ATTRIBUTE123, ATTRIBUTE124, ATTRIBUTE125, ATTRIBUTE126, ATTRIBUTE127, ATTRIBUTE128, ATTRIBUTE129, ATTRIBUTE130, ATTRIBUTE131, ATTRIBUTE132, ATTRIBUTE133, ATTRIBUTE134, ATTRIBUTE135, ATTRIBUTE136, ATTRIBUTE137, ATTRIBUTE138, ATTRIBUTE139, ATTRIBUTE140, ATTRIBUTE141, ATTRIBUTE142, ATTRIBUTE143, ATTRIBUTE144, ATTRIBUTE145, ATTRIBUTE146, ATTRIBUTE147, ATTRIBUTE148, ATTRIBUTE149, ATTRIBUTE150) =
731	                       (SELECT ATTRIBUTE14, ATTRIBUTE15, ATTRIBUTE16, ATTRIBUTE17, ATTRIBUTE18, ATTRIBUTE19, ATTRIBUTE20, ATTRIBUTE21, ATTRIBUTE22, ATTRIBUTE23, ATTRIBUTE24, ATTRIBUTE25, ATTRIBUTE26, ATTRIBUTE27, ATTRIBUTE28, ATTRIBUTE29, ATTRIBUTE30, ATTRIBUTE31, ATTRIBUTE32, ATTRIBUTE33, ATTRIBUTE34, ATTRIBUTE35, ATTRIBUTE36, ATTRIBUTE37, ATTRIBUTE38, ATTRIBUTE39, ATTRIBUTE40, ATTRIBUTE41, ATTRIBUTE42, ATTRIBUTE43, ATTRIBUTE44, ATTRIBUTE45, ATTRIBUTE46, ATTRIBUTE47, ATTRIBUTE48, ATTRIBUTE49, ATTRIBUTE50, ATTRIBUTE51, ATTRIBUTE52, ATTRIBUTE53, ATTRIBUTE54, ATTRIBUTE55, ATTRIBUTE56, ATTRIBUTE57, ATTRIBUTE58, ATTRIBUTE59, ATTRIBUTE60, ATTRIBUTE61, ATTRIBUTE62, ATTRIBUTE63, ATTRIBUTE64, ATTRIBUTE65, ATTRIBUTE66, ATTRIBUTE67, ATTRIBUTE68, ATTRIBUTE69, ATTRIBUTE70, ATTRIBUTE71, ATTRIBUTE72, ATTRIBUTE73, ATTRIBUTE74, ATTRIBUTE75, ATTRIBUTE76, ATTRIBUTE77, ATTRIBUTE78, ATTRIBUTE79, ATTRIBUTE80, ATTRIBUTE81, ATTRIBUTE82, ATTRIBUTE83, ATTRIBUTE84, ATTRIBUTE85, ATTRIBUTE86, ATTRIBUTE87, ATTRIBUTE88, ATTRIBUTE89, ATTRIBUTE90, ATTRIBUTE91, ATTRIBUTE92, ATTRIBUTE93, ATTRIBUTE94, ATTRIBUTE95, ATTRIBUTE96, ATTRIBUTE97, ATTRIBUTE98, ATTRIBUTE99, ATTRIBUTE100, ATTRIBUTE101, ATTRIBUTE102, ATTRIBUTE103, ATTRIBUTE104, ATTRIBUTE105, ATTRIBUTE106, ATTRIBUTE107, ATTRIBUTE108, ATTRIBUTE109, ATTRIBUTE110, ATTRIBUTE111, ATTRIBUTE112, ATTRIBUTE113, ATTRIBUTE114, ATTRIBUTE115, ATTRIBUTE116, ATTRIBUTE117, ATTRIBUTE118, ATTRIBUTE119, ATTRIBUTE120, ATTRIBUTE121, ATTRIBUTE122, ATTRIBUTE123, ATTRIBUTE124, ATTRIBUTE125, ATTRIBUTE126, ATTRIBUTE127, ATTRIBUTE128, ATTRIBUTE129, ATTRIBUTE130, ATTRIBUTE131, ATTRIBUTE132, ATTRIBUTE133, ATTRIBUTE134, ATTRIBUTE135, ATTRIBUTE136, ATTRIBUTE137, ATTRIBUTE138, ATTRIBUTE139, ATTRIBUTE140, ATTRIBUTE141, ATTRIBUTE142, ATTRIBUTE143, ATTRIBUTE144, ATTRIBUTE145, ATTRIBUTE146, ATTRIBUTE147, ATTRIBUTE148, ATTRIBUTE149, ATTRIBUTE150
732	                          FROM SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD DT
733	                         WHERE DT.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
734	                           AND DT.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
735	                           AND DT.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
736	                           AND TO_CHAR(DT.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
737	                           AND DT.ATTRIBUTE1 IS NULL
738	                           AND (REGEXP_LIKE(DT.ATTRIBUTE14, '^[0-9]{8}$') OR REGEXP_LIKE(DT.ATTRIBUTE15, '^[0-9]{8}$'))
739	                           AND ROWNUM = 1)
740	                 WHERE VT.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
741	                   AND VT.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
742	                   AND VT.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
743	                   AND TO_CHAR(VT.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
744	                   AND UPPER(VT.ATTRIBUTE1) = 'DIVISION*'
745	                   AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD DT2
746	                                WHERE DT2.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID AND DT2.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
747	                                  AND DT2.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
748	                                  AND TO_CHAR(DT2.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
749	                                  AND DT2.ATTRIBUTE1 IS NULL AND (REGEXP_LIKE(DT2.ATTRIBUTE14, '^[0-9]{8}$') OR REGEXP_LIKE(DT2.ATTRIBUTE15, '^[0-9]{8}$')));
750	            END;

753	            BEGIN
754	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
755	                --scm2_mgr.xLOG('V_TITLE Header Line   ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 3, TRUE);
756	                V_LOG_TYPE    := '30';
757	                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## TITLE Header Line ##########';
758	                V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
759	                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
760	                PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
761	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
762	            END;
763	
764	            /* ==========================================================================
765	               [E20260911-DMF0014FORM] 양식(헤더) 불일치 = 즉시 반려  (사용자 지시 2026-09-11)
766	                 종전에는 헤더가 달라도 그 행이 데이터로 흘러 'Invalid Division' 등
767	                 **엉뚱한 사유**로 반려됐다. 기대 헤더는 SCM_ADM_EXCEL_COLUMNS.HEADER_CONTENT
768	                 앞 13토큰(식별 컬럼) — 메타 단일 원천이라 양식이 바뀌면 자동 추종한다.
769	                 비교는 UPPER(TRIM) — 대소문자·앞뒤 공백만 관용한다.
770	               ========================================================================== */
771	            BEGIN
772	                BEGIN
773	                    SELECT HEADER_CONTENT INTO v_hdr_meta
774	                      FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_EXCEL_COLUMNS
775	                     WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
776	                     AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
777	                     AND    ROWNUM     = 1;
778	                EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN v_hdr_meta := NULL;
779	                END;
780	
781	                IF v_hdr_meta IS NOT NULL THEN
782	                    v_hdr_expect := NULL;
783	                    FOR i IN 1 .. 13 LOOP
784	                        v_hdr_expect := v_hdr_expect
785	                            || CASE WHEN i > 1 THEN '|' END
786	                            || UPPER(TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(v_hdr_meta, '[^|]+', 1, i)));
787	                    END LOOP;
788	
789	                    BEGIN
790	                        SELECT UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE1))||'|'||UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE2))||'|'||UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE3))||'|'||UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE4))||'|'||UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE5))||'|'||UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE6))||'|'||UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE7))||'|'||UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE8))||'|'||UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE9))||'|'||UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE10))||'|'||UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE11))||'|'||UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE12))||'|'||UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE13))
791	                          INTO v_hdr_file
792	                          FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
793	                         WHERE PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
794	                         AND   SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
795	                         AND   CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
796	                         AND   TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
797	                         AND   UPPER(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE1)) = 'DIVISION*'
798	                         AND   ROWNUM = 1;
799	                    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN v_hdr_file := NULL;
800	                    END;
801	
802	                    IF v_hdr_file IS NULL OR v_hdr_file <> v_hdr_expect THEN
803	                        UPDATE SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
804	                        SET    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR'
805	                             , MESSAGE_TEXT       = 'Invalid Excel Form.'
806	                        WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
807	                        AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
808	                        AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
809	                        AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
810	                        AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD';
811	
812	                        V_LOG_TYPE    := '31';
813	                        V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## ERROR Invalid Excel Form ##########';
814	                        V_DESCRIPTION := 'expect=[' || v_hdr_expect || '] file=['
815	                                      || NVL(v_hdr_file, '(header row not found)') || ']';
816	                        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
817	                        PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
818	                        COMMIT;
819	                        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
820	                        SELECT '-1' AS CODE, 'ERR_RTN: Invalid Excel Form — 업로드 양식이 현재 양식과 다릅니다. [Excel Form] 버튼으로 최신 양식을 다시 받아 작성해주세요.' AS MSG, V_LOG_TYPE||','||V_LOG_MESSAGE AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
821	                        RETURN;
822	                    END IF;
823	                END IF;
824	            END;
825	
826	            /* [E20260923-DMF0014VALMSG] 검증 메시지용 헤더 라벨 = 사용자 파일 헤더행(V_TITLE) 실측값 — 메시지 '<규칙> [<헤더>: <입력값>]' 의 <헤더> */
827	            BEGIN
828	                SELECT NVL(ATTRIBUTE1,'Division*'), NVL(ATTRIBUTE2,'Sales Site*'), NVL(ATTRIBUTE3,'Plant Site*'), NVL(ATTRIBUTE4,'Customer Project*')
829	                      ,NVL(ATTRIBUTE5,'Customer Model*'), NVL(ATTRIBUTE6,'Incoterms*'), NVL(ATTRIBUTE7,'FCST Type*'), NVL(ATTRIBUTE8,'Customs Handbook*')
830	                      ,NVL(ATTRIBUTE9,'Measure Group*'), NVL(ATTRIBUTE10,'Measure*'), NVL(ATTRIBUTE11,'Upload UOM*'), NVL(ATTRIBUTE12,'Project'), NVL(ATTRIBUTE13,'Model')
831	                INTO   v_h1, v_h2, v_h3, v_h4, v_h5, v_h6, v_h7, v_h8, v_h9, v_h10, v_h11, v_h12, v_h13
832	                FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
833	                WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
834	                AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
835	                AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
836	                AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
837	                AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'V_TITLE'
838	                AND    ROWNUM = 1;
839	            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
840	                v_h1 := 'Division*'; v_h2 := 'Sales Site*'; v_h3 := 'Plant Site*'; v_h4 := 'Customer Project*'; v_h5 := 'Customer Model*';
841	                v_h6 := 'Incoterms*'; v_h7 := 'FCST Type*'; v_h8 := 'Customs Handbook*'; v_h9 := 'Measure Group*'; v_h10 := 'Measure*';
842	                v_h11 := 'Upload UOM*'; v_h12 := 'Project'; v_h13 := 'Model';
843	            END;
844	
845	            BEGIN
846	                UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
847	                SET    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR', MESSAGE_TEXT = SUBSTR(CASE WHEN MESSAGE_TEXT IS NULL THEN 'Duplication Key ['||v_h2||': '||NVL(ATTRIBUTE2,'(empty)')||', '||v_h4||': '||NVL(ATTRIBUTE4,'(empty)')||', '||v_h5||': '||NVL(ATTRIBUTE5,'(empty)')||']' ELSE MESSAGE_TEXT || ' / ' || 'Duplication Key ['||v_h2||': '||NVL(ATTRIBUTE2,'(empty)')||', '||v_h4||': '||NVL(ATTRIBUTE4,'(empty)')||', '||v_h5||': '||NVL(ATTRIBUTE5,'(empty)')||']' END, 1, 4000)  /*[E20260923-DMF0014VALMSG]*/
848	                WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
849	                AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
850	                AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
851	                AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
852	                AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE IN ('UPLOAD','ERROR')
853	                AND    EXISTS (SELECT 1
854	                        FROM   (SELECT X.ATTRIBUTE1  AS COL1
855	                                      ,X.ATTRIBUTE2  COL2
856	                                      ,X.ATTRIBUTE3  COL3
857	                                      ,X.ATTRIBUTE4  COL4
858	                                      ,X.ATTRIBUTE5  COL5
859	                                      ,X.ATTRIBUTE6  COL6
860	                                      ,X.ATTRIBUTE7  COL7
861	                                      ,X.ATTRIBUTE8  COL8
862	                                      ,X.ATTRIBUTE9  COL9
863	                                      ,X.ATTRIBUTE10 COL10
864	                                --, X.ATTRIBUTE11 COL11
865	                                --, X.ATTRIBUTE12 COL12
866	                                FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD X
867	                                WHERE  X.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
868	                                AND    X.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
869	                                AND    X.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
870	                                AND    TO_CHAR(X.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
871	                                AND    X.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE IN ('UPLOAD','ERROR')
872	                                GROUP  BY X.ATTRIBUTE1
873	                                         ,X.ATTRIBUTE2
874	                                         ,X.ATTRIBUTE3
875	                                         ,X.ATTRIBUTE4
876	                                         ,X.ATTRIBUTE5
877	                                         ,X.ATTRIBUTE6
878	                                         ,X.ATTRIBUTE7
879	                                         ,X.ATTRIBUTE8
880	                                         ,X.ATTRIBUTE9
881	                                         ,X.ATTRIBUTE10
882	                                --, X.ATTRIBUTE11
883	                                --, X.ATTRIBUTE12
884	                                HAVING COUNT(*) > 1) T
885	                        WHERE  1 = 1
886	                        AND    T.COL1 = ATTRIBUTE1
887	                        AND    T.COL2 = ATTRIBUTE2
888	                        AND    T.COL3 = ATTRIBUTE3
889	                        AND    T.COL4 = ATTRIBUTE4
890	                        AND    T.COL5 = ATTRIBUTE5
891	                        AND    T.COL6 = ATTRIBUTE6
892	                        AND    T.COL7 = ATTRIBUTE7
893	                        AND    T.COL8 = ATTRIBUTE8
894	                        AND    T.COL9 = ATTRIBUTE9
895	                        AND    T.COL10 = ATTRIBUTE10
896	                        --AND T.COL11 = ATTRIBUTE11
897	                        --AND T.COL12 = ATTRIBUTE12
898	                        );
899	
900	                v_err_cnt := SQL%ROWCOUNT;
901	                IF v_err_cnt > 0 THEN
902	                    V_LOG_TYPE    := '40';
903	                    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## ERROR Key Duplication ##########';
904	                    V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
905	                    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
906	                    PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
907	                    COMMIT;   -- ERROR 상태 확정
908	                    -- [E20260911-DMF0014MULTI] 즉시반환 제거 — 전 검증을 끝까지 수행하고 마지막에 사유를 모아 반환한다.
909	                    --   (행별 ERROR/MESSAGE_TEXT 마킹은 위에서 이미 끝났다. 적재 게이트 V_COUNT=0 은 그대로라 안전)
910	                END IF;
911	
912	            END;
913	
914	            BEGIN
915	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
916	                --scm2_mgr.xLOG('ERROR Key Duplication ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 4, TRUE);
917	                V_LOG_TYPE    := '40';
918	                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## ERROR Key Duplication ##########';
919	                V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
920	                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
921	                PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
922	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
923	            END;
924	
925	            BEGIN
926	                UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
927	                SET    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR', MESSAGE_TEXT = SUBSTR(CASE WHEN MESSAGE_TEXT IS NULL THEN 'Invalid Division ['||v_h1||': '||NVL(ATTRIBUTE1,'(empty)')||']' ELSE MESSAGE_TEXT || ' / ' || 'Invalid Division ['||v_h1||': '||NVL(ATTRIBUTE1,'(empty)')||']' END, 1, 4000)  /*[E20260923-DMF0014VALMSG]*/
928	                WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
929	                AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
930	                AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
931	                AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
932	                AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE IN ('UPLOAD','ERROR')
933	                AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
934	                        FROM   SCM_MST_DIVISION D
935	                        WHERE  D.VALID_YN = 'Y'
936	                        AND    D.DIVISION_ID = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1
937	                        UNION
938	                        SELECT 1
939	                        FROM   SCM_MST_DIVISION D
940	                        WHERE  D.VALID_YN = 'Y'
941	                        AND    D.DIVISION_NAME = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1);
942	
943	                v_err_cnt := SQL%ROWCOUNT;
944	                IF v_err_cnt > 0 THEN
945	                    V_LOG_TYPE    := '50';
946	                    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## ERROR Invalid Division ##########';  /*[E20260911-DMF0014MSG]*/
947	                    V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
948	                    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
949	                    PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
950	                    COMMIT;   -- ERROR 상태 확정
951	                    -- [E20260911-DMF0014MULTI] 즉시반환 제거 — 전 검증을 끝까지 수행하고 마지막에 사유를 모아 반환한다.
952	                    --   (행별 ERROR/MESSAGE_TEXT 마킹은 위에서 이미 끝났다. 적재 게이트 V_COUNT=0 은 그대로라 안전)
953	                END IF;
954	
955	            END;
956	
957	            BEGIN
958	                -- Division Validation은 끝났으니 코드로 정보를 치환해줌.
959	                UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
960	                SET    ATTRIBUTE1 =
961	                       (SELECT D.DIVISION_ID
962	                        FROM   SCM_MST_DIVISION D
963	                        WHERE  D.VALID_YN = 'Y'
964	                        AND    D.DIVISION_NAME = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1)
965	                WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
966	                AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
967	                AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
968	                AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
969	                AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
970	                AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
971	                        FROM   SCM_MST_DIVISION D
972	                        WHERE  D.VALID_YN = 'Y'
973	                        AND    D.DIVISION_ID = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1);
974	            END;
975	
976	            BEGIN
977	                UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD AEU
978	                SET    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR', MESSAGE_TEXT = SUBSTR(CASE WHEN AEU.MESSAGE_TEXT IS NULL THEN 'Invalid Measure Upload Bucket ['||v_h10||': '||NVL(AEU.ATTRIBUTE10,'(empty)')||']' ELSE AEU.MESSAGE_TEXT || ' / ' || 'Invalid Measure Upload Bucket ['||v_h10||': '||NVL(AEU.ATTRIBUTE10,'(empty)')||']' END, 1, 4000)  /*[E20260923-DMF0014VALMSG]*/
979	                WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
980	                AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
981	                AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
982	                AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
983	                AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE IN ('UPLOAD','ERROR')
984	                AND    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MST_SITE GS, SCM_MST_DIVISION GD WHERE GS.DIVISION_ID = GD.DIVISION_ID AND GS.VALID_YN = 'Y' AND GS.SCM_SITE_ID = AEU.ATTRIBUTE2 AND (GD.DIVISION_ID = AEU.ATTRIBUTE1 OR GD.DIVISION_NAME = AEU.ATTRIBUTE1))  /*[E20260923-DMF0014VALMSG] 가드: Sales Site·(Site,Measure) 마스터 통과 행만 — 상위 오류가 Bucket 으로 중복 표기되지 않게*/
985	                AND    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST GF, SCM_MST_DIVISION GD WHERE GF.VALID_YN = 'Y' AND GF.DIVISION_ID = GD.DIVISION_ID AND (GD.DIVISION_ID = AEU.ATTRIBUTE1 OR GD.DIVISION_NAME = AEU.ATTRIBUTE1) AND GF.SCM_SITE_ID = AEU.ATTRIBUTE2 AND GF.MEASURE = AEU.ATTRIBUTE10)  /*[E20260923-DMF0014VALMSG] 가드: Sales Site·(Site,Measure) 마스터 통과 행만 — 상위 오류가 Bucket 으로 중복 표기되지 않게*/
986	                AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
987	                        FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST D, SCM_MST_DIVISION D2
988	                        WHERE  D.VALID_YN = 'Y'
989	                        AND    D.DIVISION_ID = D2.DIVISION_ID
990	                        AND    (D2.DIVISION_ID = AEU.ATTRIBUTE1 OR D2.DIVISION_NAME = AEU.ATTRIBUTE1)
991	                        AND    D.SCM_SITE_ID = AEU.ATTRIBUTE2
992	                        AND    D.MEASURE = AEU.ATTRIBUTE10
993	                        AND    D.UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE IN (SELECT CASE (NVL(NVL(TO_DATE(CASE WHEN LENGTH(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE16),'[^0-9]','')) >= 8 THEN SUBSTR(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE16),'[^0-9]',''),1,8) END,'YYYYMMDD') - TO_DATE(CASE WHEN LENGTH(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE15),'[^0-9]','')) >= 8 THEN SUBSTR(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE15),'[^0-9]',''),1,8) END,'YYYYMMDD'), TO_DATE(CASE WHEN LENGTH(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE15),'[^0-9]','')) >= 8 THEN SUBSTR(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE15),'[^0-9]',''),1,8) END,'YYYYMMDD') - TO_DATE(CASE WHEN LENGTH(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE14),'[^0-9]','')) >= 8 THEN SUBSTR(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE14),'[^0-9]',''),1,8) END,'YYYYMMDD')), TO_DATE(CASE WHEN LENGTH(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE17),'[^0-9]','')) >= 8 THEN SUBSTR(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE17),'[^0-9]',''),1,8) END,'YYYYMMDD') - TO_DATE(CASE WHEN LENGTH(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE16),'[^0-9]','')) >= 8 THEN SUBSTR(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE16),'[^0-9]',''),1,8) END,'YYYYMMDD')))  /* [E20260823-BUCKETNLS] 헤더 일자 = 숫자만 추출한 앞 8자리로 판정. 구식 TO_DATE(TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(x))) 는 NLS_NUMERIC_CHARACTERS 에 의존해(엑셀 '20260803.0' → 소수점 문자가 ','인 DB 에서 ORA-01722) 클라우드에서만 깨졌다. 날짜서식('2026-08-10')·과학표기·공백도 같은 값으로 수렴한다. 8자리 미만이면 NULL → 기존 NVL 폴백이 다음 간격을 쓴다(동작 보존). */  /* [BUCKET FIX 07-08] V_TITLE(주차라벨 'W29')는 TO_DATE 불가 → 날짜행(2Row, ATTRIBUTE1 NULL) 의 과학표기 날짜를 TO_NUMBER 정규화하여 간격 산출(월경계 정확 7=WEEKLY) */  /* [BUCKET FIX 07-06] 부분 첫주(월1일)=2~3번째 일자 간격(완전주) 우선 */
994	                                                                   WHEN 1 THEN
995	                                                                    'DAILY'
996	                                                                   WHEN 7 THEN
997	                                                                    'WEEKLY'
998	                                                                   ELSE
999	                                                                    'MONTHLY'
1000	                                                               END
1001	                                                        FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD Z
1002	                                                        WHERE  Z.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
1003	                                                        AND    Z.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
1004	                                                        AND    Z.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
1005	                                                        AND    TO_CHAR(Z.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
1006	                                                        AND    (Z.ATTRIBUTE1 IS NULL OR UPPER(Z.ATTRIBUTE1) = 'DIVISION*') AND Z.ATTRIBUTE15 IS NOT NULL AND    LENGTH(REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(Z.ATTRIBUTE15),'[^0-9]','')) >= 8  /* [E20260823-BUCKETNLS] 날짜서식 헤더('2026-08-10')도 채택 — 구 정규식은 숫자문자만 허용해     클라우드에서 채택 0건 → 빈 IN 집합 → 전건 'Invalid Measure Upload Bucket' 이 됐다 */));  /* [BUCKET FIX 08-14] ⭐ 1단 헤더 지원 — **헤더행**(일자행 ATTRIBUTE1 NULL 또는 타이틀행 'Division*')만 채택한다. 데이터행까지 열면 값칸('100')이 TO_DATE 되어 ORA-01840 이 난다. 구 조건(ATTRIBUTE1 IS NULL)은 일자행만 인정해, 일자행이 없는 1단 양식에서 서브쿼리가 공집합이 되어 **전건 Invalid Measure Upload Bucket** 이 됐다. 2단 파일의 주차라벨 타이틀행('W29')은 위 숫자 정규식에서 자동 탈락하므로 종전 동작은 그대로다. */
1007	
1008	                v_err_cnt := SQL%ROWCOUNT;
1009	                IF v_err_cnt > 0 THEN
1010	                    V_LOG_TYPE    := '60';
1011	                    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## ERROR Invalid Measure Upload Bucket. ##########';
1012	                    V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
1013	                    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
1014	                    PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
1015	                    COMMIT;   -- ERROR 상태 확정
1016	                    -- [E20260911-DMF0014MULTI] 즉시반환 제거 — 전 검증을 끝까지 수행하고 마지막에 사유를 모아 반환한다.
1017	                    --   (행별 ERROR/MESSAGE_TEXT 마킹은 위에서 이미 끝났다. 적재 게이트 V_COUNT=0 은 그대로라 안전)
1018	                END IF;
1019	            END;

1178	                   'Invalid Customer Model' 로 뭉뚱그렸다. 판정 = (Division, Sales Site, Customer Project) 가 고객모델 마스터에 없는 행.

1188	                AND    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MST_SITE GS, SCM_MST_DIVISION GD WHERE GS.DIVISION_ID = GD.DIVISION_ID AND GS.VALID_YN = 'Y' AND GS.SCM_SITE_ID = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE2 AND (GD.DIVISION_ID = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1 OR GD.DIVISION_NAME = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1))  /*[E20260923-DMF0014VALMSG] 가드: Division/Sales Site 오류 행은 제외(근본 원인 1건만)*/
1189	                AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL GC, SCM_MST_DIVISION GD WHERE GC.DIVISION_ID = GD.DIVISION_ID AND GC.VALID_YN = 'Y' AND (GD.DIVISION_ID = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1 OR GD.DIVISION_NAME = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1) AND GC.SCM_SITE_ID = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE2 AND GC.CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE4);  /*[E20260929-DMF0014PRJMDL] [Project]/[Model] 품목 경로(SITE_ITEM) 구제 제거 — 고객모델 마스터 단독 판정*/
1190	                v_err_cnt := SQL%ROWCOUNT;
1191	                IF v_err_cnt > 0 THEN
1192	                    V_LOG_TYPE    := '61';
1193	                    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## ERROR Invalid Customer Project. ##########';
1194	                    V_DESCRIPTION := 'rows=' || TO_CHAR(v_err_cnt);
1195	                    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
1196	                    PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
1197	                    COMMIT;   -- ERROR 상태 확정
1198	                END IF;
1199	            END IF;

1204	                WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
1205	                AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
1206	                AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
1207	                AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
1208	                AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE IN ('UPLOAD','ERROR')
1209	                AND    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MST_SITE GS, SCM_MST_DIVISION GD WHERE GS.DIVISION_ID = GD.DIVISION_ID AND GS.VALID_YN = 'Y' AND GS.SCM_SITE_ID = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE2 AND (GD.DIVISION_ID = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1 OR GD.DIVISION_NAME = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1))  /*[E20260923-DMF0014VALMSG] 가드: Sales Site·Customer Project 통과 행만 — Project 오류는 위 'Invalid Customer Project' 가 담당*/
1210	                AND    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL GC, SCM_MST_DIVISION GD WHERE GC.DIVISION_ID = GD.DIVISION_ID AND GC.VALID_YN = 'Y' AND (GD.DIVISION_ID = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1 OR GD.DIVISION_NAME = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1) AND GC.SCM_SITE_ID = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE2 AND GC.CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE4)  /*[E20260923-DMF0014VALMSG] 가드: Sales Site·Customer Project 통과 행만 — Project 오류는 위 'Invalid Customer Project' 가 담당*/
1211	                AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
1212	                        FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL CM, SCM_MST_DIVISION D2

1218	                        AND    CM.VALID_YN = 'Y');  /*[E20260929-DMF0014PRJMDL] UNION SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM([Project]/[Model]) 구제 제거 — 고객모델 마스터 단독 판정.
1219	                                                     [Project]/[Model] 열은 아래 'Invalid Project'/'Invalid Model' 이 별도 검증*/

1224	                    V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
1225	                    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;

1227	                    COMMIT;   -- ERROR 상태 확정

1229	                    --   (행별 ERROR/MESSAGE_TEXT 마킹은 위에서 이미 끝났다. 적재 게이트 V_COUNT=0 은 그대로라 안전)
1230	                END IF;

1500	            BEGIN
1501	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------

1503	                V_LOG_TYPE    := '130';

1508	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
1509	            END;
1510	
1511	            /* ==========================================================================
1512	               [E20260923-DMF0014VALMSG] 동적(기간) 구간 = 셀 단위 검증 (사용자 지시 2026-09-23)
1513	                 종전 = UNPIVOT 집계 후 행 단위 'Invalid Quantity' 1건 — 어느 기간 칸이 문제인지 알 수 없었다.
1514	                 헤더행(V_TITLE)에 기간 값이 있는 칸(ATTRIBUTE14~200)마다, 기간 순서로 ' / ' 누적:
1515	                   ① 숫자 아닌 값          → 'Please Input QTY [YYYYMMDD: 값]'   [E20260923-DMF0014BLANK] 문구 이동
1516	                   ② 소수점(정수만 허용)    → 'Invalid Decimal [YYYYMMDD: 값]'   (E20230119-000186 정수 정책 유지)
1517	                   ③ 빈값                  → **검사하지 않는다** (오류 아님 — 사용자 지시 2026-09-23)
1518	                      - 구 정책(빈값 → 'Please Input QTY [기간]')은 폐기. 그에 딸린
1519	                        DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST VALID_YN='Y' 상관 EXISTS 도 함께 제거했다.
1520	                 한 행에 31칸 이상이면 30칸까지 표기 + ' ... (+N more)' (MESSAGE_TEXT 4000 보호, 12c 호환 — ON OVERFLOW 미사용)
1521	               ========================================================================== */
1522	            BEGIN
1523	                MERGE INTO SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD A
1524	                USING (SELECT Q.UPLOAD_ID
1525	                             ,LISTAGG(CASE WHEN Q.RN <= 30 THEN Q.MSG END, ' / ') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY Q.COL_IDX)
1526	                              || CASE WHEN MAX(Q.CNT) > 30 THEN ' ... (+' || TO_CHAR(MAX(Q.CNT) - 30) || ' more)' END AS MSGS
1527	                       FROM  (SELECT P.UPLOAD_ID, P.COL_IDX, P.MSG
1528	                                    ,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY P.UPLOAD_ID ORDER BY P.COL_IDX) RN
1529	                                    ,COUNT(*)     OVER (PARTITION BY P.UPLOAD_ID) CNT
1530	                              FROM  (SELECT V.UPLOAD_ID, V.COL_IDX
1531	                                           ,CASE WHEN TRIM(V.VALUE) IS NULL THEN NULL   /*[E20260923-DMF0014BLANK] 빈칸 = 오류 아님 (사용자 지시 2026-09-23).
1532	                                                        직전 회차의 'Please Input QTY [기간]' 빈값 검증을 폐기했다.
1533	                                                        그 문구는 아래 비숫자 분기로 이동한다. */
1534	                                                 WHEN NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(V.VALUE), '^-?[0-9]*\.?[0-9]+$')
1535	                                                 THEN 'Please Input QTY [' || TRIM(H.HDR) || ': ' || V.VALUE || ']'
1536	                                                 WHEN ROUND(TO_NUMBER(TRIM(V.VALUE)), 10) - FLOOR(ROUND(TO_NUMBER(TRIM(V.VALUE)), 10)) <> 0
1537	                                                 THEN 'Invalid Decimal [' || TRIM(H.HDR) || ': ' || V.VALUE || ']'
1538	                                            END AS MSG
1539	                                     FROM  (SELECT UPLOAD_ID, ATTRIBUTE1, ATTRIBUTE2, ATTRIBUTE10, COL_IDX, VALUE
1540	                                            FROM  (SELECT UPLOAD_ID, ATTRIBUTE1, ATTRIBUTE2, ATTRIBUTE10, ATTRIBUTE14, ATTRIBUTE15, ATTRIBUTE16, ATTRIBUTE17, ATTRIBUTE18, ATTRIBUTE19, 1566	
1567	                v_err_cnt := SQL%ROWCOUNT;
1568	                IF v_err_cnt > 0 THEN

1574	                    COMMIT;   -- ERROR 상태 확정 (전 검증 계속 수행 — E20260911-DMF0014MULTI)

Sales Site 오류 행은 제외*/
1637	                AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
1638	                        FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST CF, SCM_MST_DIVISION D2

1653	                    --   (행별 ERROR/MESSAGE_TEXT 마킹은 위에서 이미 끝났다. 적재 게이트 V_COUNT=0 은 그대로라 안전)

1657	
1658	            /* [E20260818-GRAYWEEK → E20260823-GRAYZERO] 회색(입력불가) 주차 정책 전환 ─────
1659	               종전: 회색 주차에 값이 있으면 스테이징 행을 ERROR 로 바꾸고 업로드 전체를 반려했다.
1660	               변경(사용자 지시 2026-08-23): **반려하지 않는다.** 그 칸만 0 으로 적재하고
1661	                 나머지 칸은 정상값 그대로 넣는다 — 적재부 INSTEAD 값 CASE 참조.
1662	               여기서는 몇 칸이 0 처리 대상인지만 세어 로그(175)에 남긴다. 상태 변경·RETURN 없음. */
1663	            BEGIN
1664	                SELECT COUNT(*)
1665	                INTO   v_err_cnt
1666	                FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD AEU
1667	                WHERE  AEU.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
1668	                AND    AEU.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
1669	                AND    AEU.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
1670	                AND    TO_CHAR(AEU.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
1671	                AND    AEU.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
1672	                AND    EXISTS (
1673	                        SELECT 1
1674	                        FROM   (SELECT CASE W.IDX
1675	                                         WHEN 14 THEN H.ATTRIBUTE14 WHEN 15 THEN H.ATTRIBUTE15
1676	                                         WHEN 16 THEN H.ATTRIBUTE16 WHEN 17 THEN H.ATTRIBUTE17
1677	                                         ELSE H.ATTRIBUTE18 END AS WK_RAW
1678	                                      ,W.IDX
1679	                                FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD H
1680	                                      ,(SELECT 14 IDX FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 15 FROM DUAL
1681	                                        UNION ALL SELECT 16 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 17 FROM DUAL
1682	                                        UNION ALL SELECT 18 FROM DUAL) W
1683	                                WHERE  H.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
1684	                                AND    H.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
1685	                                AND    H.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
1686	                                AND    TO_CHAR(H.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
1687	                                AND    (H.ATTRIBUTE1 IS NULL OR UPPER(H.ATTRIBUTE1) = 'DIVISION*')) WK
1688	                        WHERE  WK.WK_RAW IS NOT NULL
1689	                        AND    REGEXP_LIKE(WK.WK_RAW, '^[0-9.eE+-]+$')
1690	                        /* 값이 입력된 주차만 대상 (빈칸은 통과) */
1691	                        AND    (CASE WK.IDX
1692	                                  WHEN 14 THEN AEU.ATTRIBUTE14 WHEN 15 THEN AEU.ATTRIBUTE15
1693	                                  WHEN 16 THEN AEU.ATTRIBUTE16 WHEN 17 THEN AEU.ATTRIBUTE17
1694	                                  ELSE AEU.ATTRIBUTE18 END) IS NOT NULL
1695	                        AND    NOT EXISTS (
1696	                                SELECT 1
1697	                                FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST M, SCM_MST_DIVISION MD
1698	                                WHERE  M.VALID_YN = 'Y'
1699	                                AND    M.DIVISION_ID = MD.DIVISION_ID
1700	                                AND    (MD.DIVISION_ID = AEU.ATTRIBUTE1 OR MD.DIVISION_NAME = AEU.ATTRIBUTE1)
1701	                                AND    M.SCM_SITE_ID = AEU.ATTRIBUTE2
1702	                                AND    M.MEASURE     = AEU.ATTRIBUTE10
1703	                                AND    M.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE =
1704	                                       TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(WK.WK_RAW), 'FM99999999')));
1705	
1706	                IF v_err_cnt > 0 THEN
1707	                    V_LOG_TYPE    := '175';
1708	                    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## GRAYZERO: non-editable week value -> 0 (rows=' || v_err_cnt || ') ##########';
1709	                    V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
1710	                    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
1711	                    PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
1712	                END IF;
1713	            END;
1714	
1715	            BEGIN
1716	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
1717	                --scm2_mgr.xLOG('Invalid Project ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 9, TRUE);
1718	                V_LOG_TYPE    := '170';
1719	                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## Invalid Upload UOM Costpmer FCST. ##########';
1720	                V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
1721	                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
1722	                PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
1723	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
1724	            END;

1802	                V_LOG_TYPE    := '180';
1803	                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## SUCCESS ##########';
1804	                V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
1805	                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
1806	                PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);

1810	        END IF;

1812	        BEGIN
1813	            -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------
1814	            --scm2_mgr.xLOG('SUCCESS              ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 10, TRUE);
1815	            V_LOG_TYPE    := '190';
1816	            V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## INSERT INTO DATA START ##########';
1817	            V_DESCRIPTION := P_MODE; --TO_CHAR(v_creation_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3');
1818	            D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
1819	            PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
1820	            -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------
1821	        END;
1822	
1823	        --// SCM_UI_EXCEL_UPLOAD_T =>  DMR_CAPA_ALLOC_M
1824	        --//======================================================================================
1825	        SELECT COUNT(1)
1826	        INTO   V_COUNT
1827	        FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD T
1828	        WHERE  T.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
1829	        AND    T.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
1830	        AND    T.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
1831	        AND    TO_CHAR(T.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
1832	        AND    T.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR';
1833	
1834	        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE(V_COUNT); --20240604 SM
1835	
1836	        -- Update Mode and No Errors
1837	        d_ins_from := SYSDATE; /* ⭐ 적재 시작 시각 고정 — 아래 실재 재확인의 하한 */
1838	            -- ==========================================================================
1839	            -- [E20260911-DMF0014ACC] 검증 결과 반환 — 문구는 **원래대로** (사용자 지시 원복).
1840	            --   행별 사유는 SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.MESSAGE_TEXT 에 ' / ' 로 **전부 누적**되므로
1841	            --   Error Detail 에서 그 행의 오류 항목을 한 번에 확인한다.
1842	            --   전제: 각 검증의 즉시 RETURN 제거 + 검증 대상에 기존 ERROR 행 포함
1843	            --         (UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE IN ('UPLOAD','ERROR')) — 둘 다 있어야 누적이 성립한다.
1844	            -- ==========================================================================
1845	            IF V_COUNT > 0 THEN
1846	                V_LOG_TYPE    := '90';
1847	                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## ERROR Validation Check ##########';
1848	                V_DESCRIPTION := 'error rows=' || TO_CHAR(V_COUNT);
1849	                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
1850	                PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
1851	                COMMIT;
1852	                OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
1853	                SELECT '-1' AS CODE, 'ERR_RTN: - Validation Check' AS MSG, V_LOG_TYPE||','||V_LOG_MESSAGE AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
1854	                RETURN;
1855	            END IF;
1856	            
1857	            IF P_MODE > 0 AND V_COUNT = 0 THEN  -- [DMF0014 2-Step] mode=0=검증/분류만(적재 skip) · mode>0=적재 (P_MODE>0 게이트 복원 2026-07-10)
1858	            BEGIN

1860	                V_END_YYYYMMDD   := NULL;
1861	            END;

1864	                -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------

1866	                V_LOG_TYPE    := '200';

2318	                        AND    DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI.MEASURE = T.ATTRIBUTE10) > 0
2319	                AND    YYYYMMDD BETWEEN V_START_YYYYMMDD AND V_END_YYYYMMDD;
2320	
2321	                -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------
2322	                --scm2_mgr.xLOG('SUCCESS              ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 10, TRUE);
2323	                V_LOG_TYPE    := '230';
2324	                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## DELETE FROM DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI DATE END ##########';
2325	                V_DESCRIPTION := P_MODE; --TO_CHAR(v_creation_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3');
2326	                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
2327	                PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
2328	                -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------
2329	            END;
2330	            DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('230');
2331	
2332	            -- 엑셀로 업로드된 MEASURE를 MASTER의 MEASURE로 변경
2333	            UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD AA
2334	            SET    ATTRIBUTE10 =
2335	                   (SELECT BB.MEASURE
2336	                    FROM   (SELECT DISTINCT A.DIVISION_ID, B.DIVISION_NAME, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.VALID_YN, A.MEASURE, A.UPLOAD_UOM, A.UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE
2337	                            FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST A
2338	                            INNER  JOIN SCM_MST_DIVISION B
2339	                            ON     A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
2340	                            AND    A.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID) BB
2341	                    WHERE  (BB.DIVISION_ID = AA.ATTRIBUTE1 OR BB.DIVISION_NAME = AA.ATTRIBUTE1)
2342	                    AND    BB.SCM_SITE_ID = AA.ATTRIBUTE2
2343	                    AND    BB.MEASURE = AA.ATTRIBUTE10
2344	                    AND    BB.UPLOAD_UOM = UPPER(AA.ATTRIBUTE11)
2345	                    AND    BB.UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE IN (
2346	
2347	                                                     SELECT CASE (NVL(TO_DATE(Z.ATTRIBUTE16, 'YYYYMMDD') - TO_DATE(Z.ATTRIBUTE15, 'YYYYMMDD'), TO_DATE(Z.ATTRIBUTE15, 'YYYYMMDD') - TO_DATE(Z.ATTRIBUTE14, 'YYYYMMDD')))  /* [BUCKET FIX 07-06] 부분 첫주(월1일)=2~3번째 일자 간격(완전주) 우선 */
2348	                                                                 WHEN 1 THEN
2349	                                                                  'DAILY'
2350	                                                                 WHEN 7 THEN
2351	                                                                  'WEEKLY'
2352	                                                                 ELSE
2353	                                                                  'MONTHLY'
2354	                                                             END
2355	                                                     FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD Z
2356	                                                     WHERE  Z.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
2357	                                                     AND    Z.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
2358	                                                     AND    Z.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
2359	                                                     AND    TO_CHAR(Z.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
2360	                                                     AND    Z.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'V_TITLE')
2361	                    AND    ROWNUM = 1)
2362	            WHERE  AA.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
2363	            AND    AA.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
2364	            AND    AA.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
2365	            AND    AA.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'SUCCESS'
2366	            AND    TO_CHAR(AA.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE;

3246	                AND    C.YYYYMMDD IS NOT NULL
3247	                AND    V.VALUE IS NOT NULL;
3248	
3249	                v_ins_cnt := SQL%ROWCOUNT; /* ⭐ [허위성공 차단 ①] 0행이면 조인/필터가 무너진 것 */
3250	
3251	            DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('240');
3252	            -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------
3253	            --scm2_mgr.xLOG('SUCCESS              ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 10, TRUE);
3254	            V_LOG_TYPE    := '240';
3255	            V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## INSERT INTO DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI DATE END ##########';
3256	            V_DESCRIPTION := P_MODE; --TO_CHAR(v_creation_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3');
3257	            D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
3258	            PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
3259	            -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------
3260	            IF (V_DIVISION = '40') THEN
3261	                P_PR_DM_PLN_CUST_FCST_TO_MKT_FCST_40('40');
3262	            ELSE
3263	                DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('20240605-');
3264	                IF to_char(SYSDATE,'yyyymmdd') >= '20260412' THEN -- poc 테스트 위함.
3265	                P_PR_DM_PLN_CUST_FCST_TO_MKT_FCST;
3266	                END IF;
3267	            END IF;
3268	
3269	            COMMIT;
3270	            -- END TRY
3271	
3272	            -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------
3273	            --scm2_mgr.xLOG('SUCCESS              ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 10, TRUE);
3274	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';
3275	            V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## INSERT INTO DATA END ##########';
3276	            V_DESCRIPTION := P_MODE; --TO_CHAR(v_creation_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3');
3277	            D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
3278	            PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
3279	            -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------
3280	
3281	        END IF;
3282	        COMMIT;
3283	        /* ⭐ [허위성공 차단 ②] 적재 모드인데 원장에 남은 게 없으면 성공으로 보고하지 않는다.
3284	           v_ins_cnt 만으로는 부족하다 — 하위 프로시저가 ROLLBACK 하면 행은 사라져도 이 변수는
3285	           롤백 이전 값을 그대로 들고 있다(2026-08-14 실사고). 그래서 원장을 다시 센다. */
3286	        IF NVL(P_MODE, 0) > 0 THEN
3287	            SELECT COUNT(*)
3288	            INTO   v_live_cnt
3289	            FROM   DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI
3290	            WHERE  CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
3291	            AND    CREATION_DATE >= d_ins_from;
3292	
3293	            IF NVL(v_ins_cnt, 0) = 0 OR NVL(v_live_cnt, 0) = 0 THEN
3294	                BEGIN
3295	                    PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, 'ERROR', N_LOG_COUNT
3296	                      , '########## SAVE VERIFY FAILED — inserted=' || NVL(v_ins_cnt, 0)
3297	                        || ' / live=' || NVL(v_live_cnt, 0) || ' ##########'
3298	                      , P_MODE, SYSTIMESTAMP);
3299	                EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
3300	                OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
3301	                    SELECT '-1' AS CODE
3302	                         , 'ERR_RTN: - Save verify failed (inserted=' || NVL(v_ins_cnt, 0)
3303	                           || ', persisted=' || NVL(v_live_cnt, 0) || ')' AS MSG
3304	                         , 'SAVE_VERIFY' AS EVENT
3305	                    FROM   DUAL;
3306	                RETURN;
3307	            END IF;
3308	        END IF;
3309	
3310	        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
3311	            SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS MSG, TO_CHAR(N_LOG_COUNT) AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
3312	
3313	    EXCEPTION
3314	        WHEN OTHERS THEN
3315	            ROLLBACK;
3316	            PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME
3317	                                ,'ERROR'
3318	                                ,0
3319	                                ,SQLERRM
3320	                                ,NULL
3321	                                ,SYSDATE
3322	                                ,P_IN_PARA_DATA  => V_IN_PARA_DATA
3323	                                ,P_SQL_ERR_CODE  => SQLCODE
3324	                                ,P_SQL_ERR_MSG   => SQLERRM
3325	                                ,P_SQL_BACKTRACE => DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE);
3326	            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001
3327	                                   ,'[' || V_PROGRAM_NAME || '] SQLCODE=' || SQLCODE || ' / SQLERRM=' || SQLERRM || ' / BACKTRACE=' ||
3328	                                    DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE || ' / PARAMS=' || V_IN_PARA_DATA);
3329	    END P_DMF0014_CUST_FCST_EXCEL_UPLOAD;
3330	
3331	    PROCEDURE P_DMF0014_CUST_FCST_EXCEL_UPLOAD_SM
3332	    (
3333	        P_MODE          INTEGER
3334	       ,P_CREATED_BY    VARCHAR2
3335	       ,O_RESULT_CURSOR OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
3336	    )
3337	    /*
3338	        * Program ID   : DMF0014_CUST_FCST_EXCEL_UPLOAD_SM
3339	        * Description  : DMF0014_CUST_FCST_EXCEL_UPLOAD_SM
3340	        * Program History
3341	        *===========================================================================================================
3342	        *   Date       In Charge      Description
3343	        *===========================================================================================================
3344	        * 2018. 01. 02   SongDH     Initial Release PJ20170278 전지본부 자동차 GSCM(DM/SCP) 구축 프로젝트
3345	        * 2022. 10. 26     KDW2819    [C20221024-000507]CUST FCST Upload 오류 수정
3346	        * 2023. 01. 25   kdmcc14 [E20230119-000186-RPA 고객 FCST 가 소수점 단위로 Upload 될 경우 Error 팝업 및 사유 알람기능]
3347	        * 2023. 02. 28   chano0612 [E20230227-000456]소형사업부 RPA 업로드 시 GSCM Fail 발생 -- EXCEL UPLOAD
3348	        * 2024. 02. 15   kcw3408   [E20240208-001344]고객 수요 분석 시스템_Cust.FCST upload 문의의 건(데이타 없는데 Ctrl + End 키로는 인식되어 Length가 0인 컬럼을 Check 추가)
3349	         * 2026.03.24 김준규            [PJ20264226] NextSCM Pilot 설계 및 개발
3350	        */
3351	        --DMF0014_CUST_FCST_EXCEL_UPLOAD_SM('1', 'hwayoung.lim');
3352	     AS
3353	
3354	        V_IN_PARA_DATA   VARCHAR2(4000);
3355	        V_STEP           VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
3356	        V_PROGRAM_ID     VARCHAR2(40 CHAR) := 'DMF0014';
3357	        V_SHEET_NAME     VARCHAR2(40 CHAR) := 'FCST Upload';
3358	        V_SQL_TEXT       VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
3359	        V_CREATION_DATE  VARCHAR2(40 CHAR);
3360	        V_COUNT          INTEGER;
3361	        V_START_YYYYMMDD VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
3362	        V_END_YYYYMMDD   VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
3363	        V_DIVISION       VARCHAR2(10 CHAR);
3364	        V_PROGRAM_NAME   VARCHAR2(150 CHAR) := $$PLSQL_UNIT;
3365	        V_LOG_TYPE       VARCHAR2(30 CHAR);
3366	        N_LOG_COUNT      INTEGER := 0;
3367	        V_LOG_MESSAGE    VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);
3368	        V_DESCRIPTION    VARCHAR2(500 CHAR);
3369	        D_EVENT_DATE     DATE;
3370	    BEGIN
3371	
3372	        IF UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1).COUNT > 1 THEN
3373	            V_PROGRAM_NAME := $$PLSQL_UNIT || '.' || UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1) (2);
3374	        END IF;
3375	
3376	        V_IN_PARA_DATA := JSON_OBJECT('p_mode' VALUE P_MODE, 'p_created_by' VALUE P_CREATED_BY);
3377	
3378	        -- LOG 용 변수 설정
3379	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'START';
3380	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## Start of procedure ##########';
3381	        V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
3382	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
3383	        PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
3384	
3385	        SELECT TO_CHAR(MAX(T.CREATION_DATE), 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3')
3386	        INTO   V_CREATION_DATE
3387	        FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD T
3388	        WHERE  T.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
3389	        AND    T.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
3390	        AND    T.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY;
3391	        --  AND UPPER(METADATA1_VALUE) != 'BASIS YYYYMM';
3392	        IF V_CREATION_DATE IS NULL THEN
3393	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';
3394	            V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';
3395	            V_DESCRIPTION := 'Data Not Found [' || TO_CHAR(V_CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') || ']';
3396	            D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
3397	            PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
3398	        END IF;
3399	
3400	        --DMF0014_CUST_FCST_EXCEL_UPLOAD_SM('1', 'hwayoung.lim');
3401	        BEGIN
3402	            SELECT ATTRIBUTE1
3403	            INTO   V_DIVISION
3404	            FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
3405	            WHERE  PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
3406	            AND    SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
3407	            AND    CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
3408	            AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
3409	            AND    UPPER(ATTRIBUTE1) <> 'DIVISION*'
3410	                  --AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'SUCCESS'
3411	            AND    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
3412	            FETCH  FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;
3413	        END;
3414	
3415	        -- p_mode = 0 이면 Validation
3416	        IF NVL(P_MODE, 0) = 0 THEN
3417	            BEGIN
3418	                UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD T
3419	                SET    UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD', MESSAGE_TEXT = NULL
3420	                WHERE  T.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
3421	                AND    T.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
3422	                AND    T.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
3423	                AND    TO_CHAR(T.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
3424	                AND    NVL(T.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE, 'X') != 'UPLOAD';
3425	            END;

3428	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------

3431	                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## RESET Status = UPLOAD ##########';
3432	                V_DESCRIPTION := P_MODE;
3433	                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
3434	                PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
3435	                -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
3436	            END;

3523	                        AND    T.COL3 = ATTRIBUTE3

4816	            AND    CREATED_BY         = p_created_by
4817	            AND    TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = v_creation_date

4819	            AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
4820	                            FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL CM
4821	                            WHERE  CM.DIVISION_ID           = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1

4830	            BEGIN
4831	            -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
4832	            --scm2_mgr.xLOG('Invalid Model ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 10, TRUE);
4833	            v_log_type := '17';

4838	            -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
4839	            END;
4840	            */

5325	                -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------
5326	                --scm2_mgr.xLOG('SUCCESS              ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 10, TRUE);
5327	                V_LOG_TYPE    := '20-1';
5328	                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## DELETE FROM DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI DATE END ##########';
5329	                V_DESCRIPTION := P_MODE; --TO_CHAR(v_creation_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3');
5330	                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
5331	                PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
5332	                -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------
5333	                DELETE FROM DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI
5334	                WHERE  1 = 1
5335	                AND    MEASURE_GROUP = 'CUST_FCST'
5336	                      -- modified by ytchoi 180305  incoterms, fcst_type, dom_exp 조건 없어서 추가.
5337	                AND    (SELECT COUNT(1)
5338	                        FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD T
5339	                        WHERE  T.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
5340	                        AND    T.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
5341	                        AND    T.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
5342	                        AND    TO_CHAR(T.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5343	                        AND    T.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'SUCCESS'
5344	                        AND    DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI.DIVISION_ID = T.ATTRIBUTE1
5345	                        AND    DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI.SCM_SITE_ID = T.ATTRIBUTE2
5346	                        AND    DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI.PLANT_SITE_ID = T.ATTRIBUTE3
5347	                        AND    DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI.CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE = T.ATTRIBUTE4
5348	                        AND    DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI.CUSTOMER_MODEL = T.ATTRIBUTE5
5349	                        AND    DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI.INCOTERMS = T.ATTRIBUTE6
5350	                        AND    DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI.FCST_TYPE = T.ATTRIBUTE7
5351	                        AND    DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI.DOM_EXP = T.ATTRIBUTE8
5352	                        AND    DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI.MEASURE = T.ATTRIBUTE10) > 0
5353	                AND    YYYYMMDD BETWEEN V_START_YYYYMMDD AND V_END_YYYYMMDD;
5354	
5355	                -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------
5356	                --scm2_mgr.xLOG('SUCCESS              ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 10, TRUE);
5357	                V_LOG_TYPE    := '20';
5358	                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## DELETE FROM DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI DATE END ##########';
5359	                V_DESCRIPTION := P_MODE; --TO_CHAR(v_creation_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3');
5360	                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
5361	                PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
5362	                -- -------------------------------------------------------------------------
5363	            END;
5364	            DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('20');
