5366	            -- 엑셀로 업로드된 MEASURE를 MASTER의 MEASURE로 변경
5367	            UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD AA
5368	            SET    ATTRIBUTE10 =
5369	                   (SELECT BB.MEASURE
5370	                    FROM   (SELECT DISTINCT A.DIVISION_ID, B.DIVISION_NAME, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.VALID_YN, A.MEASURE, A.UPLOAD_UOM, A.UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE
5371	                            FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST A
5372	                            INNER  JOIN SCM_MST_DIVISION B
5373	                            ON     A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
5374	                            AND    A.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID) BB
5375	                    WHERE  (BB.DIVISION_ID = AA.ATTRIBUTE1 OR BB.DIVISION_NAME = AA.ATTRIBUTE1)
5376	                    AND    BB.SCM_SITE_ID = AA.ATTRIBUTE2
5377	                    AND    BB.MEASURE = AA.ATTRIBUTE10
5378	                    AND    BB.UPLOAD_UOM = AA.ATTRIBUTE11
5379	                    AND    BB.UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE IN (SELECT CASE (NVL(TO_DATE(Z.ATTRIBUTE16, 'YYYYMMDD') - TO_DATE(Z.ATTRIBUTE15, 'YYYYMMDD'), TO_DATE(Z.ATTRIBUTE15, 'YYYYMMDD') - TO_DATE(Z.ATTRIBUTE14, 'YYYYMMDD')))  /* [BUCKET FIX 07-06] 부분 첫주(월1일)=2~3번째 일자 간격(완전주) 우선 */
5380	                                                                WHEN 1 THEN
5381	                                                                 'DAILY'
5382	                                                                WHEN 7 THEN
5383	                                                                 'WEEKLY'
5384	                                                                ELSE
5385	                                                                 'MONTHLY'
5386	                                                            END
5387	                                                     FROM   SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD Z
5388	                                                     WHERE  Z.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
5389	                                                     AND    Z.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
5390	                                                     AND    Z.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
5391	                                                     AND    TO_CHAR(Z.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5392	                                                     AND    Z.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'V_TITLE')
5393	                    FETCH  FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY)
5394	            WHERE  AA.PROGRAM_ID = V_PROGRAM_ID
5395	            AND    AA.SHEET_NAME = V_SHEET_NAME
5396	            AND    AA.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY
5397	            AND    AA.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'SUCCESS'
5398	            AND    TO_CHAR(AA.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5399	            AND    EXISTS (SELECT 1
5400	                    FROM   (SELECT DISTINCT A.DIVISION_ID, B.DIVISION_NAME, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.MEASURE, A.UPLOAD_UOM, A.UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE
5401	                            FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST A
5402	                            INNER  JOIN SCM_MST_DIVISION B
5403	                            ON     A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
5404	                            AND    A.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID) BB
5405	                    WHERE  (BB.DIVISION_ID = AA.ATTRIBUTE1 OR BB.DIVISION_NAME = AA.ATTRIBUTE1)
5406	                    AND    BB.SCM_SITE_ID = AA.ATTRIBUTE2
5407	                    AND    BB.MEASURE = AA.ATTRIBUTE10
5408	                    AND    BB.UPLOAD_UOM = AA.ATTRIBUTE11);

6330	    PROCEDURE P_DMF0014_GET_CUST_FCST_UPLOAD_DATA
6331	    (
6332	        P_DIVISION_ID    VARCHAR2
6333	       ,P_DEPT_ID        VARCHAR2
6334	       ,P_END_CUSTOMER   VARCHAR2
6335	       ,P_SCM_SITE_ID    VARCHAR2
6336	       ,P_PLANT_SITE_ID  VARCHAR2
6337	       ,P_PROJECT_CODE   VARCHAR2
6338	       ,P_ITEM_ID        VARCHAR2
6339	       ,P_START_YYYYMMDD VARCHAR2
6340	       ,P_END_YYYYMMDD   VARCHAR2
6341	       ,P_BUCKET_TYPE    VARCHAR2
6342	       ,P_DOM_EXP        VARCHAR2
6343	       ,P_CREATED_BY     VARCHAR2
6344	       , --, p_debug OUT VARCHAR2
6345	        P_USERINFO       VARCHAR2
6346	       ,P_USER_ID        VARCHAR2
6347	       ,P_MENU_ID        VARCHAR2
6348	       ,O_RC1            OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
6349	       ,O_RESULT_CURSOR  OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
6350	    )
6351	    /*
6352	         DMF0014_GET_CUST_FCST_UPLOAD_DATA(p_division_id='81', p_dept_id='ALL', p_end_customer='ALL', p_scm_site_id='ALL', p_plant_site_id='ALL', p_project_code = NULL);
6353	         , p_item_id = NULL, p_start_yyyymmdd='20230904', p_end_yyyymmdd='20240310', p_bucket_type='WEEKLY', p_dom_exp='ALL', p_created_by='chano0612', p_userinfo='ADMIN'
6354	        */
6355	     AS
6356	
6357	        V_PROGRAM_NAME   VARCHAR2(200) := $$PLSQL_UNIT;
6358	        V_SQL_TEXT       CLOB;  -- [CLOB FIX 07-06] 32767->CLOB (GET DAILY 184col>32767)
6359	        V_ITERATION      CLOB;  -- [CLOB FIX 07-06] 4000->CLOB (GET WEEKLY 27col 누적>4000 ORA-06502)
6360	        V_COLUMN_TEXT    VARCHAR2(200 CHAR);
6361	        V_COLUMN_NAME    VARCHAR2(50 CHAR);
6362	        V_YYYYMMDD       VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
6363	        V_START_YYYYMMDD VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
6364	        V_GROUP_ID       VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) := NULL;
6365	        V_CNT_1          INTEGER := 0;
6366	        V_IN_PARA_DATA   VARCHAR2(4000);
6367	        V_STEP           VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
6368	        V_LOG_TYPE       VARCHAR2(20) := NULL;
6369	        N_LOG_COUNT      NUMBER := 0;
6370	        V_LOG_MESSAGE    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
6371	        V_DESCRIPTION    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
6372	        D_EVENT_DATE     DATE := SYSDATE;
6373	        V_PROC_INPUT     CLOB := NULL;
6374	        V_SQL_CODE       VARCHAR2(100) := NULL;
6375	        V_SQL_ERRMSG     VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
6376	        V_BACKTRACE      VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
6377	    BEGIN
6378	
6379	        IF UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1).COUNT > 1 THEN
6380	            V_PROGRAM_NAME := $$PLSQL_UNIT || '.' || UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1) (2);
6381	        END IF;
6382	
6383	        V_IN_PARA_DATA := JSON_OBJECT('p_division_id' VALUE P_DIVISION_ID
6384	                                     ,'p_dept_id' VALUE P_DEPT_ID
6385	                                     ,'p_end_customer' VALUE P_END_CUSTOMER
6386	                                     ,'p_scm_site_id' VALUE P_SCM_SITE_ID
6387	                                     ,'p_plant_site_id' VALUE P_PLANT_SITE_ID
6388	                                     ,'p_project_code' VALUE P_PROJECT_CODE
6389	                                     ,'p_item_id' VALUE P_ITEM_ID
6390	                                     ,'p_start_yyyymmdd' VALUE P_START_YYYYMMDD
6391	                                     ,'p_end_yyyymmdd' VALUE P_END_YYYYMMDD
6392	                                     ,'p_bucket_type' VALUE P_BUCKET_TYPE
6393	                                     ,'p_dom_exp' VALUE P_DOM_EXP
6394	                                     ,'p_created_by' VALUE P_CREATED_BY);
6395	
6396	        IF UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1).COUNT > 1 THEN
6397	            V_PROGRAM_NAME := $$PLSQL_UNIT || '.' || UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1) (2);
6398	        END IF;
6399	
6400	        --  v_group_id := 'ADMIN'
6401	        -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6402	        --            20200311 KHSONG C20200103-000586 권한그룹을 LOOKUP관리 (BEGIN)
6403	        -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6404	        SELECT COUNT(1)
6405	        INTO   V_CNT_1
6406	        FROM   SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
6407	        WHERE  LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP'
6408	        AND    VALID_YN = 'Y'
6409	        AND    SEGMENT2 = 'ALL' -- 'ALL'인경우 DM Planner (W/S) 제외 / 주석처리인경우 DM Planner (W/S) 포함
6410	        AND    SEGMENT1 = P_USERINFO;
6411	        IF V_CNT_1 > 0 THEN
6412	            V_GROUP_ID := 'ADMIN';
6413	        END IF;
6414	        -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6415	        --            20200311 KHSONG C20200103-000586 권한그룹을 LOOKUP관리 (END)
6416	        -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6417	        SELECT A.BASIS_START_DATE INTO V_START_YYYYMMDD FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A WHERE A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = P_START_YYYYMMDD;
6418	
6419	        V_ITERATION := NULL;
6420	
6421	        IF P_BUCKET_TYPE = 'DAILY' THEN
6422	            -- c_cust_fcst CURSOR FAST_FORWARD FOR
6423	            FOR REC IN (SELECT 'D_' || BASIS_YYYYMMDD AS COLUMN_NAME, BASIS_YYYYMMDD YYYYMMDD
6424	                        FROM   SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
6425	                        WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= V_START_YYYYMMDD --TO_CHAR(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'YYYYMMDD')
6426	                        AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= P_END_YYYYMMDD --TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 6), 'YYYYMMDD')
6427	                        ORDER  BY COLUMN_NAME) LOOP
6428	
6429	                V_COLUMN_NAME := REC.COLUMN_NAME;
6430	                V_YYYYMMDD    := REC.YYYYMMDD;
6431	                V_COLUMN_TEXT := ', SUM(CASE WHEN CP.YYYYMMDD = ''v_yyyymmdd'' THEN CP.QTY ELSE NULL END) AS v_column_name ';
6432	
6433	                V_COLUMN_TEXT := REPLACE(V_COLUMN_TEXT, 'v_yyyymmdd', V_YYYYMMDD);
6434	                V_COLUMN_TEXT := REPLACE(V_COLUMN_TEXT, 'v_column_name', V_COLUMN_NAME);
6435	
6436	                V_ITERATION := V_ITERATION || V_COLUMN_TEXT;
6437	
6438	            END LOOP;
6439	
6440	        ELSIF P_BUCKET_TYPE = 'WEEKLY' THEN
6441	            -- c_cust_fcst CURSOR FAST_FORWARD FOR
6442	            FOR REC IN (SELECT 'D_' || MIN(A.BASIS_START_DATE) COLUMN_NAME, A.ISO_YYYYWW --A.BASIS_YYYYMM + MIN(SUBSTR('00' || TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(A.BASIS_START_DATE, 'IW')), 'YYYY-MM-DD'), -2)) YYYYMMDD
6443	                        FROM   SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
6444	                        WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= V_START_YYYYMMDD --TO_CHAR(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'YYYYMMDD')
6445	                        AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= P_END_YYYYMMDD --TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 6), 'YYYYMMDD')
6446	                        GROUP  BY A.ISO_YYYYWW, A.WEEK_NAME
6447	                        ORDER  BY COLUMN_NAME) LOOP
6448	
6449	                V_COLUMN_NAME := REC.COLUMN_NAME;
6450	                V_YYYYMMDD    := REC.ISO_YYYYWW;
6451	
6452	                V_COLUMN_TEXT := ', SUM(CASE WHEN SUBSTR(CP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 4) || LPAD(TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(CP.YYYYMMDD, ''YYYYMMDD''), ''IW''), 2, ''0'') = ''v_yyyymmdd'' THEN CP.QTY ELSE NULL END) AS v_column_name ';
6453	
6454	                V_COLUMN_TEXT := REPLACE(V_COLUMN_TEXT, 'v_yyyymmdd', V_YYYYMMDD);
6455	                V_COLUMN_TEXT := REPLACE(V_COLUMN_TEXT, 'v_column_name', V_COLUMN_NAME);
6456	
6457	                V_ITERATION := V_ITERATION || V_COLUMN_TEXT;
6458	
6459	            END LOOP;
6460	
6461	        ELSIF P_BUCKET_TYPE = 'MONTHLY' THEN
6462	            -- c_cust_fcst CURSOR FAST_FORWARD FOR
6463	            FOR REC IN (SELECT 'D_' || MIN(A.BASIS_YYYYMM) || '01' AS COLUMN_NAME, MIN(A.BASIS_YYYYMM) YYYYMMDD
6464	                        FROM   SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
6465	                        WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= V_START_YYYYMMDD --TO_CHAR(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'YYYYMMDD')
6466	                        AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= P_END_YYYYMMDD --TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 6), 'YYYYMMDD')
6467	                        GROUP  BY A.MONTH_NAME
6468	                        ORDER  BY COLUMN_NAME) LOOP
6469	
6470	                V_COLUMN_NAME := REC.COLUMN_NAME;
6471	                V_YYYYMMDD    := REC.YYYYMMDD;
6472	                V_COLUMN_TEXT := ', SUM(CASE WHEN SUBSTR(CP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = ''v_yyyymmdd'' THEN CP.QTY ELSE NULL END) AS v_column_name ';
6473	
6474	                V_COLUMN_TEXT := REPLACE(V_COLUMN_TEXT, 'v_yyyymmdd', V_YYYYMMDD);
6475	                V_COLUMN_TEXT := REPLACE(V_COLUMN_TEXT, 'v_column_name', V_COLUMN_NAME);
6476	
6477	                V_ITERATION := V_ITERATION || V_COLUMN_TEXT;
6478	
6479	            END LOOP;
6480	        END IF;
6481	
6482	        V_SQL_TEXT := ';
6483	        SELECT CP.DIVISION_ID
6484	             , CP.SCM_SITE_ID
6485	             , CP.PLANT_SITE_ID
6486	             , CP.CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE
6487	             , CP.CUSTOMER_MODEL
6488	             , CP.INCOTERMS
6489	             , CP.FCST_TYPE
6490	             , CP.DOM_EXP
6491	             , CP.MEASURE_GROUP
6492	             , CP.MEASURE
6493	             , CP.UPLOAD_UOM
6494	             , CP.PROJECT_CODE
6495	             , CP.ITEM_ID
6496	        --[PJ2024-50461] Team Mapping------------------------------------
6497	             --, CASE WHEN ((FN_GET_DEPT_LIST(''p_created_by'', S.DEPT_ID) = ''Y'') OR ''v_group_id'' =''ADMIN'')
6498	                --THEN ''Y'' ELSE ''N'' END AS EDIT_FLAG
6499	             --, CASE WHEN ((FN_GET_DEPT_LIST(''p_created_by'', TEAM.DEPT_ID) = ''Y'') OR ''v_group_id'' =''ADMIN'')
6500	             --   THEN ''Y'' ELSE ''N'' END AS EDIT_FLAG
6501	             -- 2026-04-01 김준규 SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM 의 DEPT_ID를 사용 시작
6502	             , CASE WHEN ((FN_GET_DEPT_LIST(''p_created_by'', ITM.DEPT_ID) = ''Y'') OR ''v_group_id'' =''ADMIN'')
6503	                THEN ''Y'' ELSE ''N'' END AS EDIT_FLAG
6504	             -- 2026-04-01 김준규 SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM 의 DEPT_ID를 사용 종료
6505	        --[PJ2024-50461] Team Mapping------------------------------------
6506	             v_iteration
6507	             ,MAX(TEI.EDIT_YN) EDIT_YN
6508	        FROM   DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI CP
6509	        INNER  JOIN TABLE(PKG_SCM_USER_MNU.PR_SCM_EDIT_YN(''' || P_USER_ID || ''', ''' || P_MENU_ID || ''')) TEI
6510	        ON     TEI.UNIT_ID = CP.DIVISION_ID

6519	                ON S.DIVISION_ID = ITM.DIVISION_ID
6520	                AND S.SCM_SITE_ID = ITM.SCM_SITE_ID
6521	                AND CP.PROJECT_CODE = ITM.PROJECT_CODE--[PJ2024-50611] 조인 추가

6523	         -- 2026-04-01 김준규 팀 변경 필요 없게 됨 시작
6524	         --LEFT JOIN (

6532	         -- 2026-04-01 김준규 팀 변경 필요 없게 됨 종료
6533	--[PJ2024-50461] Team Mapping------------------------------------
6534	        WHERE  1 = 1
6535	        AND    CP.DIVISION_ID   = S.DIVISION_ID
6536	        AND    CP.SCM_SITE_ID   = S.SCM_SITE_ID

6575	               , CP.PLANT_SITE_ID

7125	        WHEN OTHERS THEN
7126	            -- ERROR LOG
7127	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';
7128	            V_LOG_MESSAGE := SUBSTR(SQLERRM, 1, 1000);
7129	            V_DESCRIPTION := 'ERROR_LINE : ' || TO_CHAR(NULL);
7130	            D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
7131	            NULL;
7132	
7133	            PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
7134	
7135	            -- END CATCH
7136	
7137	            -- 종료 LOG
7138	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';
7139	            V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';
7140	            V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
7141	            D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
7142	
7143	            PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
7144	
7145	            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001
7146	                                   ,'[' || V_PROGRAM_NAME || '] SQLCODE=' || SQLCODE || ' / SQLERRM=' || SQLERRM || ' / BACKTRACE=' ||
7147	                                    DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE || ' / PARAMS=' || V_IN_PARA_DATA);
7148	    END P_DMF0014_SAVE_CUST_FCST_UPLOAD_DATA;
7149	
7150	
7151	    ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7152	    -- [E20260814-EXCELFORM] DMF0014 Excel Form 조회 (hook #135 — Excel Form/Upload 표준)
7153	    --   요구: "EXCEL UPLOAD 기능이 있는 경우 DMF0082 의 EXCEL FORM/UPLOAD 양식을 기본 RULE 로"
7154	    --   계약:
7155	    --     · IN  = 메인 GET(P_DMF0014_GET_CUST_FCST_UPLOAD_DATA) 과 **동일 축·동일 이름 15개**
7156	    --             → FE 가 store.filters 를 가공 없이 전달하면 그대로 성립한다.
7157	    --     · OUT = 업로드 양식의 **정보 컬럼 13종**(SCM_ADM_EXCEL_COLUMNS.HEADER_CONTENT
7158	    --             ATTRIBUTE1~13 과 1:1). 값 컬럼 W27~W31(ATTRIBUTE14~18)은 **입력 전용**이라
7159	    --             SP 가 내리지 않는다 — FE 가 빈칸으로 채운다(hook #135 §3-4).
7160	    --     · 본문은 메인 GET 의 FROM/WHERE 를 그대로 쓰고 **동적 기간 컬럼(v_iteration)만 제외**한다.
7161	    --       (조회조건에 걸린 데이터 = 양식 대상 이라는 §3-1 정의를 구조적으로 보장)
7162	    ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7163	    PROCEDURE P_DMF0014_GET_EXCEL_FORM_DATA
7164	    (
7165	        P_DIVISION_ID    VARCHAR2
7166	       ,P_DEPT_ID        VARCHAR2
7167	       ,P_END_CUSTOMER   VARCHAR2
7168	       ,P_SCM_SITE_ID    VARCHAR2
7169	       ,P_PLANT_SITE_ID  VARCHAR2
7170	       ,P_PROJECT_CODE   VARCHAR2
7171	       ,P_ITEM_ID        VARCHAR2
7172	       ,P_START_YYYYMMDD VARCHAR2
7173	       ,P_END_YYYYMMDD   VARCHAR2
7174	       ,P_BUCKET_TYPE    VARCHAR2
7175	       ,P_DOM_EXP        VARCHAR2
7176	       ,P_CREATED_BY     VARCHAR2
7177	       ,P_USERINFO       VARCHAR2
7178	       ,P_USER_ID        VARCHAR2
7179	       ,P_MENU_ID        VARCHAR2
7180	       ,O_RC1            OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
7181	       ,O_RESULT_CURSOR  OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
7182	    )
7183	    AS
7184	        V_PROGRAM_NAME VARCHAR2(200) := $$PLSQL_UNIT;
7185	        V_SQL_TEXT     CLOB;
7186	        V_GROUP_ID     VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) := NULL;
7187	        V_CNT_1        INTEGER := 0;
7188	        V_DEPT_SQL     VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
7189	        V_LOG_TYPE     VARCHAR2(20) := NULL;
7190	        N_LOG_COUNT    NUMBER := 0;
7191	        V_LOG_MESSAGE  VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
7192	        V_DESCRIPTION  VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
7193	        D_EVENT_DATE   DATE := SYSDATE;
7194	        V_PROC_INPUT   CLOB := NULL;
7195	        V_SQL_CODE     VARCHAR2(100) := NULL;
7196	        V_SQL_ERRMSG   VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
7197	        V_BACKTRACE    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
7198	    BEGIN
7199	        V_PROC_INPUT := JSON_OBJECT('p_division_id' VALUE P_DIVISION_ID
7200	                                   ,'p_dept_id' VALUE P_DEPT_ID
7201	                                   ,'p_end_customer' VALUE P_END_CUSTOMER
7202	                                   ,'p_scm_site_id' VALUE P_SCM_SITE_ID
7203	                                   ,'p_plant_site_id' VALUE P_PLANT_SITE_ID
7204	                                   ,'p_project_code' VALUE P_PROJECT_CODE
7205	                                   ,'p_item_id' VALUE P_ITEM_ID
7206	                                   ,'p_start_yyyymmdd' VALUE P_START_YYYYMMDD
7207	                                   ,'p_end_yyyymmdd' VALUE P_END_YYYYMMDD
7208	                                   ,'p_bucket_type' VALUE P_BUCKET_TYPE
7209	                                   ,'p_dom_exp' VALUE P_DOM_EXP
7210	                                   ,'p_created_by' VALUE P_CREATED_BY);
7211	
7212	        SELECT COUNT(1)
7213	        INTO   V_CNT_1
7214	        FROM   SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
7215	        WHERE  LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP'
7216	        AND    VALID_YN = 'Y'
7217	        AND    SEGMENT2 = 'ALL'
7218	        AND    SEGMENT1 = P_USERINFO;
7219	
7220	        IF NVL(V_CNT_1, 0) > 0 THEN
7221	            V_GROUP_ID := 'ADMIN';
7222	        ELSE
7223	            V_GROUP_ID := P_USERINFO;
7224	        END IF;
7225	
7226	        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7227	        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7228	        -- [E20260827-EXCELFORM-HDRONLY] Excel Form = 데이터 없이 **헤더만** (사용자 요청 2026-08-27).
7229	        --   종전 V7(키 조합 실데이터 전량)은 백업에 보존. 커서 컬럼명·시그니처는 FE 계약대로 유지하되
7230	        --   식별 컬럼은 전부 NULL 인 헤더 캐리어 1행만 반환한다:
7231	        --     PERIOD_BUCKETS     = 조회구간(P_START~P_END)·버킷타입의 버킷 일자 전량 → FE 값 컬럼 생성
7232	        --     UPLOAD_VALID_WEEKS = 그중 division 의 DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST VALID_YN='Y' 버킷 → 입력가능 표식
7233	        --   FE(CustomerFcstTemplateModal)는 SCM_SITE_ID NULL 행을 헤더 캐리어로 판정해 데이터행 0건으로 내보낸다.
7234	        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7235	        OPEN O_RC1 FOR
7236	            SELECT CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(120))  AS DIVISION_ID
7237	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(240))  AS SCM_SITE_ID
7238	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(240))  AS PLANT_SITE_ID
7239	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(200))  AS CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE
7240	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(400))  AS CUSTOMER_MODEL
7241	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(40))   AS INCOTERMS
7242	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(40))   AS FCST_TYPE
7243	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(40))   AS DOM_EXP
7244	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(200))  AS MEASURE_GROUP
7245	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(200))  AS MEASURE
7246	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(40))   AS UPLOAD_UOM
7247	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(200))  AS PROJECT_CODE
7248	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(200))  AS ITEM_ID
7249	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(200))  AS CUSTOMER_CODE
7250	                 , (SELECT LISTAGG(V.SBV, ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY V.SBV)
7251	                      FROM (SELECT DISTINCT MF.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE AS SBV
7252	                              FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST MF
7253	                             WHERE MF.VALID_YN = 'Y'
7254	                               AND MF.UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = P_BUCKET_TYPE
7255	                               AND (P_DIVISION_ID IS NULL OR P_DIVISION_ID = 'ALL'
7256	                                    OR INSTR(',' || P_DIVISION_ID || ',', ',' || MF.DIVISION_ID || ',') > 0)
7257	                               AND MF.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE BETWEEN P_START_YYYYMMDD AND P_END_YYYYMMDD) V
7258	                   ) AS UPLOAD_VALID_WEEKS
7259	                 , (SELECT LISTAGG(BK.SBV, ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY BK.SBV)
7260	                      FROM (SELECT DISTINCT MB.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE AS SBV
7261	                              FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST MB
7262	                             WHERE MB.UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = P_BUCKET_TYPE
7263	                               AND MB.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE BETWEEN P_START_YYYYMMDD AND P_END_YYYYMMDD) BK
7264	                   ) AS PERIOD_BUCKETS
7265	              FROM DUAL;
7266	
7267	
7268	        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
7269	            SELECT '0' AS RESULT_CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS RESULT_MSG, '0' AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
7270	
7271	        BEGIN DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_SQL_TEXT); EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
7272	
7273	    EXCEPTION
7274	        WHEN OTHERS THEN
7275	            BEGIN DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_SQL_TEXT); EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
7276	            V_SQL_CODE   := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);
7277	            V_SQL_ERRMSG := SUBSTR(SQLERRM, 1, 4000);
7278	            V_BACKTRACE  := SUBSTR(DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE, 1, 4000);
7279	            BEGIN
7280	                SCM_MGR.PR_DM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(
7281	                    V_PROGRAM_NAME, 'ERROR', N_LOG_COUNT
7282	                  , '########## FAIL END of procedure ##########', V_DESCRIPTION
7283	                  , SYSDATE, P_CREATED_BY, V_PROC_INPUT, V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE);
7284	            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
7285	            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001, '[' || V_PROGRAM_NAME || '] ' || V_SQL_ERRMSG || CHR(10) || V_BACKTRACE);
7286	    END P_DMF0014_GET_EXCEL_FORM_DATA;
7287	
7288	    -- [2026-09-15 UIPKG] standalone PR_DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING 취합
7289	PROCEDURE P_PR_DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING (P_ACCEPT_FLAG VARCHAR2 /* 업무구분(EDI, RPA) */
7290	
7291	                                                    )
7292	/*
7293	    * Program ID  : PR_DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING
7294	    * Description  : N:N Model Mapping 데이터 기초값 생성
7295	    * 테이블
7296	      DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING_M
7297	      DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING_DTL_N
7298	      DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING_DTL_H
7299	    * Program History
7300	    *===================================================================================================
7301	    *   Date       In Charge      Description
7302	    *===================================================================================================
7303	    2024.04.18    kcw3408        N:N Model Mapping_배치작업 신규
7304	    2024.12.19    KANG.H.J       N:N Model Mapping_RPA/EXCEL 로직 삭제
7305	    2026.03.22    김준규           [PJ20264226] NextSCM Pilot 설계 및 개발
7306	    2026.07.06   ZIONEX   [PJ202650304] NextSCM Migration
7307	    */
7308	    /*
7309	    PR_DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING('EDI');    --EDI(PR_DM_DNM_EDI_CALLOFF CALL)
7310	    PR_DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING('RPA');    --RPA/ECEL(PR_DM_PLN_CUST_FCST_TO_MKT_FCST,PR_DM_PLN_CUST_FCST_TO_MKT_FCST_40 CALL)
7311	    PR_DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING('EXCEL');  --RPA/EXCEL 수기입력건
7312	    */
7313	    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
7314	 AS
7315	
7316	    V_PROGRAM_NAME     VARCHAR2(150 CHAR) := $$PLSQL_UNIT;
7317	    V_LOG_TYPE         VARCHAR2(30 CHAR);
7318	    N_LOG_COUNT        INTEGER := 0;
7319	    V_LOG_MESSAGE      VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);
7320	    V_DESCRIPTION      VARCHAR2(500 CHAR);
7321	    D_EVENT_DATE       DATE;
7322	    N_DELETE_COUNT     INTEGER := 0;
7323	    N_INSERT_COUNT     INTEGER := 0;
7324	    V_CREATED_BY       VARCHAR2(150 CHAR) := 'SYS_BATCH_DATA';
7325	    V_0MONTH_TUESDAY   VARCHAR2(08 CHAR);
7326	    V_DELIMITER        VARCHAR2(1 CHAR);
7327	    V_ACCEPT_FLAG      VARCHAR2(10 CHAR);
7328	    V_CNT              INTEGER := 0;
7329	    V_FROM_YYYYMMDD    VARCHAR2(08 CHAR);
7330	    V_DELETE_YYYYMMDD  VARCHAR2(08 CHAR);
7331	    V_VALID_START_DATE VARCHAR2(08 CHAR);
7332	    V_VALID_END_DATE   VARCHAR2(08 CHAR);
7333	    V_IN_PARA_DATA     CLOB;
7334	    V_STEP             VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR) := NULL;
7335	BEGIN
7336	
7337	    IF UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1).COUNT > 1 THEN
7338	        V_PROGRAM_NAME := $$PLSQL_UNIT || '.' || UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1) (2);
7339	    END IF;
7340	
7341	    V_IN_PARA_DATA := JSON_OBJECT('p_accept_flag' VALUE P_ACCEPT_FLAG);
7342	
7343	
7344	    DELETE FROM GT_DCIM_DM_DNM_EDI_CALLOFF_ITE;
7345	    DELETE FROM GT_DCIM_DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING_D;
7346	
7347	
7348	    /* LOG 용 변수 설정 */
7349	    V_LOG_TYPE    := 'START';
7350	    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## START OF PROCEDURE ##########';
7351	    V_DESCRIPTION := 'PR_DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING: ' || P_ACCEPT_FLAG;
7352	    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
7353	    PR_DM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE, V_CREATED_BY);
7354	
7355	    BEGIN
7356	        /* 오윤종 PI 요청으로 셋팅함 */
7357	        V_FROM_YYYYMMDD   := '20240101';
7358	        V_DELETE_YYYYMMDD := TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -12), 'YYYYMMDD');
7359	        /* v_from_yyyymmdd := TO_CHAR(TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, - 12), 'YYYYMMDD') || '1201', 'YYYYMMDD') */
7360	        V_VALID_START_DATE := TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYYMMDD');
7361	        V_VALID_END_DATE   := '20991231';
7362	        V_ACCEPT_FLAG      := P_ACCEPT_FLAG;
7363	    END;
7364	
7365	    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('A1--' || TO_CHAR(SYSTIMESTAMP, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') || P_ACCEPT_FLAG);
7366	
7367	    IF V_ACCEPT_FLAG = 'EDI'
7368	    /*
7369	              대상추출(DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING_M 에 없는건 추출 _EDI
7370	            */
7371	     THEN
7372	        /* 테이블중복제거 */
7373	        /* 임시확인용테이블 중복제거 */
7374	        INSERT
7375	        INTO GT_DCIM_DM_DNM_EDI_CALLOFF_ITE
7376	            SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
7377	                  ,A.SENDER_CODE
7378	                  ,A.SENDER_NAME
7379	                  ,NVL(RTRIM(LTRIM(A.UNLOADING_POINT)), ' ') UNLOADING_POINT
7380	                  ,NVL(RTRIM(LTRIM(A.CUSTOMER_PLANT)), ' ') CUSTOMER_PLANT
7381	                  ,NVL(RTRIM(LTRIM(A.CUST_MATERIAL_CODE)), ' ') CUST_MATERIAL_CODE
7382	                  ,A.SHIPTO_CODE
7383	                  ,A.SOLDTO_CODE
7384	                  ,A.ITEM_ID
7385	                  ,A.SCM_SITE_ID
7386	                  ,A.EDI_INCOTERMS
7387	                  ,A.PROJECT_CODE
7388	                  ,NVL(B.END_CUSTOMER, ' ') END_CUSTOMER
7389	                  ,CASE A.ERP_FCST_TYPE
7390	                       WHEN 'M' THEN
7391	                        'MPS'
7392	                       WHEN 'S' THEN
7393	                        'SMC'
7394	                       ELSE
7395	                        'MPS'
7396	                   END AS FCST_TYPE
7397	                  ,'E' AS DOM_EXP
7398	            FROM   DM_DNM_EDI_CALLOFF_ITEM A
7399	            LEFT   OUTER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE B
7400	            ON     A.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID
7401	            AND    A.SCM_SITE_ID = B.SCM_SITE_ID
7402	            AND    B.SITE_TYPE = 'SALES'
7403	            /* AND B.VALID_YN        = 'Y' */
7404	            WHERE  A.CREATE_DATE >= V_FROM_YYYYMMDD
7405	            AND    A.STATUS_CODE = 'S'
7406	            AND    A.ERP_STATUS IS NULL
7407	            AND    A.SENDER_NAME <> 'LUCID'
7408	            GROUP  BY A.DIVISION_ID
7409	                     ,A.SENDER_CODE
7410	                     ,A.SENDER_NAME
7411	                     ,A.UNLOADING_POINT
7412	                     ,A.CUSTOMER_PLANT
7413	                     ,A.CUST_MATERIAL_CODE
7414	                     ,A.SHIPTO_CODE
7415	                     ,A.SOLDTO_CODE
7416	                     ,A.ITEM_ID
7417	                     ,A.SCM_SITE_ID
7418	                     ,A.STATUS_CODE
7419	                     ,A.SSPM_STATUS
7420	                     ,A.EDI_INCOTERMS
7421	                     ,A.PROJECT_CODE
7422	                     ,B.END_CUSTOMER
7423	                     ,A.SALES_PLANT_SITE_STATUS
7424	                     ,A.ERP_STATUS
7425	                     ,A.ERP_FCST_TYPE;
7426	
7427	        INSERT INTO DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING_M
7428	            (DIVISION_ID
7429	            ,DATA_TYPE_CODE
7430	            ,END_CUSTOMER
7431	            ,SCM_SITE_ID
7432	            ,PROJECT_CODE
7433	            ,FCST_TYPE
7434	            ,INCOTERMS
7435	            ,DOM_EXP
7436	            ,ITEM_ID
7437	            ,ACTIVE_YN
7438	            ,UNLOADING_POINT
7439	            ,CUSTOMER_PLANT
7440	            ,SOLDTO_CODE
7441	            ,SHIPTO_CODE
7442	            ,CUSTOMER_ITEM_ID
7443	            ,CREATED_BY
7444	            ,CREATION_DATE
7445	            ,LAST_UPDATED_BY
7446	            ,LAST_UPDATE_DATE)
7447	            SELECT T1.DIVISION_ID
7448	                  ,T1.DATA_TYPE_CODE
7449	                  ,T1.END_CUSTOMER
7450	                  ,T1.SCM_SITE_ID
7451	                  ,T1.PROJECT_CODE
7452	                  ,T1.FCST_TYPE
7453	                  ,T1.INCOTERMS
7454	                  ,T1.DOM_EXP
7455	                  ,T1.ITEM_ID
7456	                  ,T1.ACTIVE_YN
7457	                  ,T1.UNLOADING_POINT
7458	                  ,T1.CUSTOMER_PLANT
7459	                  ,T1.SOLDTO_CODE
7460	                  ,T1.SHIPTO_CODE
7461	                  ,T1.CUST_MATERIAL_CODE
7462	                  ,'BATCH SYSTEM' AS CREATED_BY
7463	                  ,SYSTIMESTAMP CREATION_DATE
7464	                  ,'BATCH SYSTEM' AS LAST_UPDATED_BY
7465	                  ,SYSTIMESTAMP LAST_UPDATE_DATE
7466	            FROM   (SELECT DISTINCT A.DIVISION_ID
7467	                                   ,'EDI' AS DATA_TYPE_CODE
7468	                                   ,NVL(A.END_CUSTOMER, '-') END_CUSTOMER
7469	                                   ,A.SCM_SITE_ID
7470	                                   ,A.PROJECT_CODE
7471	                                   ,A.FCST_TYPE
7472	                                   ,A.EDI_INCOTERMS INCOTERMS
7473	                                   ,A.DOM_EXP
7474	                                   ,A.ITEM_ID
7475	                                   ,'Y' AS ACTIVE_YN
7476	                                   ,A.UNLOADING_POINT
7477	                                   ,A.CUSTOMER_PLANT
7478	                                   ,A.SOLDTO_CODE
7479	                                   ,A.SHIPTO_CODE
7480	                                   ,A.CUST_MATERIAL_CODE
7481	                                   ,ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.END_CUSTOMER, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.FCST_TYPE, A.EDI_INCOTERMS, A.DOM_EXP, A.ITEM_ID, A.UNLOADING_POINT, A.CUSTOMER_PLANT, A.CUST_MATERIAL_CODE ORDER BY A.UNLOADING_POINT, A.CUSTOMER_PLANT, A.CUST_MATERIAL_CODE) NUM
7482	                    FROM   GT_DCIM_DM_DNM_EDI_CALLOFF_ITE A) T1
7483	            LEFT   OUTER JOIN DM_CUST_ITEM_MAPPING_M T2
7484	            ON     T1.DIVISION_ID = T2.DIVISION_ID
7485	            AND    T1.DATA_TYPE_CODE = T2.DATA_TYPE_CODE
7486	            AND    T1.END_CUSTOMER = T2.END_CUSTOMER
7487	            AND    T1.SCM_SITE_ID = T2.SCM_SITE_ID
7488	            AND    T1.PROJECT_CODE = T2.PROJECT_CODE
7489	            AND    T1.FCST_TYPE = T2.FCST_TYPE
7490	            AND    T1.INCOTERMS = T2.INCOTERMS
7491	            AND    T1.DOM_EXP = T2.DOM_EXP
7492	            AND    T1.ITEM_ID = T2.ITEM_ID
7493	                  /* JSSBAE */
7494	            AND    (RTRIM(LTRIM(T1.UNLOADING_POINT)) = RTRIM(LTRIM(T2.UNLOADING_POINT)) OR
7495	                  (RTRIM(LTRIM(T1.UNLOADING_POINT)) IS NULL AND RTRIM(LTRIM(T2.UNLOADING_POINT)) IS NULL))
7496	            AND    (RTRIM(LTRIM(T1.CUSTOMER_PLANT)) = RTRIM(LTRIM(T2.CUSTOMER_PLANT)) OR
7497	                  (RTRIM(LTRIM(T1.CUSTOMER_PLANT)) IS NULL AND RTRIM(LTRIM(T2.CUSTOMER_PLANT)) IS NULL))
7498	            AND    (RTRIM(LTRIM(T1.CUST_MATERIAL_CODE)) = RTRIM(LTRIM(T2.CUSTOMER_ITEM_ID)) OR
7499	                  (RTRIM(LTRIM(T1.CUST_MATERIAL_CODE)) IS NULL AND RTRIM(LTRIM(T2.CUSTOMER_ITEM_ID)) IS NULL))
7500	            WHERE  T2.DIVISION_ID IS NULL
7501	            AND    T1.NUM = 1;
7502	    END IF;
7503	
7504	    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('A2--' || TO_CHAR(SYSTIMESTAMP, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3'));
7505	    /*

7914	    -- [2026-09-15 UIPKG] standalone PR_DM_PLN_CUST_FCST_TO_MKT_FCST 취합
7915	PROCEDURE P_PR_DM_PLN_CUST_FCST_TO_MKT_FCST 
7916	
7917	/* 데이터 확인이 필요할 경우 '--확인' 을 '--확인' 으로 변경하면 DEBUG 모드이고 */
7918	    /* '--확인' 을 '--확인' 으로 변경하면 일반 모드임 */
7919	    /*
7920	    ● PR_DM_PLN_CUST_FCST_TO_MKT_FCST 로직 구조
7921	    -- --------------------------------------------------------
7922	    LOOP_1 (n_PSI_GROUP_CNT : MEASURE별 INPUT된 ROWS만큼 처리)
7923	    -- --------------------------------------------------------
7924	        -- ------------------------------------------------------
7925	        LOOP_1 (CUR_CAR_RESULT : 커서 로딩처리)
7926	        -- ------------------------------------------------------
7927	            -- --------------------------------------------------------
7928	            LOOP_3 (n_PSI_GROUP_CNT : MEASURE별 INPUT된 ROWS만큼 처리)
7929	            -- --------------------------------------------------------
7930	                - PLAN WEEK 추출
7931	                - MEASURE별 BUCKET_TYPE 추출
7932	                - UPLOAD_UOM 추출
7933	                - CONVERT_QTY_PACK 산출
7934	                - BUCKET_TYPE별 MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION 설정날짜로 SPLIT
7935	                    -- -----------------------------------------------------
7936	                    LOOP_4 (@n_bucketcount : BUCKET수량만큼 ROWS 증가 처리)
7937	                    -- -----------------------------------------------------
7938	                        - DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 테이블에 INSERT
7939	                    -- -----------------------------------------------------
7940	                    LOOP_4_END (@n_bucketcount : BUCKET수량만큼 ROWS 증가 처리)
7941	                    -- -----------------------------------------------------
7942	                - DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI 테이블의 처리데이터 작업중임을 마킹 UPDATE
7943	
7944	            -- --------------------------------------------------------
7945	            LOOP_3_END
7946	            -- --------------------------------------------------------
7947	        -- ------------------------------------------------------
7948	        LOOP_1_END (CLOSE 커서)
7949	        -- ------------------------------------------------------
7950	        - Input 데이터중 실제 적용될 시작과 끝 날짜산출
7951	        - DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI 테이블의 SPLIT 처리완료된 데이터 INSERT
7952	        - DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 테이블에 SPLIT 처리완료된 데이터 INSERT
7953	    -- --------------------------------------------------------
7954	    LOOP_1_END
7955	    -- --------------------------------------------------------
7956	    */
7957	    /*===================================================================================================
7958	     * Program ID   : PR_DM_PLN_CUST_FCST_TO_MKT_FCST
7959	     * Description  : MKT FCST - Car Production Result Mapping
7960	     * Program History
7961	     *===================================================================================================
7962	     *   Date       In Charge      Description
7963	     *===================================================================================================
7964	     * 2018. 01. 11    KHSONG      Initial Release [PJ20170278] 전지본부 자동차 GSCM(DM/SCP) 구축 프로젝트

8424	                                           AND VALID_YN = 'Y' /* 20180409 SongKH Add */
8425	                                       )
8426	                      GROUP BY UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE
8427	                             , SUMMARY_BUCKET_TYPE
8428	                             , PLAN_WEEK;
8429	                    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE(N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE);
8430	                    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE(N_SUMMARY_BUCKET_TYPE);
8431	                END IF;
8432	                IF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'MONTHLY' THEN
8433	                    V_START_WEEK := V_YYYYMMDD;
8434	                ELSE
8435	                    V_START_WEEK := TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(TO_DATE(V_YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'MON') + (-7), 'YYYYMMDD');
8436	                END IF;
8437	                DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE ' ||V_CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE);
8438	                DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('CUSTOMER_MODEL ' ||V_CUSTOMER_MODEL);
8439	                /*---- PROJECT_CODE 정보 추출하여 저장 ----*/
8440	                BEGIN
8441	                    SELECT PROJECT_CODE
8442	                      INTO N_PROJECT_CODE
8443	                      FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL
8444	                     WHERE 1 = 1
8445	                       AND DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
8446	                       AND CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE = V_CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE
8447	                       AND CUSTOMER_MODEL = V_CUSTOMER_MODEL
8448	                       AND SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID;
8449	                    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
8450	                        N_PROJECT_CODE := NULL;
8451	                END;
8452	
8453	                DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE ' ||V_CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE);
8454	                DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('CUSTOMER_MODEL ' ||V_CUSTOMER_MODEL);
8455	
8456	                /* 20180228 추가 (S) */
8457	                IF N_PROJECT_CODE IS NULL THEN
8458	                    BEGIN
8459	                        SELECT PROJECT_CODE
8460	                          INTO N_PROJECT_CODE
8461	                          FROM SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM
8462	                         WHERE 1 = 1
8463	                           AND DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
8464	                           AND PROJECT_CODE = V_PROJECT_CODE
8465	                           AND ITEM_ID = V_ITEM_ID
8466	                           AND SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID;
8467	                    END;
8468	                END IF;
8469	                /* 20180228 추가 (E) */
8470	                DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('PROJECT_CODE ' ||N_PROJECT_CODE);
8471	
8472	                /*---- ITEM_ID 정보 추출하여 저장 ----*/
8473	                BEGIN
8474	                    SELECT ITEM_ID
8475	                      INTO N_ITEM_ID
8476	                      FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL
8477	                     WHERE 1 = 1
8478	                       AND DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
8479	                       AND CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE = V_CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE
8480	                       AND CUSTOMER_MODEL = V_CUSTOMER_MODEL
8481	                       AND SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID;
8482	                    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
8483	                        N_ITEM_ID := NULL;
8484	                END;
8485	
8486	                /* 20180228 추가 (S) */
8487	                IF N_ITEM_ID IS NULL THEN
8488	                    BEGIN
8489	                        SELECT ITEM_ID
8490	                          INTO N_ITEM_ID
8491	                          FROM SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM
8492	                         WHERE 1 = 1
8493	                           AND DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
8494	                           AND PROJECT_CODE = V_PROJECT_CODE
8495	                           AND ITEM_ID = V_ITEM_ID
8496	                           AND SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID;
8497	                    END;
8498	                END IF;
8499	
8500	                /* CODE값이 존재하지 않는경우 처리불가  20180319 SKH MODIFY */
8501	                DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('PROJECT_CODE ' ||N_PROJECT_CODE);
8502	                DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('ITEM_ID ' ||N_ITEM_ID);
8503	                IF N_PROJECT_CODE IS NULL OR N_PROJECT_CODE IS NULL OR N_ITEM_ID IS NULL OR N_ITEM_ID IS NULL THEN
8504	                    V_LOG_TYPE := 'INVALID';
8505	                    V_LOG_MESSAGE := 'Project_code OR Item_ID 가 존재하지 않습니다.';
8506	                    GOTO ERR_RTN;
8507	                END IF;
8508	                /* 20180228 추가 (E) */
8509	
8510	                /* 추출된 PROJECT_CODE, ITEM ID 값으로 대체 */
8511	                BEGIN
8512	                    V_ITEM_ID := N_ITEM_ID;
8513	                    V_PROJECT_CODE := N_PROJECT_CODE;
8514	                END;
8515	                /*---- 마스터의 ITEM_CLASS_NAME 추출 ----*/
8516	                BEGIN
8517	                    SELECT UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(ITEM_CLASS_NAME)))
8518	                      INTO N_ITEM_CLASS_NAME
8519	                      FROM SCM_MST_ITEM_SN
8520	                     WHERE 1 = 1
8521	                       AND ITEM_ID = N_ITEM_ID
8522	                      GROUP BY ITEM_CLASS_NAME;
8523	                END;
8524	
8525	                /*---- UOM 추출 ----*/
8526	                N_UPLOAD_UOM := CASE
8527	                                    WHEN (N_ITEM_CLASS_NAME LIKE '%CAR%') THEN
8528	                                     'CAR'
8529	                                    WHEN (N_ITEM_CLASS_NAME LIKE '%PACK%') THEN
8530	                                     'PACK'
8531	                                    WHEN (N_ITEM_CLASS_NAME LIKE '%MODULE%') THEN
8532	                                     'MODULE'
8533	                                    WHEN (N_ITEM_CLASS_NAME LIKE '%CELL%') THEN
8534	                                     'CELL'
8535	                                END;
8536	
8537	                /* 20180320 제품이 아닌 상품인 경우에는 기본단위로 사용함 SKH ADDED */
8538	                IF N_UPLOAD_UOM IS NULL THEN
8539	                    N_UPLOAD_UOM := 'CELL';
8540	                END IF;
8541	
8542	                /*---- CONVERT_QTY_PACK 정보 추출하여 저장 ----*/
8543	                BEGIN
8544	                    SELECT NVL(CONVERT_QTY_PACK, 1)
8545	                      INTO N_CONVERT_QTY_PACK
8546	                      FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL /* 20180301 수정 */
8547	                     WHERE 1 = 1
8548	                       AND DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
8549	                       AND CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE = V_CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE
8550	                       AND CUSTOMER_MODEL = V_CUSTOMER_MODEL
8551	                       AND SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID;
8552	                    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
8553	                        N_CONVERT_QTY_PACK := NULL;
8554	                END;
8555	                /*---- CONVERT_QTY_MODULE 정보 추출하여 저장 ----*/
8556	                BEGIN
8557	                    SELECT NVL(CONVERT_QTY_MODULE, 1)
8558	                      INTO N_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE
8559	                      FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL /* 20180301 수정 */
8560	                     WHERE 1 = 1
8561	                       AND DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
8562	                       AND CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE = V_CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE
8563	                       AND CUSTOMER_MODEL = V_CUSTOMER_MODEL
8564	                       AND SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID;
8565	                    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
8566	                        N_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE := NULL;
8567	                END;
8568	                /*---- CONVERT_QTY_CELL 정보 추출하여 저장 ----*/
8569	                BEGIN
8570	                    SELECT NVL(CONVERT_QTY_CELL, 1)
8571	                      INTO N_CONVERT_QTY_CELL
8572	                      FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL /* 20180301 수정 */
8573	                     WHERE 1 = 1
8574	                       AND DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
8575	                       AND CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE = V_CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE
8576	                       AND CUSTOMER_MODEL = V_CUSTOMER_MODEL
8577	                       AND SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID
8578	                      GROUP BY CONVERT_QTY_CELL
8579	                             , DIVISION_ID
8580	                             , CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE
8581	                             , CUSTOMER_MODEL
8582	                             , SCM_SITE_ID;
8583	                    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
8584	                        N_CONVERT_QTY_CELL := NULL;
8585	                END;
8586	
8587	                /*---- Convert Qty 구하기 START ----*/
8588	                BEGIN
8589	                    N_CONVERT_QTY := CASE
8590	                                         WHEN (V_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CAR') THEN
8591	                                          CASE
8592	                                              WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CAR ' THEN
8593	                                               V_QTY /* n_CONVERT_QTY_PACK */
8594	                                              WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'PACK' THEN
8595	                                               V_QTY * N_CONVERT_QTY_PACK
8596	                                              WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'MODULE' THEN
8597	                                               V_QTY * N_CONVERT_QTY_PACK * N_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE
8598	                                              WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CELL' THEN
8599	                                               V_QTY * N_CONVERT_QTY_PACK * N_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE * N_CONVERT_QTY_CELL
8600	                                          END
8601	                                         WHEN (V_UPLOAD_UOM = 'PACK') THEN
8602	                                          CASE
8603	                                              WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'PACK' THEN
8604	                                               V_QTY
8605	                                              WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'MODULE' THEN
8606	                                               V_QTY * N_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE /* * n_CONVERT_QTY_PACK * n_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE */
8607	                                              WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CELL' THEN
8608	                                               V_QTY * N_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE * N_CONVERT_QTY_CELL /* * n_CONVERT_QTY_PACK * n_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE * n_CONVERT_QTY_CELL */
8609	                                          END
8610	                                         WHEN (V_UPLOAD_UOM = 'MODULE') THEN
8611	                                          CASE
8612	                                              WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'MODULE' THEN
8613	                                               V_QTY /* * n_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE */
8614	                                              WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CELL' THEN
8615	                                               V_QTY * N_CONVERT_QTY_CELL /* * n_CONVERT_QTY_PACK * n_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE * n_CONVERT_QTY_CELL */
8616	                                          END
8617	                                         WHEN (V_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CELL') THEN
8618	                                          CASE
8619	                                              WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CELL' THEN
8620	                                               V_QTY /* * n_CONVERT_QTY_CELL */
8621	                                          END
8622	                                     END;
8623	
8624	                END;
8625	
8626	                /* DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL에 설정안된 measure 는 초기화해줌 20180301추가 */
8627	                BEGIN
8628	                    IF N_CONVERT_QTY IS NULL OR N_CONVERT_QTY = 0 THEN
8629	                        N_CONVERT_QTY := V_QTY * 1;
8630	                    END IF;
8631	
8632	                    /*---- Convert Qty 구하기 END ----*/
8633	                    /* bucketcount 산출 */
8634	                    BEGIN
8635	                        N_BUCKETINSERT := 1; /* 초기화 */
8636	                        N_BUCKETCOUNT := 0; /* 초기화 */
8637	                        N_CALC_QTY := 0; /* 초기화 */
8638	                        N_ADD_QTY := 0; /* 초기화 */
8639	                    END;
8640	
8641	                    /* 캘린더 임시테이블 생성 */
8642	                    BEGIN
8643	                        INSERT
8644	                        INTO GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI
8645	                            SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD
8646	                                 , WEEK_NO
8647	                              FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
8648	                             WHERE END_FLAG = 'x';
8649	                        DELETE FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI; /* 원본 TRUNCATE→GTT는 DELETE(단일TX 원자성 보존·Oracle TRUNCATE 암묵COMMIT 회피) */
8650	                    END;
8651	
8652	                    /* BUCKET_TYPE별 MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION 설정날짜로 SPLIT (START) */
8653	                    BEGIN
8654	                        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
8655	                        IF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'MONTHLY'
8656	                        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
8657	                         THEN
8658	                            BEGIN
8659	                                INSERT
8660	                                INTO GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI
8661	                                    SELECT B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
8662	                                         , ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) RN
8663	                                      FROM DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION A
8664	                                         , SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M B
8665	                                         , SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C
8666	                                     WHERE A.DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
8667	                                       AND A.SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID
8668	                                       AND A.INCOTERMS = V_INCOTERMS
8669	                                       AND A.YYYYMMDD = B.DAYOFWEEK
8670	                                       AND A.BUCKET_PER = 'Y'
8671	                                       AND A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
8672	                                       AND B.CALENDAR_YYYY = C.CALENDAR_YYYY
8673	                                       AND B.CALENDAR_MONTH = C.CALENDAR_MONTH
8674	                                       AND C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = V_START_WEEK; /* 해당 주차 */
8675	
8676	                                SELECT MAX(RN)
8677	                                  INTO N_BUCKETCOUNT
8678	                                  FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI;
8679	
8680	                                /* -------------------------------------------------- MST_BUCKET 이 설정안된 경우도 처리되도록 20180301 추가 (START) */
8681	                                IF N_BUCKETCOUNT IS NULL OR N_BUCKETCOUNT = 0 THEN
8682	                                    BEGIN
8683	                                        DELETE FROM GT_DPCF_RESULTS_2;
8684	
8685	                                        INSERT
8686	                                        INTO GT_DPCF_RESULTS_2
8687	                                            SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD
8688	                                                 , DAYOFWEEK
8689	                                              FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
8690	                                             WHERE BASIS_YYYYMM = SUBSTR(V_START_WEEK, 1, 6) /* 매월 1일 날짜로 해당월의 모든 날짜 선택 */ /* AND DAYOFWEEK IN ('MON','TUE','WED','THU','FRI') ORDER BY BASIS_YYYYMMDD   -- 20180424 skh modify */
8691	                                               AND DAYOFWEEK IN (SELECT SEGMENT3
8692	                                                                   FROM SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
8693	                                                                  WHERE 1 = 1
8694	                                                                    AND SEGMENT1 = V_DIVISION_ID
8695	                                                                    AND LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DEFAULT_BUCKET_DAY'
8696	                                                                    AND SEGMENT2 = N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE
8697	                                                                ) /* 20180424 skh 조건변경 */
8698	                                              ORDER BY BASIS_YYYYMMDD;
8699	
8700	                                        INSERT
8701	                                        INTO GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI
8702	                                            SELECT B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
8703	                                                 , ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) RN
8704	                                              FROM GT_DPCF_RESULTS_2 A
8705	                                                 , SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M B
8706	                                             WHERE A.DAYOFWEEK = B.DAYOFWEEK
8707	                                               AND A.YYYYMMDD = B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD;
8708	
8709	                                        SELECT MAX(RN)
8710	                                          INTO N_BUCKETCOUNT
8711	                                          FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI;
8712	
8713	                                        N_AUTO_SPLIT_DATA := 'Y';
8714	
8715	                                    END;
8716	                                END IF;
8717	
8718	                                /* -------------------------------------------------- MST_BUCKET 이 설정안된 경우도 처리되도록 20180301 추가 (END) */
8719	                                /* bucketcount 산출 */
8720	
8721	                            END;
8722	                            /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
8723	                        ELSIF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'WEEKLY'
8724	                        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
8725	                         THEN
8726	                            BEGIN
8727	                                INSERT
8728	                                INTO GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI
8729	                                    SELECT B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
8730	                                         , ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) RN
8731	                                      FROM DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION A
8732	                                         , SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M B
8733	                                         , SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C
8734	                                     WHERE A.DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
8735	                                       AND A.SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID
8736	                                       AND A.INCOTERMS = V_INCOTERMS
8737	                                       AND A.YYYYMMDD = B.DAYOFWEEK
8738	                                       AND A.BUCKET_PER = 'Y'
8739	                                       AND A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
8740	                                       AND B.ISO_YYYYWW = C.ISO_YYYYWW
8741	                                       AND C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = V_START_WEEK; /* 해당 주차 */
8742	                                /* bucketcount 산출 */
8743	                                SELECT MAX(RN)
8744	                                  INTO N_BUCKETCOUNT
8745	                                  FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI;
8746	
8747	                                /* -------------------------------------------------- MST_BUCKET 이 설정안된 경우도 처리되도록 20180301 추가 (START) */
8748	                                IF N_BUCKETCOUNT IS NULL OR N_BUCKETCOUNT = 0 THEN
8749	                                    BEGIN
8750	                                        DELETE FROM GT_DPCF_RESULTS_2;
8751	
8752	                                        /* 날짜선택오류로 ISO_WEEK 조건추가 20180402 (SONGKH) */
8753	                                        INSERT
8754	                                        INTO GT_DPCF_RESULTS_2
8755	                                            SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD
8756	                                                 , DAYOFWEEK
8757	                                              FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M Z
8758	                                                 , (SELECT DISTINCT ISO_WEEK
8759	                                                         , ISO_YYYYWW
8760	                                                      FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M W
8761	                                                     WHERE BASIS_START_DATE = V_START_WEEK
8762	                                                   ) K /* 매주 월요일 날짜로 해당주의 날짜 선택 */
8763	                                             WHERE Z.ISO_WEEK = K.ISO_WEEK
8764	                                               AND Z.ISO_YYYYWW = K.ISO_YYYYWW /* AND DAYOFWEEK IN ('MON','TUE','WED','THU','FRI') ORDER BY BASIS_YYYYMMDD   -- 20180424 skh modify */
8765	                                               AND DAYOFWEEK IN (SELECT SEGMENT3
8766	                                                                   FROM SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
8767	                                                                  WHERE 1 = 1
8768	                                                                    AND SEGMENT1 = V_DIVISION_ID
8769	                                                                    AND LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DEFAULT_BUCKET_DAY'
8770	                                                                    AND SEGMENT2 = N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE
8771	                                                                ) /* 20180424 skh 조건변경 */
8772	                                              ORDER BY BASIS_YYYYMMDD;
8773	                                        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE(N_BUCKETCOUNT);
8774	                                        /* PRINT v_DIVISION_ID */
8775	                                        /* PRINT n_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE */
8776	                                        /* 날짜선택오류로 ISO_WEEK 조건추가 20180402 (SONGKH) */
8777	
8778	                                        INSERT
8779	                                        INTO GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI
8780	                                            SELECT B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
8781	                                                 , ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) RN
8782	                                              FROM GT_DPCF_RESULTS_2 A
8783	                                                 , SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M B
8784	                                             WHERE A.DAYOFWEEK = B.DAYOFWEEK
8785	                                               AND A.YYYYMMDD = B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD;
8786	                                        SELECT MAX(RN)
8787	                                          INTO N_BUCKETCOUNT
8788	                                          FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI;
8789	                                        /* 확인 SELECT n_bucketcount AS 'n_bucketcount IMSI' */
8790	
8791	                                        N_AUTO_SPLIT_DATA := 'Y';
8792	
8793	                                    END;
8794	                                END IF;
8795	
8796	                                /* -------------------------------------------------- MST_BUCKET 이 설정안된 경우도 처리되도록 20180301 추가 (END) */
8797	                            END;
8798	
8799	                        ELSIF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'DAILY'
8800	                        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
8801	                         THEN
8802	                            BEGIN
8803	
8804	                                INSERT
8805	                                INTO GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI
8806	                                    (BASIS_YYYYMMDD, RN)
8807	                                VALUES
8808	                                    (V_YYYYMMDD, 1);
8809	                                N_BUCKETCOUNT := 1;
8810	                            END;
8811	                        END IF;
8812	                        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
8813	                    END;
8814	
8815	                    /* BUCKET_TYPE별 MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION 설정날짜로 SPLIT (END) */
8816	
8817	                    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- bucket loop START */
8818	                    WHILE N_BUCKETINSERT <= N_BUCKETCOUNT LOOP
8819	                        /* 변수 초기화 */
8820	                        BEGIN
8821	                            N_BATCH_RESULT := NULL;
8822	                        END;
8823	
8824	                        /* 대상날짜가 해당 MEASURE 의 BUCKET TYPE 과 동일한지 확인하여 같지않으면 INSERT INTO 처리하지 않으려고함 (START) ---------------------------------------------- */
8825	                        BEGIN
8826	                            V_DAY_VALID := NULL; /* 변수초기화 */
8827	                            N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE := NULL; /* 변수초기화 */
8828	
8829	                            SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD
8830	                              INTO V_DAY_VALID
8831	                              FROM (SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD
8832	                                         , RN
8833	                                      FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI
8834	                                      ORDER BY BASIS_YYYYMMDD
8835	                                   )
8836	                             WHERE RN = N_BUCKETINSERT;
8837	                        END;
8838	
8839	                        /* Convert QTY값 산출 */
8840	                        IF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'DAILY' THEN
8841	                            BEGIN
8842	                                N_CALC_QTY := V_QTY; /* DAILY 인경우는 계산필요없이 INPUT값을 그대로 사용함 */
8843	
8844	                                SELECT UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE /* 해당주차의 bucket 추출 */
8845	                                  INTO N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE
8846	                                  FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST
8847	                                 WHERE 1 = 1
8848	                                   AND DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
8849	                                   AND SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID
8850	                                   AND MEASURE_GROUP = V_MEASURE_GROUP
8851	                                   AND MEASURE = V_MEASURE /* AND SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE = v_day_valid -- daily인경우 INPUT된 날짜 */
8852	                                   AND SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE = TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(TO_DATE(V_DAY_VALID, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'MON') + (- 7), 'YYYYMMDD') /* 2024.01.12 Philsu Park 수정. */
8853	                                   AND PLAN_WEEK = N_PLAN_WEEK
8854	                                   AND VALID_YN = 'Y'; /* 20180409 SongKH Add */
8855	
8856	                            EXCEPTION
8857	                                /* ⭐ [2026-08-14] 마스터에 해당 주차 bucket 이 없으면 NULL 로 둔다.
8858	                                   N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE 은 L909 에서 NULL 초기화되고 아래 979행에
8859	                                   `IF N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE IS NULL` 분기가 이미 있다 = NULL 은 설계된 경로.
8860	                                   핸들러가 없어 ORA-01403 이 위로 튀었고, 이 프로시저의
8861	                                   WHEN OTHERS THEN ROLLBACK 이 **호출자(DMF0014 업로드)의 INSERT까지**
8862	                                   되돌려 "저장 완료인데 0건" 사고가 났다. */
8863	                                WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
8864	                                    N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE := NULL;
8865	                                WHEN TOO_MANY_ROWS THEN
8866	                                    /* 마스터가 모호하면 유효 bucket 을 단정하지 않는다(동일 NULL 경로). */
8867	                                    N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE := NULL;
8868	                            END;
8869	                        ELSE
8870	                            BEGIN
8871	                                SELECT UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE /* 해당주차의 bucket 추출 */
8872	                                  INTO N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE
8873	                                  FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST
8874	                                 WHERE 1 = 1
8875	                                   AND DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
8876	                                   AND SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID
8877	                                   AND MEASURE_GROUP = V_MEASURE_GROUP
8878	                                   AND MEASURE = V_MEASURE
8879	                                   AND SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE = TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(TO_DATE(V_DAY_VALID, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'MON') + (- 7), 'YYYYMMDD') /* INPUT된 날짜의 당주 월요일 */
8880	                                   AND PLAN_WEEK = N_PLAN_WEEK
8881	                                   AND VALID_YN = 'Y'; /* 20180409 SongKH Add */
8882	                            EXCEPTION
8883	                                /* ⭐ [2026-08-14] 마스터에 해당 주차 bucket 이 없으면 NULL 로 둔다.
8884	                                   N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE 은 L909 에서 NULL 초기화되고 아래 979행에
8885	                                   `IF N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE IS NULL` 분기가 이미 있다 = NULL 은 설계된 경로.
8886	                                   핸들러가 없어 ORA-01403 이 위로 튀었고, 이 프로시저의
8887	                                   WHEN OTHERS THEN ROLLBACK 이 **호출자(DMF0014 업로드)의 INSERT까지**
8888	                                   되돌려 "저장 완료인데 0건" 사고가 났다. */
8889	                                WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
8890	                                    N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE := NULL;
8891	                                WHEN TOO_MANY_ROWS THEN
8892	                                    /* 마스터가 모호하면 유효 bucket 을 단정하지 않는다(동일 NULL 경로). */
8893	                                    N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE := NULL;
8894	                            END;
8895	                        END IF;
8896	
8897	                        IF N_BUCKETINSERT < N_BUCKETCOUNT /* 마지막데이터가 아닌경우 SPLIT된값을 넣어줌 */
8898	                         THEN
8899	                            N_CALC_QTY := TRUNC(N_CONVERT_QTY / N_BUCKETCOUNT, 0);
8900	                            N_ADD_QTY := N_ADD_QTY + N_CALC_QTY;
8901	
8902	                        ELSE
8903	                            /* 마지막 데이터인경우 나머지값을 몰아주기 */
8904	                            N_CALC_QTY := N_CONVERT_QTY - N_ADD_QTY;
8905	                        END IF;
8906	
8907	                        /* 최신 UPLOAD_UOM 값을 변수에 셋팅 */
8908	                        BEGIN
8909	                            N_UPLOAD_UOM_KEY := N_UPLOAD_UOM;
8910	                        END;
8911	
8912	                        /* BUCKET_TYPE이 다른경우도 input 받은 measure 로 INSERT INTO 생성하도록 변경 (20180305) */
8913	                        BEGIN
8914	                            IF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE <> N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE THEN
8915	                                N_UPLOAD_UOM_KEY := N_UPLOAD_UOM; /* 주차별 BUCKET_TYPE 이 달라도 그대로 사용함 */
8916	                            END IF;
8917	                        END;
8918	
8919	                        /* BUCKET_TYPE이 다른경우도 input 받은 measure 로 INSERT INTO 생성하도록 변경 (20180305) */
8920	                        BEGIN
8921	                            IF N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE IS NULL THEN
8922	                                N_UPLOAD_UOM_KEY := NULL;
8923	                            END IF;
8924	                        END;
8925	
8926	                        /* MERGE대상 : SPLIT_COMPLETED / SPLIT만 하여 확인하는 MEASURE : UPLOAD_COMPLETED */
8927	                        BEGIN
8928	                            IF N_UPLOAD_UOM_KEY IS NULL THEN
8929	                                N_BATCH_RESULT := 'UPLOAD_COMPLETED';
8930	                            ELSE
8931	                                N_BATCH_RESULT := 'SPLIT_COMPLETED';
8932	                            END IF;
8933	                        END;

8936	                        BEGIN
8937	                            IF N_AUTO_SPLIT_DATA = 'Y' THEN
8938	                                N_BATCH_RESULT := 'SPLIT_COMPLETED';
8939	                            END IF;
8940	                        END;

8943	                            /* insert into  DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI */
8944	                            INSERT

8966	                                , MST_UPLOAD_UOM

8968	                                , MST_SUMMARY_BUCKET_TYPE
8969	                                , MST_CONVERT_QTY
8970	                                , PLAN_WEEK

8990	                                     , (SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD FROM(SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY BASIS_YYYYMMDD) RN FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI) T WHERE RN = N_BUCKETINSERT)

9146	            /* ---input 데이터의 시작과 끝 산출 ------------------------------------------------------------- (start) EEEE */
9147	BEGIN
9148	                DELETE FROM GT_DPCF_MIN_MAX_PSI_2; /* ⭐ [2026-08-14] MEASURE별 cursor 작업이므로 초기화
9149	                   — 이 GTT 는 ON COMMIT PRESERVE 라 지우지 않으면 measure 마다 누적되어
9150	                     아래 `WHERE RN = 1` 이 복수 행을 반환한다(ORA-01422). 형제 블록 L1233 과 동일 패턴. */
9151	                INSERT
9152	                INTO GT_DPCF_MIN_MAX_PSI_2
9153	                    (YYYYMMDD, RN)
9154	                    SELECT YYYYMMDD
9155	                         , ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY YYYYMMDD) RN
9156	                      FROM GT_DPCF_T3_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI
9157	                      GROUP BY YYYYMMDD
9158	                      ORDER BY YYYYMMDD;

9160	            END;

9163	                  INTO N_MIN_YYYYMMDD
9164	                  FROM GT_DPCF_MIN_MAX_PSI_2
9165	                 WHERE RN = 1;
9166	
9167	                SELECT YYYYMMDD
9168	                  INTO N_MAX_YYYYMMDD
9169	                  FROM GT_DPCF_MIN_MAX_PSI_2
9170	                 WHERE RN = (SELECT MAX(RN)
9171	                               FROM GT_DPCF_MIN_MAX_PSI_2
9172	                            );
9173	            END;

9182	                SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
9183	                     , A.SCM_SITE_ID
9184	                     , A.MEASURE
9185	                     , A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE START_DAY
9186	                     , CASE
9187	                            WHEN SUMMARY_BUCKET_TYPE = 'WEEKLY' THEN TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(TO_DATE(SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'SUN') + (0), 'YYYYMMDD')

9191	                INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT DIVISION_ID
9192	                                 , SCM_SITE_ID

9195	                           ) RSLT
9196	                  ON (A.DIVISION_ID = RSLT.DIVISION_ID AND A.SCM_SITE_ID = RSLT.SCM_SITE_ID AND A.MEASURE_GROUP = RSLT.MEASURE_GROUP)
9197	                 WHERE 1 = 1
9198	                   AND VALID_YN = 'Y' /* 20240507 KCW3408 수정 */
9199	                   AND A.PLAN_WEEK = (SELECT MAX(PLAN_WEEK)
9200	                                        FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST X
9201	                                       WHERE X.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID
9202	                                         AND X.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID
9203	                                         AND X.MEASURE_GROUP = A.MEASURE_GROUP
9204	                                     )
9205	                   AND A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE >= TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') + (- 7 * 1), 'YYYYMMDD') /* 당주 월요일 이후데이터만 */
9206	                   AND SUMMARY_BUCKET_TYPE IN ('DAILY', 'WEEKLY')
9207	                  UNION

9209	                     , A.SCM_SITE_ID
9210	                     , A.MEASURE
9211	                     , CASE
9212	                            WHEN SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE = TO_CHAR(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), 'YYYYMMDD') THEN TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') + (- 7 * 1), 'YYYYMMDD')
9213	                            ELSE SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE
9214	                        END AS START_DAY
9215	                     , TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(TO_DATE(SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE, 'YYYYMMDD'), 1) - 1, 'YYYYMMDD') END_DAY
9216	                  FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST A
9217	                INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT DIVISION_ID
9218	                                 , SCM_SITE_ID
9219	                                 , MEASURE_GROUP
9220	                              FROM GT_DPCF_T3_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI
9221	                           ) RSLT

9223	                 WHERE 1 = 1
9224	                   AND VALID_YN = 'Y'
9225	                   AND A.PLAN_WEEK = (SELECT MAX(PLAN_WEEK)
9226	                                        FROM DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST X

9228	                                         AND X.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID
9229	                                         AND X.MEASURE_GROUP = A.MEASURE_GROUP
9230	                                     )

9242	
9243	            BEGIN
9244	                IF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'DAILY' THEN
9245	                    SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD
9246	                      INTO N_MAX_YYYYMMDD
9247	                      FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M T
9248	                     WHERE T.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(N_MAX_YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD') + 0, 'YYYYMMDD'); /* MAX_YYYYMMDD 셋팅 */
9249	                ELSIF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'WEEKLY' THEN
9250	                    N_MIN_YYYYMMDD := TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(TO_DATE(N_MIN_YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'MON') + (-7), 'YYYYMMDD'); /* MIN_YYYYMMDD 셋팅 */
9251	                    SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD
9252	                      INTO N_MAX_YYYYMMDD
9253	                      FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M T
9254	                     WHERE T.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(N_MAX_YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD') + 6, 'YYYYMMDD'); /* MAX_YYYYMMDD 셋팅 */
9255	                ELSIF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'MONTHLY' THEN
9256	                    SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD
9257	                      INTO N_MAX_YYYYMMDD
9258	                      FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M T
9259	                     WHERE T.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(TO_DATE(N_MAX_YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD'), 1) - 1, 'YYYYMMDD'); /* MAX_YYYYMMDD 셋팅 */
9260	                END IF;
9261	            END;
