9482	
9483	            /*-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
9484	            /* [E20260818-MKTDEL] ⭐ 삭제 범위 = 삽입 범위 (PK 충돌 근본 제거)
9485	               위 선삭제는 YYYYMMDD 를 N_MIN~N_MAX 구간으로만 지운다. 그런데 아래 INSERT 대상은
9486	               `MAP.START_DAY~END_DAY` ∩ `>= 이번주 월요일` 이라 **범위가 다르다** —
9487	               삭제되지 않은 기존 행과 새 행이 PK(10키)에서 충돌해 ORA-00001 이 났다
9488	               (2026-08-18 실사고: DMF0014 업로드가 '저장 완료'인데 원장 0건).
9489	               ⇒ INSERT 가 적재할 키 집합 그대로 한 번 더 정밀 삭제한다. */
9490	            BEGIN
9491	                DELETE FROM DM_PLN_MKT_FCST T
9492	                WHERE  EXISTS (SELECT 1
9493	                        FROM   GT_DPCF_T2_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI A
9494	                        INNER JOIN (SELECT DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, MEASURE, START_DAY, END_DAY
9495	                                      FROM GT_DPCF_TMP_SITE_CUSTOMER_MAP_2
9496	                                     GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, MEASURE, START_DAY, END_DAY) MAP
9497	                          ON (A.DIVISION_ID = MAP.DIVISION_ID AND A.SCM_SITE_ID = MAP.SCM_SITE_ID
9498	                              AND A.MEASURE = MAP.MEASURE)
9499	                        WHERE  A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN MAP.START_DAY AND MAP.END_DAY
9500	                        AND    A.YYYYMMDD >= TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') + (- 7), 'YYYYMMDD')
9501	                        AND    T.DIVISION_ID   = A.DIVISION_ID
9502	                        AND    T.PROJECT_CODE  = A.PROJECT_CODE
9503	                        AND    T.SCM_SITE_ID   = A.SCM_SITE_ID
9504	                        AND    T.ITEM_ID       = A.ITEM_ID
9505	                        AND    T.DOM_EXP       = A.DOM_EXP
9506	                        AND    T.FCST_TYPE     = A.FCST_TYPE
9507	                        AND    T.INCOTERMS     = A.INCOTERMS
9508	                        AND    T.MEASURE       = A.MEASURE
9509	                        AND    T.YYYYMMDD      = A.YYYYMMDD
9510	                        AND    T.PLANT_SITE_ID = A.PLANT_SITE_ID);
9511	            END;
9512	
9513	            BEGIN
9514	/* [E20260818-MKTMERGE] ⭐ INSERT → MERGE 전환 (사용자 승인 2026-08-18)
9515	                   동일 파일 2회차 저장이 1회차 적재분과 PK(10키) 충돌해 ORA-00001 로 실패했다.
9516	                   선삭제만으로는 완전히 막히지 않아(재현 확보), 적재 자체를 MERGE 로 바꿔
9517	                   **있으면 UPDATE / 없으면 INSERT** 로 만든다 = 재업로드가 덮어쓰기가 되어 멱등해진다.
9518	                   ⚠ 소스에 PK 중복이 생기면 ORA-30926 으로 드러난다(현 실측 중복 0건). */
9519	                MERGE INTO DM_PLN_MKT_FCST T
9520	                /* [E20260818-MKTDISTINCT] ⭐ DISTINCT 무력화 정정 — 팬아웃 복제 제거
9521	                   MAP 조인이 같은 행을 2건으로 부풀리는데, 종전에는 선택목록의 SYSTIMESTAMP 때문에
9522	                   DISTINCT 가 접지 못해 동일 PK 2건이 INSERT 되어 ORA-00001 이 났다(실측 확인).
9523	                   ⇒ DISTINCT 는 **결정적 컬럼에만** 적용하고 SYSTIMESTAMP·상수는 바깥에서 부여한다. */
9524	                USING (SELECT X.DIVISION_ID, X.PROJECT_CODE, X.SCM_SITE_ID, X.ITEM_ID, X.DOM_EXP
9525	                            , X.FCST_TYPE, X.INCOTERMS, X.MEASURE, X.PLANT_SITE_ID, X.YYYYMMDD
9526	                            , X.QTY, X.CREATED_BY, X.CREATION_DATE, X.LAST_UPDATED_BY
9527	                            , SYSTIMESTAMP              AS LAST_UPDATE_DATE
9528	                            , 'UPLOAD_COMPLETED'        AS ATTRIBUTE1
9529	                            , X.MST_UPLOAD_UOM          AS ATTRIBUTE2
9530	                            , X.MST_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE  AS ATTRIBUTE3
9531	                            , X.MST_SUMMARY_BUCKET_TYPE AS ATTRIBUTE4
9532	                            , X.MST_CONVERT_QTY         AS ATTRIBUTE5
9533	                            , X.PLAN_WEEK               AS ATTRIBUTE6
9534	                            , X.SPLIT_RESULT            AS ATTRIBUTE7
9535	                            , X.ATTRIBUTE8, X.ATTRIBUTE9, X.ATTRIBUTE10
9536	                          FROM (SELECT * FROM (
9537	                                   /* [E20260818-MKTPKGUARD] ⭐ PK 1행 확정
9538	                                      계측 실측: 중복 행이 갈라지는 컬럼은 CREATION_DATE 하나뿐(나머지 전부 동일).
9539	                                      이전 업로드분 PSI 행과 이번 분이 같은 MKT 10키로 겹치는데 DISTINCT 가
9540	                                      CREATION_DATE 차이 때문에 못 접어 ORA-00001 이 났다.
9541	                                      ⇒ 최신(CREATION_DATE DESC) 1건만 채택한다. */
9542	                                   SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
9543	                                     , A.PROJECT_CODE
9544	                                     , A.SCM_SITE_ID
9545	                                     , A.ITEM_ID
9546	                                     , A.DOM_EXP
9547	                                     , A.FCST_TYPE
9548	                                     , A.INCOTERMS
9549	                                     , A.MEASURE
9550	                                     , A.PLANT_SITE_ID
9551	                                     , A.YYYYMMDD
9552	                                     , A.QTY
9553	                                     , A.CREATED_BY
9554	                                     , A.CREATION_DATE
9555	                                     , A.LAST_UPDATED_BY
9556	                                     , A.MST_UPLOAD_UOM
9557	                                     , A.MST_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE
9558	                                     , A.MST_SUMMARY_BUCKET_TYPE
9559	                                     , A.MST_CONVERT_QTY
9560	                                     , A.PLAN_WEEK
9561	                                     , A.SPLIT_RESULT
9562	                                     , A.ATTRIBUTE8
9563	                                     , A.ATTRIBUTE9
9564	                                     , A.ATTRIBUTE10
9565	                                        , ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE, A.YYYYMMDD, A.PLANT_SITE_ID
9566	                                              ORDER BY A.CREATION_DATE DESC NULLS LAST
9567	                                                     , A.QTY DESC NULLS LAST) AS RN_PKG
9568	                                     FROM GT_DPCF_T2_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI A
9569	                    INNER JOIN (SELECT DIVISION_ID
9570	                                     , SCM_SITE_ID
9571	                                     , MEASURE
9572	                                     , START_DAY
9573	                                     , END_DAY
9574	                                  FROM GT_DPCF_TMP_SITE_CUSTOMER_MAP_2
9575	                                  GROUP BY DIVISION_ID
9576	                                         , SCM_SITE_ID
9577	                                         , MEASURE
9578	                                         , START_DAY
9579	                                         , END_DAY
9580	                               ) MAP
9581	                      ON (A.DIVISION_ID = MAP.DIVISION_ID AND A.SCM_SITE_ID = MAP.SCM_SITE_ID AND A.MEASURE = MAP.MEASURE)
9582	                     WHERE 1 = 1
9583	                       AND A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN MAP.START_DAY
9584	                       AND MAP.END_DAY
9585	                       AND A.YYYYMMDD >= TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') + (- 7), 'YYYYMMDD')
9586	                                 ) Y
9587	                                WHERE Y.RN_PKG = 1) X
9588	                      ) S
9589	                ON (    T.DIVISION_ID    = S.DIVISION_ID
9590	                    AND T.PROJECT_CODE   = S.PROJECT_CODE
9591	                    AND T.SCM_SITE_ID    = S.SCM_SITE_ID
9592	                    AND T.ITEM_ID        = S.ITEM_ID
9593	                    AND T.DOM_EXP        = S.DOM_EXP
9594	                    AND T.FCST_TYPE      = S.FCST_TYPE
9595	                    AND T.INCOTERMS      = S.INCOTERMS
9596	                    AND T.MEASURE        = S.MEASURE
9597	                    AND T.YYYYMMDD       = S.YYYYMMDD

9854	
9855	            */
9856	            BEGIN
9857	                SELECT N_FCST_INSERT_COUNT + COUNT(*)
9858	                  INTO N_FCST_INSERT_COUNT
9859	                  FROM GT_DPCF_T2_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI
9860	                 WHERE 1 = 1
9861	                   AND BATCH_RESULT = 'SPLIT_COMPLETED';
9862	            END;
9863	
9864	            /* GROUP 로딩 COUNT 증가 */
9865	            BEGIN
9866	                N_PSI_GROUP_INSERT := N_PSI_GROUP_INSERT + 1;
9867	            END;
9868	
9869	            /* GROUP별로 작업종료후 Temp Table TRUNCATE */
9870	            IF N_PSI_GROUP_INSERT <= N_PSI_GROUP_CNT THEN
9871	                /* GROUP별로 작업종료후 Temp Table TRUNCATE */
9872	                DELETE FROM GT_DPCF_T2_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI; /* 원본 TRUNCATE→GTT는 DELETE(단일TX 원자성 보존·Oracle TRUNCATE 암묵COMMIT 회피) */
9873	                DELETE FROM GT_DPCF_T3_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI; /* 원본 TRUNCATE→GTT는 DELETE(단일TX 원자성 보존·Oracle TRUNCATE 암묵COMMIT 회피) */
9874	            END IF;
9875	
9876	        END LOOP;
9877	
9878	        /* [E20241126-000426]_Cust.FCST(P+P) Measure 로직 수정 START */
9879	        /* 프로시져를 호출하여, CUST_FCST 메져 데이터를 생성한다 */
9880	
9881	        SELECT NVL(MAX(T.SEQ), -1)   /*[E20260929-DMF0014CFSEQ] 대상 0건 = 루프 0회 명시*/
9882	          INTO V_CNT
9883	          FROM GT_DPCF_CUST_FCST_DIVISION T;
9884	        V_I := 0;
9885	        V_CUST_FCST_DIVISION := NULL;
9886	
9887	        /* 할당단위별 루프 */
9888	        WHILE V_I <= V_CNT LOOP
9889	            SELECT DIVISION_ID
9890	              INTO V_CUST_FCST_DIVISION
9891	              FROM GT_DPCF_CUST_FCST_DIVISION T
9892	             WHERE T.SEQ = V_I;
9893	
9894	            PR_DM_PLN_MKT_FCST_MAKE_CUST_FCST(V_CUST_FCST_DIVISION);
9895	            V_I := V_I + 1;
9896	        END LOOP;
9897	        /* [E20241126-000426]_Cust.FCST(P+P) Measure 로직 수정 END */
9898	        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- PSI GROUP LOOP END */
9899	        /* ############################################################################################################# */
9900	        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- PSI GROUP LOOP END */
9901	
9902	        /* [원자성] MSSQL 원본의 COMMIT 은 상위 BEGIN TRAN 내부 실행이라 @@TRANCOUNT 감소일 뿐 실커밋이 아니었다. */
9903	        /* END */
9904	
9905	        /* ----------------------------------------------------------- */

9908	        /* IF v_log_message IS NULL */
9909	        /* SELECT 'success' ; */
9910	
9911	        /* 종료 LOG */
9912	        V_LOG_TYPE := 'END';
9913	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';
9914	        V_DESCRIPTION := 'psi_update_count= ' ||TO_CHAR(N_PSI_UPDATE_COUNT)||' / psi_delete_count= ' ||TO_CHAR(N_PSI_DELETE_COUNT)||
9915	                         ' / psi_insert_count= ' ||TO_CHAR(N_PSI_INSERT_COUNT)||' / fcst_delete_count= ' ||TO_CHAR(N_FCST_DELETE_COUNT)||

9917	        /* || ' */
9918	         ;

9922	
9923	        << ERR_RTN >>
9924	        NULL;
9925	        IF V_LOG_TYPE = 'INVALID' THEN
9926	            ROLLBACK;
9927	            PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
9928	        END IF;
9929	
9930	    END;
9931	    /* ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
9932	
9933	EXCEPTION
9934	    /* ----------------------------------------------------------- */
9935	    /* 트랜잭션 (END) */
9936	    /* ERROR LOG VALUE SAVE */
9937	    WHEN OTHERS THEN
9938	        ROLLBACK;
9939	        PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME
9940	                            , 'ERROR'
9941	                            , 0
9942	                            , SQLERRM
9943	                            , NULL
9944	                            , SYSDATE
9945	                            , P_IN_PARA_DATA  => V_IN_PARA_DATA
9946	                            , P_SQL_ERR_CODE  => SQLCODE
9947	                            , P_SQL_ERR_MSG   => SQLERRM
9948	                            , P_SQL_BACKTRACE => DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE);
9949	        /* [E20260818-RAISE] ⭐ 예외를 삼키지 않는다.
9950	           종전에는 ROLLBACK 후 정상 종료해, 호출자(DMF0014 Excel Upload)의 INSERT 까지
9951	           되돌아간 사실이 상위로 전달되지 않았다 = "저장 완료인데 원장 0건"(허위 성공).
9952	           로그를 남긴 뒤 그대로 전파해 호출자가 실패로 끝나게 한다. */
9953	        RAISE;
9954

9956	END P_PR_DM_PLN_CUST_FCST_TO_MKT_FCST;
9957	
9958	    -- [2026-09-15 UIPKG] standalone PR_DM_PLN_CUST_FCST_TO_MKT_FCST_40 취합
9959	PROCEDURE P_PR_DM_PLN_CUST_FCST_TO_MKT_FCST_40 (P_DIVISION_ID IN VARCHAR2
9960	    /* p_active_flag            VARCHAR2    -- 필요없음 */
9961	    )
9962	
9963	/* 데이터 확인이 필요할 경우 '--확인' 을 '--확인' 으로 변경하면 DEBUG 모드이고 */
9964	    /* '--확인' 을 '--확인' 으로 변경하면 일반 모드임 */
9965	/*
9966	    ● PR_DM_PLN_CUST_FCST_TO_MKT_FCST_40 로직 구조
9967	    -- --------------------------------------------------------
9968	    LOOP_1 (n_PSI_GROUP_CNT : MEASURE별 INPUT된 ROWS만큼 처리)
9969	    -- --------------------------------------------------------
9970	        -- ------------------------------------------------------
9971	        LOOP_1 (CUR_CAR_RESULT : 커서 로딩처리)
9972	        -- ------------------------------------------------------
9973	            -- --------------------------------------------------------
9974	            LOOP_3 (n_PSI_GROUP_CNT : MEASURE별 INPUT된 ROWS만큼 처리)
9975	            -- --------------------------------------------------------
9976	                - PLAN WEEK 추출
9977	                - MEASURE별 BUCKET_TYPE 추출
9978	                - UPLOAD_UOM 추출
9979	                - CONVERT_QTY_PACK 산출
9980	                - BUCKET_TYPE별 MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION 설정날짜로 SPLIT
9981	                    -- -----------------------------------------------------
9982	                    LOOP_4 (@n_bucketcount : BUCKET수량만큼 ROWS 증가 처리)
9983	                    -- -----------------------------------------------------
9984	                        - DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 테이블에 INSERT
9985	                    -- -----------------------------------------------------
9986	                    LOOP_4_END (@n_bucketcount : BUCKET수량만큼 ROWS 증가 처리)
9987	                    -- -----------------------------------------------------
9988	                - DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI 테이블의 처리데이터 작업중임을 마킹 UPDATE
9989	
9990	            -- --------------------------------------------------------
9991	            LOOP_3_END
9992	            -- --------------------------------------------------------
9993	        -- ------------------------------------------------------
9994	        LOOP_1_END (CLOSE 커서)
9995	        -- ------------------------------------------------------
9996	        - Input 데이터중 실제 적용될 시작과 끝 날짜산출

9999	    -- --------------------------------------------------------
10000	    LOOP_1_END
10001	    -- --------------------------------------------------------
10002	    */

10432	                    /* v_ITEM_ID := n_ITEM_ID */
10433	                    /* v_PROJECT_CODE := n_PROJECT_CODE */
10434	                    /* END */

10436	                    N_UPLOAD_UOM := CASE
10437	                                        WHEN (N_ITEM_CLASS_NAME LIKE '%CAR%') THEN
10438	                                         'CAR'
10439	                                        WHEN (N_ITEM_CLASS_NAME LIKE '%PACK%') THEN
10440	                                         'PACK'
10441	                                        WHEN (N_ITEM_CLASS_NAME LIKE '%MODULE%') THEN
10442	                                         'MODULE'
10443	                                        WHEN (N_ITEM_CLASS_NAME LIKE '%CELL%') THEN
10444	                                         'CELL'
10445	                                    END;
10446	
10447	                    /* 20180320 제품이 아닌 상품인 경우에는 기본단위로 사용함 SKH ADDED */
10448	                    IF N_UPLOAD_UOM IS NULL THEN
10449	                        N_UPLOAD_UOM := 'CELL';
10450	                    END IF;
10451	
10452	                    /*---- CONVERT_QTY_PACK 정보 추출하여 저장 ----*/
10453	                    BEGIN
10454	                        SELECT NVL(MAX(CONVERT_QTY_PACK), 1)
10455	                        INTO   N_CONVERT_QTY_PACK
10456	                        FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL  /* 20180301 수정 */
10457	                        WHERE  1 = 1
10458	                        AND    DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
10459	                              /* AND CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE = v_CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE */
10460	                        AND    CUSTOMER_MODEL = V_CUSTOMER_MODEL
10461	                        AND    SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID;
10462	                    END;
10463	                    /*---- CONVERT_QTY_MODULE 정보 추출하여 저장 ----*/
10464	                    BEGIN
10465	                        SELECT NVL(MAX(CONVERT_QTY_MODULE), 1)
10466	                        INTO   N_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE
10467	                        FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL  /* 20180301 수정 */
10468	                        WHERE  1 = 1
10469	                        AND    DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
10470	                        AND    CUSTOMER_MODEL = V_CUSTOMER_MODEL
10471	                        AND    SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID;
10472	                    END;
10473	                    /*---- CONVERT_QTY_CELL 정보 추출하여 저장 ----*/
10474	                    BEGIN
10475	                        SELECT NVL(MAX(CONVERT_QTY_CELL), 1)
10476	                        INTO   N_CONVERT_QTY_CELL
10477	                        FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_MODEL  /* 20180301 수정 */
10478	                        WHERE  1 = 1
10479	                        AND    DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
10480	                        AND    CUSTOMER_MODEL = V_CUSTOMER_MODEL
10481	                        AND    SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID;
10482	                    END;
10483	
10484	                    /*---- Convert Qty 구하기 START ----*/
10485	                    BEGIN
10486	                        N_CONVERT_QTY := CASE
10487	                                             WHEN (V_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CAR') THEN
10488	                                              CASE
10489	                                                  WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CAR ' THEN
10490	                                                   V_QTY  /* n_CONVERT_QTY_PACK */
10491	                                                  WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'PACK' THEN
10492	                                                   V_QTY * N_CONVERT_QTY_PACK
10493	                                                  WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'MODULE' THEN
10494	                                                   V_QTY * N_CONVERT_QTY_PACK * N_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE
10495	                                                  WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CELL' THEN
10496	                                                   V_QTY * N_CONVERT_QTY_PACK * N_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE * N_CONVERT_QTY_CELL
10497	                                              END
10498	                                             WHEN (V_UPLOAD_UOM = 'PACK') THEN
10499	                                              CASE
10500	                                                  WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'PACK' THEN
10501	                                                   V_QTY
10502	                                                  WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'MODULE' THEN
10503	                                                   V_QTY * N_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE  /* * n_CONVERT_QTY_PACK * n_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE */
10504	                                                  WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CELL' THEN
10505	                                                   V_QTY * N_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE * N_CONVERT_QTY_CELL  /* * n_CONVERT_QTY_PACK * n_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE * n_CONVERT_QTY_CELL */
10506	                                              END
10507	                                             WHEN (V_UPLOAD_UOM = 'MODULE') THEN
10508	                                              CASE
10509	                                                  WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'MODULE' THEN
10510	                                                   V_QTY  /* * n_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE */
10511	                                                  WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CELL' THEN
10512	                                                   V_QTY * N_CONVERT_QTY_CELL  /* * n_CONVERT_QTY_PACK * n_CONVERT_QTY_MODULE * n_CONVERT_QTY_CELL */
10513	                                              END
10514	                                             WHEN (V_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CELL') THEN
10515	                                              CASE
10516	                                                  WHEN N_UPLOAD_UOM = 'CELL' THEN
10517	                                                   V_QTY  /* * n_CONVERT_QTY_CELL */
10518	                                              END
10519	                                         END;
10520	
10521	                        IF N_CONVERT_QTY IS NULL OR N_CONVERT_QTY = 0 THEN
10522	                            N_CONVERT_QTY := V_QTY * 1;
10523	                        END IF;
10524	                        /*---- Convert Qty 구하기 END ----*/
10525	                        N_BUCKETINSERT := 1;  /* 초기화 */
10526	                        N_BUCKETCOUNT  := 0;  /* 초기화 */
10527	                        N_CALC_QTY     := 0;  /* 초기화 */
10528	                        N_ADD_QTY      := 0;  /* 초기화 */
10529	
10530	                        /* 캘린더 임시테이블 생성 */
10531	                        DELETE FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOM; /* 원본 TRUNCATE→GTT는 DELETE(단일TX 원자성 보존·Oracle TRUNCATE 암묵COMMIT 회피) */
10532	
10533	                        /* BUCKET_TYPE별 MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION 설정날짜로 SPLIT (START) */
10534	                        /* PRINT('3.BUCKET_TYPE별 MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION 설정날짜로 SPLIT (START)') */
10535	                        BEGIN
10536	                            /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
10537	                            IF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'MONTHLY' THEN
10538	                                INSERT
10539	                                INTO GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOM
10540	                                    (BASIS_YYYYMMDD, RN)
10541	                                    SELECT B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD, ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) RN
10542	                                    FROM   DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION A, SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M B, SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C
10543	                                    WHERE  A.DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
10544	                                    AND    A.SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID
10545	                                    AND    A.INCOTERMS = V_INCOTERMS
10546	                                    AND    A.YYYYMMDD = B.DAYOFWEEK
10547	                                    AND    A.BUCKET_PER = 'Y'
10548	                                    AND    A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
10549	                                    AND    B.CALENDAR_YYYY = C.CALENDAR_YYYY
10550	                                    AND    B.CALENDAR_MONTH = C.CALENDAR_MONTH
10551	                                    AND    C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = V_START_WEEK;  /* 해당 주차 */
10552	                                SELECT MAX(RN) INTO N_BUCKETCOUNT FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOM;
10553	
10554	                                /* -------------------------------------------------- MST_BUCKET 이 설정안된 경우도 처리되도록 20180301 추가 (START) */
10555	                                IF N_BUCKETCOUNT IS NULL OR N_BUCKETCOUNT = 0 THEN
10556	                                    DELETE FROM GT_DPCF_RESULTS;
10557	
10558	                                    INSERT
10559	                                    INTO GT_DPCF_RESULTS
10560	                                        (YYYYMMDD, DAYOFWEEK)
10561	                                        SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD, DAYOFWEEK
10562	                                        FROM   SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
10563	                                        WHERE  BASIS_YYYYMM = SUBSTR(V_START_WEEK, 1, 6)  /* 매월 1일 날짜로 해당월의 모든 날짜 선택 */
10564	                                              /* AND DAYOFWEEK IN ('MON','TUE','WED','THU','FRI') ORDER BY BASIS_YYYYMMDD   -- 20180424 skh modify */
10565	                                        AND    DAYOFWEEK IN (SELECT SEGMENT3
10566	                                                             FROM   SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
10567	                                                             WHERE  1 = 1
10568	                                                             AND    SEGMENT1 = V_DIVISION_ID
10569	                                                             AND    LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DEFAULT_BUCKET_DAY'
10570	                                                             AND    SEGMENT2 = N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE)  /* 20180424 skh 조건변경 */
10571	                                        ORDER  BY BASIS_YYYYMMDD;
10572	
10573	                                    INSERT
10574	                                    INTO GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOM
10575	                                        (BASIS_YYYYMMDD, RN)
10576	                                        SELECT B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD, ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) RN
10577	                                        FROM   GT_DPCF_RESULTS A, SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M B
10578	                                        WHERE  A.DAYOFWEEK = B.DAYOFWEEK
10579	                                        AND    A.YYYYMMDD = B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD;
10580	                                    SELECT MAX(RN) INTO N_BUCKETCOUNT FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOM;
10581	
10582	                                    N_AUTO_SPLIT_DATA := 'Y';
10583	
10584	                                END IF;
10585	
10586	                                /* -------------------------------------------------- MST_BUCKET 이 설정안된 경우도 처리되도록 20180301 추가 (END) */
10587	                                /* bucketcount 산출 */
10588	                                /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
10589	                            ELSIF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'WEEKLY' THEN
10590	                                /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
10591	                                BEGIN
10592	                                    INSERT
10593	                                    INTO GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOM
10594	                                        (BASIS_YYYYMMDD, RN)
10595	                                        SELECT B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD, ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) RN
10596	                                        FROM   DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION A, SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M B, SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C
10597	                                        WHERE  A.DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
10598	                                        AND    A.SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID
10599	                                        AND    A.INCOTERMS = V_INCOTERMS
10600	                                        AND    A.YYYYMMDD = B.DAYOFWEEK
10601	                                        AND    A.BUCKET_PER = 'Y'
10602	                                        AND    A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
10603	                                        AND    B.ISO_YYYYWW = C.ISO_YYYYWW
10604	                                        AND    C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = V_START_WEEK;  /* 해당 주차 */
10605	                                    /* bucketcount 산출 */
10606	                                    SELECT MAX(RN) INTO N_BUCKETCOUNT FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOM;
10607	
10608	                                    /* -------------------------------------------------- MST_BUCKET 이 설정안된 경우도 처리되도록 20180301 추가 (START) */
10609	                                    IF N_BUCKETCOUNT IS NULL OR N_BUCKETCOUNT = 0 THEN
10610	                                        DELETE FROM GT_DPCF_RESULTS;
10611	
10612	                                        /* 날짜선택오류로 ISO_WEEK 조건추가 20180402 (SONGKH) */
10613	                                        INSERT
10614	                                        INTO GT_DPCF_RESULTS
10615	                                            (YYYYMMDD, DAYOFWEEK)
10616	                                            SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD, DAYOFWEEK
10617	                                            FROM   SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M Z
10618	                                                  ,(SELECT DISTINCT ISO_WEEK, ISO_YYYYWW FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M W WHERE BASIS_START_DATE = V_START_WEEK) K  /* 매주 월요일 날짜로 해당주의 날짜 선택 */
10619	                                            WHERE  Z.ISO_WEEK = K.ISO_WEEK
10620	                                            AND    Z.ISO_YYYYWW = K.ISO_YYYYWW
10621	                                                  /* AND DAYOFWEEK IN ('MON','TUE','WED','THU','FRI') ORDER BY BASIS_YYYYMMDD   -- 20180424 skh modify */
10622	                                            AND    DAYOFWEEK IN (SELECT SEGMENT3
10623	                                                                 FROM   SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
10624	                                                                 WHERE  1 = 1
10625	                                                                 AND    SEGMENT1 = V_DIVISION_ID
10626	                                                                 AND    LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DEFAULT_BUCKET_DAY'
10627	                                                                 AND    SEGMENT2 = N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE)  /* 20180424 skh 조건변경 */
10628	                                            ORDER  BY BASIS_YYYYMMDD;
10629	
10630	                                        /* 날짜선택오류로 ISO_WEEK 조건추가 20180402 (SONGKH) */
10631	                                        INSERT
10632	                                        INTO GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOM
10633	                                            (BASIS_YYYYMMDD, RN)
10634	                                            SELECT B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD, ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) RN
10635	                                            FROM   GT_DPCF_RESULTS A, SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M B
10636	                                            WHERE  A.DAYOFWEEK = B.DAYOFWEEK
10637	                                            AND    A.YYYYMMDD = B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD;
10638	                                        SELECT MAX(RN) INTO N_BUCKETCOUNT FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOM;
10639	                                        /* 확인 SELECT n_bucketcount AS 'n_bucketcount IMSI' */
10640	                                        N_AUTO_SPLIT_DATA := 'Y';
10641	
10642	                                    END IF;
10643	
10644	                                    /* -------------------------------------------------- MST_BUCKET 이 설정안된 경우도 처리되도록 20180301 추가 (END) */
10645	                                END;
10646	
10647	                            ELSE
10648	                                /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
10649	                                IF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'DAILY' THEN
10650	
10651	                                    INSERT
10652	                                    INTO GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOM
10653	                                        (BASIS_YYYYMMDD, RN)
10654	                                    VALUES
10655	                                        (V_YYYYMMDD, 1);
10656	                                    N_BUCKETCOUNT := 1;
10657	                                END IF;
10658	                                /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
10659	                            END IF;
10660	                        END;
10661	                    END;
10662	
10663	                    /* BUCKET_TYPE별 MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION 설정날짜로 SPLIT (END) */
10664	                    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- bucket loop START */
10665	                    WHILE N_BUCKETINSERT <= N_BUCKETCOUNT LOOP
10666	                        /* 변수 초기화 */
10667	                        BEGIN
10668	                            N_BATCH_RESULT := NULL;
10669	                        END;
10670	
10671	                        /* 대상날짜가 해당 MEASURE 의 BUCKET TYPE 과 동일한지 확인하여 같지않으면 INSERT INTO 처리하지 않으려고함 (START) ---------------------------------------------- */
10672	                        BEGIN
10673	                            V_DAY_VALID         := NULL;  /* 변수초기화 */
10674	                            N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE := NULL;  /* 변수초기화 */
10675	
10676	                            SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD INTO V_DAY_VALID FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOM WHERE RN = N_BUCKETINSERT;
10677	                        END;
10678	
10679	                        /* Convert QTY값 산출 */
10680	                        IF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'DAILY' THEN
10681	                            N_CALC_QTY := V_QTY;  /* DAILY 인경우는 계산필요없이 INPUT값을 그대로 사용함 */
10682	
10683	                            SELECT UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE
10684	                            INTO   N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE
10685	                            FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST
10686	                            WHERE  1 = 1
10687	                            AND    DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
10688	                            AND    SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID
10689	                            AND    MEASURE_GROUP = V_MEASURE_GROUP
10690	                            AND    MEASURE = V_MEASURE
10691	                            AND    SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE = V_DAY_VALID  /* daily인경우 INPUT된 날짜 */
10692	                            AND    PLAN_WEEK = N_PLAN_WEEK
10693	                            AND    VALID_YN = 'Y';  /* 20180409 SongKH Add */
10694	
10695	                        ELSE
10696	                            SELECT UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE
10697	                            INTO   N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE  /* 해당주차의 bucket 추출 */
10698	                            FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST
10699	                            WHERE  1 = 1
10700	                            AND    DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
10701	                            AND    SCM_SITE_ID = V_SCM_SITE_ID
10702	                            AND    MEASURE_GROUP = V_MEASURE_GROUP
10703	                            AND    MEASURE = V_MEASURE
10704	                            AND    SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE = TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(TO_DATE(V_DAY_VALID, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'MON') + (-7), 'YYYYMMDD')  /* INPUT된 날짜의 당주 월요일 */
10705	                            AND    PLAN_WEEK = N_PLAN_WEEK
10706	                            AND    VALID_YN = 'Y';  /* 20180409 SongKH Add */
10707	
10708	                        END IF;
10709	
10710	                        IF N_BUCKETINSERT < N_BUCKETCOUNT THEN
10711	
10712	                            N_CALC_QTY := TRUNC(N_CONVERT_QTY / N_BUCKETCOUNT, 0);
10713	                            N_ADD_QTY  := N_ADD_QTY + N_CALC_QTY;
10714	
10715	                        ELSE
10716	                            /* 마지막 데이터인경우 나머지값을 몰아주기 */
10717	                            N_CALC_QTY := N_CONVERT_QTY - N_ADD_QTY;
10718	                        END IF;
10719	
10720	                        /* 최신 UPLOAD_UOM 값을 변수에 셋팅 */
10721	                        BEGIN
10722	                            N_UPLOAD_UOM_KEY := N_UPLOAD_UOM;
10723	                        END;
10724	
10725	                        /* BUCKET_TYPE이 다른경우도 input 받은 measure 로 INSERT INTO 생성하도록 변경 (20180305) */
10726	                        BEGIN
10727	                            IF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE <> N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE THEN
10728	                                N_UPLOAD_UOM_KEY := N_UPLOAD_UOM;  /* 주차별 BUCKET_TYPE 이 달라도 그대로 사용함 */
10729	                            END IF;
10730	                        END;
10731	
10732	                        /* BUCKET_TYPE이 다른경우도 input 받은 measure 로 INSERT INTO 생성하도록 변경 (20180305) */
10733	                        BEGIN
10734	                            IF N_VALID_BUCKET_TYPE IS NULL THEN
10735	                                N_UPLOAD_UOM_KEY := NULL;
10736	                            END IF;
10737	                        END;
10738	
10739	                        /* MERGE대상 : SPLIT_COMPLETED / SPLIT만 하여 확인하는 MEASURE : UPLOAD_COMPLETED */
10740	                        BEGIN
10741	                            IF N_UPLOAD_UOM_KEY IS NULL THEN
10742	                                N_BATCH_RESULT := 'UPLOAD_COMPLETED';
10743	                            ELSE
10744	                                N_BATCH_RESULT := 'SPLIT_COMPLETED';
10745	                            END IF;
10746	                        END;
10747	
10748	                        /* MASTER에 BUCKET 설정이 없는 데이터인경우 */
10749	                        BEGIN
10750	                            IF N_AUTO_SPLIT_DATA = 'Y' THEN
10751	                                N_BATCH_RESULT := 'SPLIT_COMPLETED';
10752	                            END IF;
10753	                        END;
10754	
10755	                        BEGIN
10756	                            /* insert into  DM_PLN_CUSTOMER_PSI */
10757	                            INSERT
10758	                            INTO GT_DPCF_TEMP3_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER  /*  */
10759	                                (DIVISION_ID
10760	                                ,CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE
10761	                                ,CUSTOMER_MODEL
10762	                                ,SCM_SITE_ID
10763	                                ,DOM_EXP
10764	                                ,FCST_TYPE
10765	                                ,MEASURE_GROUP
10766	                                ,MEASURE
10767	                                ,YYYYMMDD
10768	                                ,INCOTERMS
10769	                                ,PLANT_SITE_ID
10770	                                ,PROJECT_CODE
10771	                                ,ITEM_ID
10772	                                ,QTY
10773	                                ,UPLOAD_UOM
10774	                                ,CREATED_BY
10775	                                ,CREATION_DATE
10776	                                ,LAST_UPDATED_BY
10777	                                ,LAST_UPDATE_DATE
10778	                                ,BATCH_RESULT
10779	                                ,MST_UPLOAD_UOM
10780	                                ,MST_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE
10781	                                ,MST_SUMMARY_BUCKET_TYPE
10782	                                ,MST_CONVERT_QTY
10783	                                ,PLAN_WEEK
10784	                                ,SPLIT_RESULT
10785	                                ,ATTRIBUTE1
10786	                                ,ATTRIBUTE2
10787	                                ,ATTRIBUTE3
10788	                                ,ATTRIBUTE4
10789	                                ,ATTRIBUTE5
10790	                                ,ATTRIBUTE6
10791	                                ,ATTRIBUTE7
10792	                                ,ATTRIBUTE8
10793	                                ,ATTRIBUTE9
10794	                                ,ATTRIBUTE10)
10795	                            VALUES
10796	                                (V_DIVISION_ID
10797	                                ,V_CUSTOMER_PROJECT_CODE
10798	                                ,V_CUSTOMER_MODEL
10799	                                ,V_SCM_SITE_ID
10800	                                ,V_DOM_EXP
10801	                                ,V_FCST_TYPE
10802	                                ,V_MEASURE_GROUP
10803	                                 /* ,RTRIM(LTRIM(TO_CHAR(TO_CHAR(v_MEASURE) || '_new_temp'))) */
10804	                                ,V_MEASURE || '_new_temp'
10805	                                ,(SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD FROM GT_DPCF_CALENDAR_DM_PLN_CUSTOM WHERE RN = N_BUCKETINSERT)
10806	                                ,V_INCOTERMS
10807	                                ,V_PLANT_SITE_ID
10808	                                ,'IM'  /* v_PROJECT_CODE */
10809	                                ,V_ITEM_ID
10810	                                ,N_CALC_QTY  /* ROW별로 나눠진 값을 넣음 */
10811	                                ,V_UPLOAD_UOM
10812	                                ,V_CREATED_BY
10813	                                ,V_CREATION_DATE
10814	                                ,V_LAST_UPDATED_BY
10815	                                ,SYSTIMESTAMP
10816	                                ,N_BATCH_RESULT
10817	                                ,N_UPLOAD_UOM
10818	                                ,N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE
10819	                                ,N_SUMMARY_BUCKET_TYPE
10820	                                ,V_MST_CONVERT_QTY
10821	                                ,V_PLAN_WEEK
10822	                                ,V_SPLIT_RESULT
10823	                                ,V_ATTRIBUTE1
10824	                                ,V_ATTRIBUTE2
10825	                                ,V_ATTRIBUTE3
10826	                                ,V_ATTRIBUTE4
10827	                                ,V_ATTRIBUTE5
10828	                                ,V_ATTRIBUTE6
10829	                                ,V_ATTRIBUTE7
10830	                                ,V_ATTRIBUTE8
10831	                                ,V_ATTRIBUTE9
10832	                                ,V_ATTRIBUTE10);
10833	                        END;
10834	
10835	                        BEGIN

10958	                    (YYYYMMDD, RN)
10959	                    SELECT YYYYMMDD, ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY YYYYMMDD) RN FROM GT_DPCF_TEMP3_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER GROUP BY YYYYMMDD ORDER BY YYYYMMDD;
10960	
10961	            END;
10962	            BEGIN
10963	                SELECT YYYYMMDD INTO N_MIN_YYYYMMDD FROM GT_DPCF_MIN_MAX_PSI WHERE RN = 1;
10964	
10965	                SELECT YYYYMMDD INTO N_MAX_YYYYMMDD FROM GT_DPCF_MIN_MAX_PSI WHERE RN = (SELECT MAX(RN) FROM GT_DPCF_MIN_MAX_PSI);
10966	            END;

10969	            /* PRINT('5.input 데이터의 시작과 끝 산출') */
10970	            /* -INSERT INTO 할 실제 데이터의 시작과 끝 구간 산출 ----------------------------------------------- (start) 20180409 */
10971	            DELETE FROM GT_DPCF_TMP_SITE_CUSTOMER_MAP;  /* MEASURE별 cursor 작업이므로 초기화 */
10972	
10973	            INSERT
10974	            INTO GT_DPCF_TMP_SITE_CUSTOMER_MAP
10975	                SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
10976	                      ,A.SCM_SITE_ID
10977	                      ,A.MEASURE
10978	                      ,A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE START_DAY
10979	                      ,CASE
10980	                           WHEN A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_TYPE = 'WEEKLY' THEN
10981	                            TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(TO_DATE(A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE, 'YYYYMMDD') /*[E20260827-NLSDATE]*/, 'SUN') + (0), 'YYYYMMDD')
10982	                           ELSE
10983	                            A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE
10984	                       END END_DAY
10985	                FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST A
10986	                INNER  JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, MEASURE_GROUP FROM GT_DPCF_TEMP3_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER) RSLT
10987	                ON     (A.DIVISION_ID = RSLT.DIVISION_ID AND A.SCM_SITE_ID = RSLT.SCM_SITE_ID AND A.MEASURE_GROUP = RSLT.MEASURE_GROUP)
10988	                WHERE  1 = 1
10989	                AND    VALID_YN = 'Y'
10990	                AND    A.PLAN_WEEK = (SELECT MAX(A2.PLAN_WEEK)
10991	                                      FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST A2
10992	                                      WHERE  A2.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID
10993	                                      AND    A2.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID
10994	                                      AND    A2.MEASURE_GROUP = A.MEASURE_GROUP)
10995	                AND    A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE >= TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') + (-7 * 1), 'YYYYMMDD')  /* 당주 월요일 이후데이터만 */
10996	                AND    A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_TYPE IN ('DAILY', 'WEEKLY')
10997	
10998	                UNION
10999	
11000	                SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
11001	                      ,A.SCM_SITE_ID
11002	                      ,A.MEASURE
11003	                      ,CASE
11004	                           WHEN A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE = TO_CHAR(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), 'YYYYMMDD') THEN
11005	                            TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') + (-7 * 1), 'YYYYMMDD')
11006	                           ELSE
11007	                            A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE
11008	                       END AS START_DAY
11009	                      ,TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(TO_DATE(A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE, 'YYYYMMDD'), 1) - 1, 'YYYYMMDD') END_DAY
11010	                FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST A
11011	                INNER  JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, MEASURE_GROUP FROM GT_DPCF_TEMP3_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER) RSLT
11012	                ON     (A.DIVISION_ID = RSLT.DIVISION_ID AND A.SCM_SITE_ID = RSLT.SCM_SITE_ID AND A.MEASURE_GROUP = RSLT.MEASURE_GROUP)
11013	                WHERE  1 = 1
11014	                AND    A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
11015	                AND    A.PLAN_WEEK = (SELECT MAX(A2.PLAN_WEEK)
11016	                                      FROM   DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST A2
11017	                                      WHERE  A2.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID
11018	                                      AND    A2.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID
11019	                                      AND    A2.MEASURE_GROUP = A.MEASURE_GROUP)
11020	                AND    A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_VALUE >= TO_CHAR(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), 'YYYYMMDD')
11021	
11022	                AND    A.SUMMARY_BUCKET_TYPE IN ('MONTHLY');
11023

11025	            /* select * from GT_DPCF_TEMP3_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER */

11029	            /* (88) 데이터의 시작~끝 날짜 구하기 ---------------------------------------------- (start) */
11030	            BEGIN
11031	                IF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'DAILY' THEN
11032	                    SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD INTO N_MAX_YYYYMMDD FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M T WHERE T.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = TO_CHAR(N_MAX_YYYYMMDD + (0), 'YYYYMMDD');  /* MAX_YYYYMMDD 셋팅 */
11033	                ELSIF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'WEEKLY' THEN
11034	                    N_MIN_YYYYMMDD := TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(TO_DATE(N_MIN_YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'MON') + (-7), 'YYYYMMDD');  /* MIN_YYYYMMDD 셋팅 */
11035	                    SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD
11036	                    INTO   N_MAX_YYYYMMDD
11037	                    FROM   SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M T
11038	                    WHERE  T.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(N_MAX_YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD') + 6, 'YYYYMMDD');  /* MAX_YYYYMMDD 셋팅 */
11039	                ELSIF N_UPLOAD_BUCKET_TYPE = 'MONTHLY' THEN
11040	                    SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD
11041	                    INTO   N_MAX_YYYYMMDD
11042	                    FROM   SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M T
11043	                    WHERE  T.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(TO_DATE(N_MAX_YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD'), 1) - 1, 'YYYYMMDD');  /* MAX_YYYYMMDD 셋팅 */
11044	                END IF;
11045	            END;

11186	            /*-------------------- DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 테이블 DELETE FROM count 확인 --------------------*/
11187	            BEGIN
11188	                DECLARE

11190	                BEGIN
11191	                    SELECT COUNT(*) CNT
11192	                    INTO   V_FCST_DEL_CNT
11193	                    FROM   DM_PLN_MKT_FCST A
11194	                    WHERE  1 = 1
11195	                    AND    EXISTS (SELECT 1
11196	                            FROM   GT_DPCF_TEMP2_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER B
11197	                            WHERE  A.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID
11198	                            AND    A.SCM_SITE_ID = B.SCM_SITE_ID

11200	                            AND    A.ITEM_ID = B.ITEM_ID
11201	                            AND    A.DOM_EXP = B.DOM_EXP
11202	                            AND    A.FCST_TYPE = B.FCST_TYPE
11203	                            AND    A.INCOTERMS = B.INCOTERMS
11204	                            AND    A.PLANT_SITE_ID = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
11205	                            AND    A.MEASURE = B.MEASURE
11206	                            /* AND A.YYYYMMDD >= n_MIN_YYYYMMDD  -- 추출된 데이터의 MIN ~ MAX의 구간  --20180409 modify */
11207	                            /* AND A.YYYYMMDD <= n_MAX_YYYYMMDD  -- 추출된 데이터의 MIN ~ MAX의 구간  --20180409 modify */
11208	                            );
11209	                    N_FCST_DELETE_COUNT := N_FCST_DELETE_COUNT + V_FCST_DEL_CNT;
11210	                END;
11211	            END;
11212	
11213	            /* PRINT('7.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 테이블 insert전 데이터 중복제거') */
11214	            /*-------------------- DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 테이블 insert전 데이터 중복제거 --------------------*/
11215	            BEGIN
11216	
11217	                DELETE FROM DM_PLN_MKT_FCST
11218	                WHERE  (SELECT COUNT(1)
11219	                        FROM   GT_DPCF_TEMP2_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER B
11220	                        WHERE  1 = 1
11221	                        AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID
11222	                        AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.SCM_SITE_ID = B.SCM_SITE_ID
11223	                              /* and  DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.PROJECT_CODE       =  B.PROJECT_CODE */
11224	                        AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.ITEM_ID = B.ITEM_ID
11225	                        AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.DOM_EXP = B.DOM_EXP
11226	                        AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.FCST_TYPE = B.FCST_TYPE
11227	                        AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.INCOTERMS = B.INCOTERMS
11228	                        AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.PLANT_SITE_ID = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
11229	                        AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.MEASURE = B.MEASURE
11230	                        AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.YYYYMMDD >= N_MIN_YYYYMMDD  /* 추출된 데이터의 MIN ~ MAX의 구간 */
11231	                        AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.YYYYMMDD <= N_MAX_YYYYMMDD  /* 추출된 데이터의 MIN ~ MAX의 구간 */
11232	                        ) > 0;
11233	            END;
11234	            /*-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
11235	            /* SELECT * FROM DM_PLN_MKT_FCST WHERE MEASURE = 'CUST_FCST' AND SCM_SITE_ID = 'IM_CN_NGG' AND CREATION_DATE > '2023-01-01 00:00' */
11236	            /* PRINT('7_1.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 테이블 insert전 데이터 중복제거') */
11237	
11238	            /* SELECT * FROM GT_DPCF_TEMP2_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER */
11239	            /* PRINT('8_0.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 테이블 insert =================== START ============================') */
11240

11242	            BEGIN

11245	                    INSERT INTO DM_PLN_MKT_FCST
11246	                        (DIVISION_ID
11247	                        ,PROJECT_CODE
11248	                        ,SCM_SITE_ID
11249	                        ,ITEM_ID
11250	                        ,DOM_EXP
11251	                        ,FCST_TYPE

11253	                        ,MEASURE
11254	                        ,PLANT_SITE_ID
11255	                        ,YYYYMMDD
11256	                        ,QTY
11257	                        ,CREATED_BY
11258	                        ,CREATION_DATE
11259	                        ,LAST_UPDATED_BY
11260	                        ,LAST_UPDATE_DATE
11261	                        ,ATTRIBUTE8
11262	                        ,ATTRIBUTE9
11263	                        ,ATTRIBUTE10)
11264	                        SELECT DISTINCT DIVISION_ID
11265	                                       ,'IM'
11266	                                       ,SCM_SITE_ID
11267	                                       ,ITEM_ID
11268	                                       ,DOM_EXP
11269	                                       ,FCST_TYPE

11283	                        WHERE  1 = 1
11284	                              /* AND BATCH_RESULT = 'UPLOAD_COMPLETED'  -- modified by ytchoi 180305 */
11285	                              /* added by ytchoi 180305 */
11286	                        AND    DIVISION_ID = '40'
11287	                        AND    YYYYMMDD >= N_MIN_YYYYMMDD  /* 추출된 데이터의 MIN ~ MAX의 구간 */
11288	                        AND    YYYYMMDD <= N_MAX_YYYYMMDD;  /* 추출된 데이터의 MIN ~ MAX의 구간 */
11289	                END IF;
11290	            END;
11291	            /* PRINT('8_0.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 테이블 insert =================== END ============================') */
11292	            /* SELECT * FROM DM_PLN_MKT_FCST WHERE MEASURE = 'CUST_FCST' AND LAST_UPDATED_BY = 'chano0612' */
11293	            /*-------------------- DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 테이블 MERGE  --------------------*/
11294	            /* PRINT('8.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 테이블 MERGE') */
11295	
11296	            DELETE FROM DM_PLN_MKT_FCST
11297	            WHERE  (SELECT COUNT(1)
11298	                    FROM   GT_DPCF_TEMP2_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER B
11299	                    WHERE  DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID
11300	                    AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.SCM_SITE_ID = B.SCM_SITE_ID
11301	                          /* and  DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.PROJECT_CODE       =  B.PROJECT_CODE */
11302	                    AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.ITEM_ID = B.ITEM_ID
11303	                    AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.DOM_EXP = B.DOM_EXP
11304	                    AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.FCST_TYPE = B.FCST_TYPE
11305	                    AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.INCOTERMS = B.INCOTERMS
11306	                    AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.PLANT_SITE_ID = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
11307	                          /* AND B.BATCH_RESULT = 'SPLIT_COMPLETED' -- modified by ytchoi 180305 */
11308	                    AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.MEASURE = 'CUST_FCST'
11309	                          /* (20230912 : CUST_FCST MEASURE 삭제시 당주(월) 이전 데이터는 삭제 안되도록 수정) */
11310	                    AND    DM_PLN_MKT_FCST.YYYYMMDD >= TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') + (-7), 'YYYYMMDD')) > 0;
11311	
11312	            /* PRINT('8_1.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST INSERT') */
11313	            INSERT INTO DM_PLN_MKT_FCST
11314	                (DIVISION_ID
11315	                ,PROJECT_CODE
11316	                ,SCM_SITE_ID
11317	                ,ITEM_ID
11318	                ,DOM_EXP
11319	                ,FCST_TYPE
11320	                ,INCOTERMS
11321	                ,MEASURE
11322	                ,PLANT_SITE_ID
11323	                ,YYYYMMDD
11324	                ,QTY
11325	                ,CREATED_BY
11326	                ,CREATION_DATE
11327	                ,LAST_UPDATED_BY
11328	                ,LAST_UPDATE_DATE
11329	                ,ATTRIBUTE1
11330	                ,ATTRIBUTE2
11331	                ,ATTRIBUTE3
11332	                ,ATTRIBUTE4
11333	                ,ATTRIBUTE5
11334	                ,ATTRIBUTE6
11335	                ,ATTRIBUTE7
11336	                ,ATTRIBUTE8
11337	                ,ATTRIBUTE9
11338	                ,ATTRIBUTE10)
11339	                SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
11340	                      ,A.PROJECT_CODE
11341	                      ,A.SCM_SITE_ID
11342	                      ,A.ITEM_ID
11343	                      ,A.DOM_EXP
11344	                      ,A.FCST_TYPE
11345	                      ,A.INCOTERMS
11346	                      ,'CUST_FCST'
11347	                      ,A.PLANT_SITE_ID
11348	                      ,A.YYYYMMDD
11349	                      ,A.QTY
11350	                      ,A.CREATED_BY
11351	                      ,A.CREATION_DATE
11352	                      ,A.LAST_UPDATED_BY
11353	                      ,A.LAST_UPDATE_DATE
11354	                      ,A.ATTRIBUTE1
11355	                      ,A.ATTRIBUTE2
11356	                      ,A.ATTRIBUTE3
11357	                      ,A.ATTRIBUTE4
11358	                      ,A.ATTRIBUTE5
11359	                      ,A.ATTRIBUTE6
11360	                      ,A.ATTRIBUTE7
11361	                      ,A.ATTRIBUTE8
11362	                      ,A.ATTRIBUTE9
11363	                      ,A.ATTRIBUTE10
11364	                FROM   DM_PLN_MKT_FCST A
11365	                INNER  JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, INCOTERMS, PLANT_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE
11366	                             FROM   GT_DPCF_TEMP2_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER V) RSLT
11367	                ON     (A.DIVISION_ID = RSLT.DIVISION_ID AND A.SCM_SITE_ID = RSLT.SCM_SITE_ID
11368	                       /* and a.MEASURE       = rslt.MEASURE */
11369	                       AND A.ITEM_ID = RSLT.ITEM_ID AND A.INCOTERMS = RSLT.INCOTERMS AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID = RSLT.PLANT_SITE_ID
11370	                       /* and a.PROJECT_CODE   = rslt.PROJECT_CODE */
11371	                       AND A.DOM_EXP = RSLT.DOM_EXP AND A.FCST_TYPE = RSLT.FCST_TYPE
11372	                       /* and a.yyyymmdd      = rslt.v_yyyymmdd */
11373	                       )
11374	                INNER  JOIN (SELECT DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, MEASURE, START_DAY, END_DAY FROM GT_DPCF_TMP_SITE_CUSTOMER_MAP) MAP
11375	
11376	                ON     (A.DIVISION_ID = MAP.DIVISION_ID AND A.SCM_SITE_ID = MAP.SCM_SITE_ID AND A.MEASURE = MAP.MEASURE)
11377	                WHERE  1 = 1
11378	                AND    A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN MAP.START_DAY AND MAP.END_DAY
11379	                AND    A.YYYYMMDD >= TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') + (-7), 'YYYYMMDD');
11380	
11381	            BEGIN
11382	                /* [변환주의] MSSQL: #TEMP2(GT_DPCF_TEMP2)에서 BATCH_RESULT='SPLIT_COMPLETED' COUNT 누적. */
11383	
11384	                SELECT N_FCST_INSERT_COUNT + COUNT(*)
11385	                INTO   N_FCST_INSERT_COUNT
11386	                FROM   GT_DPCF_TEMP2_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER
11387	                WHERE  1 = 1
11388	                AND    BATCH_RESULT = 'SPLIT_COMPLETED';
11389	            END;
11390	
11391	            /* GROUP 로딩 COUNT 증가 */
11392	            BEGIN
11393	                N_PSI_GROUP_INSERT := N_PSI_GROUP_INSERT + 1;
11394	            END;
11395	
11396	            /* GROUP별로 작업종료후 Temp Table TRUNCATE */
11397	            IF N_PSI_GROUP_INSERT <= N_PSI_GROUP_CNT THEN
11398	
11399	                /* GROUP별로 작업종료후 Temp Table TRUNCATE */
11400	                DELETE FROM GT_DPCF_TEMP2_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER; /* 원본 TRUNCATE→GTT는 DELETE(단일TX 원자성 보존·Oracle TRUNCATE 암묵COMMIT 회피) */
11401	                DELETE FROM GT_DPCF_TEMP3_DM_PLN_CUSTOMER; /* 원본 TRUNCATE→GTT는 DELETE(단일TX 원자성 보존·Oracle TRUNCATE 암묵COMMIT 회피) */
11402	            END IF;
11403	
11404	
11405	        END LOOP;
11406	
11407	        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- PSI GROUP LOOP END */
11408	        /* ############################################################################################################# */
11409	        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- PSI GROUP LOOP END */
11410	
11411	    END IF;
11412	
11413	    /* 종료 LOG */
11414	    V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';
11415	    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';
11416	    V_DESCRIPTION := 'psi_update_count= ' || TO_CHAR(N_PSI_UPDATE_COUNT) || ' / psi_delete_count= ' || TO_CHAR(N_PSI_DELETE_COUNT) || ' / psi_insert_count= ' ||
11417	                     TO_CHAR(N_PSI_INSERT_COUNT) || ' / fcst_delete_count= ' || TO_CHAR(N_FCST_DELETE_COUNT) || ' / fcst_insert_count= ' ||
11418	                     TO_CHAR(N_FCST_INSERT_COUNT);
11419	    /* || ' */
11420	    D_EVENT_DATE := SYSTIMESTAMP;
11421	
11422	    PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
11423	
11424	
11425	    /* [원자성] MSSQL 원본의 COMMIT 은 상위 BEGIN TRAN 내부 실행이라 @@TRANCOUNT 감소일 뿐 실커밋이 아니었다. */
11426	    /* END */
11427	
11428	
11429	    /* ----------------------------------------------------------- */
11430	EXCEPTION
11431	    WHEN OTHERS THEN
11432	        /* ----------------------------------------------------------- */
11433	        /* 트랜잭션 (END) */
11434

11436	        /* ERROR LOG VALUE SAVE */
11437	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';

11440	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
11441	        /* SELECT '[' || TO_CHAR(SQLCODE) || ']' || SQLERRM;  / *-- 에러메시지 DISPLAY --* / */
11442	        V_DESCRIPTION := '[' || TO_CHAR(SQLCODE) || ']' || SQLERRM; /*-- 에러로그 --*/

11451

11456	                         TO_CHAR(N_PSI_INSERT_COUNT) || ' / fcst_delete_count= ' || TO_CHAR(N_FCST_DELETE_COUNT) || ' / fcst_insert_count= ' ||
11457	                         TO_CHAR(N_FCST_INSERT_COUNT);
11458	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
11459	        PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
